---

> Ntụaka zuru ezu maka environment variable ọ bụla OmniRoute na-amata.
> Maka template mbido-ngwa-ngwa, lee [`.env.example`](../../.env.example).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Variable ọ bụla edepụtara ebe a ga-apụtakwa na `.env.example`, variable ọ bụla dị na
> `.env.example` ga-apụtakwa ebe a. `npm run check:env-doc-sync`
> na-amanye nke a mgbe a na-eme commit nakwa na CI. Iji kpachapụ variable ụma, tinye ya na
> allowlist dị n'ime `scripts/check/check-env-doc-sync.mjs`.

## 1. Nzuzo Achọrọ

Nzuzo ndị etinyere akara **Ee** nke a na-apụghị ịtọgharị ezubere iche na mgbakwunye mbu (`JWT_SECRET`, `API_KEY_SECRET`) ma debe na `server.env` n'ime nchekwa data — debe ha kpọmkwem mgbe nchekwa ahụ bụ nke na-enweghị nkwụnye. Ihe nzuzo pụrụ iche bụ `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET`: a naghị echekwa ya na `server.env` — ebe ntinye sava na-emepụta uru nke a na-ejikarị naanị site n'ịkekọrịta nke na-arụ ọrụ n'ime usoro, ya mere ndị ahịa eriri mpụga ga-achọ ka edobe ya kpọmkwem.

| Variable                     | Achọrọ                 | Ndabara           | Faịlụ Isi                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ---------------------------- | ---------------------- | ----------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `JWT_SECRET`                 | **Ee**                 | _(none)_          | `src/lib/auth`                                            | Na-edekọ / na-enyocha kuki nzukọ dashboard (JWT). Mepụta ya na `openssl rand -base64 48`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `API_KEY_SECRET`             | **Ee**                 | _(none)_          | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                   | Igodo nzuzo AES maka uru API key na-ezere n'okpuru nchekwa na SQLite. Mepụta ya na `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `INITIAL_PASSWORD`           | Mba                    | _(unset)_         | `src/lib/auth/managementPassword.ts`                      | Na-etinye okwuntughe dashboard na mmalite ebe okwuntughe adịghị echekwara; a na-efunahụ ya ozugbo echekwara okwuntughe. Ntụrụ npm na nsụgharị isi mmalite na-akopi `.env.example` gaa na `.env`, ya mere ha na-amalite na akara ngosi a mara nke ọma `CHANGEME`: dochie ya tupu boot mbụ, ma ọ bụ debe `INITIAL_PASSWORD=` (efu) na `.env` ahụ iji mepụta okwuntughe na onye nduzi nbata n'oge nleta mbụ, dịka agwa echekwara (ndabara nke oyiyi Docker). Gbanwee okwuntughe echekwara na Dashboard → Settings → Security, ma ọ bụ gbaa `omniroute reset-password` (`node bin/reset-password.mjs` na nchịkọta isi mmalite) na nke a etinyere `DATA_DIR` n'ọnọdụ nchekwa data nke sava. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET` | **Ee** (n'ọrụ mmepụta) | _(unset)_         | `src/app/api/internal/codex-responses-ws/route.ts`        | Nzuzo nke ejikọtara maka eriri WebSocket Codex Responses nke ime ụlọ. Na-enyocha arịrịọ eriri n'etiti Electron/browser WS relay na OmniRoute. ⚠️ **ACHỌRỌ n'ọrụ mmepụta — mgbe etinyebeghị, a na-ajụ arịrịọ WS bridge niile.** Mepụta ya na `openssl rand -base64 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SW_BUILD_ID`      | Mba                    | _(git SHA)_       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Njirimara doro anya maka iwepu cache nke service-worker maka PWA shell (#11779); nke mbụ n'usoro ngwọta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SOURCE_VERSION`             | Mba                    | _(unset)_         | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/assembleStandalone.mjs` | Nke abụọ na usoro — etinyere site n'aka ndị na-ewu PaaS (dịka Heroku-style) dịka nkwenye etinyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_SW_BUILD_ID`    | Mba                    | _(derived)_       | `src/shared/components/PwaRegister.tsx`                   | Uru ọha nke wụrụ n'ọrụ mgbe wụrụ nke onye ahịa ji debanye aha `/sw.js?v=…`; sitere na nke abụọ n'elu, mgbe ahụ na-esote git SHA.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_STAMP_TOKEN` | Mba (akpaghị aka)      | _(auto per boot)_ | `src/server/authz/policies/management.ts`                 | Nzuzo nke usoro ọ bụla na-egosi na akara onye mmekọ IP a tụkwasịrị obi sitere na sava HTTP nke OmniRoute (`scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`). Middleware authz na-ekwere ebe arịrịọ si (loopback / LAN gating nke ụzọ LOCAL_ONLY) naanị mgbe akara ahụ nwere token a. A na-emepụta ya akpaghị aka kwa boot — hapụ ya unset; debe ya naanị maka nhazi usoro ọtụtụ usoro nke ga-ekekọrịta akara ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUSTED_PROXIES`  | Mba                    | _(unset)_         | `scripts/dev/peer-stamp.mjs`                              | IPs ma ọ bụ ogo CIDR nke reverse proxies kewapụrụ site na kọma nke `X-Forwarded-For` / `X-Real-IP` nke ndepụta ikikere/mkpagbu IP nwere ike kweta, tinyere loopback, adreesị netwọkụ nzuzo na Cloudflare edges, nke a na-ekwere mgbe niile. A chọrọ ya naanị maka proxy nọ na adreesị ọha ọ bụla ọzọ; ma ọ bụghị ya, adreesị nke proxy ahụ onwe ya bụ nke ihe nzacha na-atụle. Throttle nke onye ahịa nke relay (`/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`) na-eji otu adreesị onye ahịa a dozie, ọ bụghị naanị na isi ozi nnyefe.                                                                                                                                                              |

### Iwu Mepụta

```bash
# Mepụta nzuzo anọ niile ozugbo:
echo "JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)"
echo "API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
echo "INITIAL_PASSWORD=$(openssl rand -base64 16)"
echo "OMNIROUTE_WS_BRIDGE_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 32)"
```

> [!NTỊAKA]
> Egbula commit faịlụ `.env` nke nwere ezi nzuzo n'ịchịkwa njikwa ụdị. Faịlụ `.gitignore` arụla ọrụ nke ịkpọpụ `.env`, mana jide n'aka tupu ịtọgharị.

## 2. Nchekwa & Nchekwa Data

OmniRoute na-eji **SQLite** (site na `better-sqlite3`) maka nchekwa niile. Mgbanwe ndị a na-achịkwa ebe data dị, izo ya, na usoro ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                                    | Ndabara                       | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                         | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `DATA_DIR`                                 | `~/.omniroute/`               | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Mgbọrọgwụ ndekọ maka SQLite DB, ndabere, na faịlụ data. Gbanwee ya maka Docker volumes ma ọ bụ ụzọ ahaziri ahazi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_DEFAULT_DATA_DIR`         | _(edozighị)_                  | `src/lib/dataPaths.ts`                                | Ụzọ mgbapụ maka nchekwa DATA_DIR nke ule/eval (#10428). A na-atụgharị ule na nyocha Node eval/print (`-e`/`--eval`/`-p`/`--print`, gụnyere ụdị `--eval=`/`--print=`) na-enweghị `DATA_DIR` gaa na ndekọ nwa oge a na-atụfu, ka ha ghara imeghe ezigbo nchekwa data onye ọrụ; tọọ ya na `1` iji laghachi na ezigbo ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_SHA`                      | _(edozighị)_                  | `src/lib/monitoring/buildSha.ts`                      | Git SHA nke artifact na-agba. Edebara ya site na `npm run build:release`; enwere ike ịgbanye ya na containers ndị ebupụtara na-enweghị sentinel `dist/BUILD_SHA`. E gosipụtara ya dị ka `system.buildSha` na `/api/monitoring/health`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELEASE_REF`                    | `origin/main`                 | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Ref nke ọnụ ụzọ provenance nke pack-artifact na-eji enyocha SHA build (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CANARY_BUILD`             | _(edozighị)_                  | `scripts/build/buildProvenance.ts`                    | Tọọ ya na `1` iji kwe ka ịkpọkọta build nke SHA ya na-adịghị na ahịrị mwepụta, na-edekọ ya dị ka canary a kpachapụrụ anya kama ịda ọnụ ụzọ ahụ (#10427).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_API_KEY`                  | _(edozighị)_                  | `scripts/ops/deploy-canary.mjs`                       | Igodo API maka nyocha anwụrụ ọkụ nke canary-deploy, ezigara dị ka `Authorization: Bearer` na `/v1/chat/completions`. Naanị script deploy na-eji ya (#10429), ọ bụghị server. Ọ metụtaraghị mgbanwe `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_*` nke CLI smoke harness a na-ahọrọ (`RUN_CLI_SMOKE=1`, `OMNIROUTE_SMOKE_BASE_URL/MODEL/API_KEY_ENV/TARGETS/TIMEOUT_MS` na `tests/integration/upstream-cli-smoke.int.test.ts`) — lee [Njikọ CLI → Nnwale anwụrụ ọkụ n'ezie](../guides/CLI-INTEGRATIONS.md).                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILDING`                       | _(edozighị)_                  | `src/lib/buildPhase.ts`                               | Mgbama oge build (#10060): `scripts/build/build-next-isolated.mjs` na-atọ ya na `1` ma ndị ọrụ build ọ bụla a mụpụtara na-eketa ya, ka oyi akwa DB weghachi stub na-enweghị mmetụta kama ibudata addon better-sqlite3 nke ala (nke na-akwụsị onye ọrụ mgbe ọ pụta). E setịghị ya maka server na-agba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_NATIVE_DEP_CHECK`          | `0`                           | `scripts/check/check-native-deps.mjs`                 | Tọọ ya na `1` iji hapụ nyocha prebuild nke native-dependency nhọrọ maka osisi vendored pụrụ iche. Nke a adịghị eme ka dependency na-efu bụrụ nke a pụrụ iwu; jiri naanị mgbe a na-enye native dependencies na mpụga usoro.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR`                       | _(edozighị)_                  | `open-sse/executors/promptql/threadSticky.ts`         | **Alias ndabere** maka `DATA_DIR`, a na-enyocha ya naanị mgbe `DATA_DIR` edozighị. A na-eji ya achọta cache nnọkọ thread-sticky nke PromptQL executor na diski (`<dir>/promptql-thread-sessions.json`); ọ bụrụ na edozighị mgbanwe abụọ ahụ, cache ahụ na-anọgide naanị na ebe nchekwa (anaghị echekwa ya n'ofe mmaliteghachi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR`                    | _(edozighị)_                  | `src/lib/plugins/scanner.ts`                          | Ndekọ **runtime plugin scanner** na-agụ — na mgbọrọgwụ plugin manager na-etinye na ya — na-emeri ndabara ewepụtara site na home (#11827). Tụzie ya na osisi plugin bind-mounted na Docker/K8s kama ịkwaga HOME naanị iji bugharịa ụzọ scan (HOME na-achịkwa omume ndị ọzọ niile metụtara home). Unset = `~/.omniroute/plugins`, ma ọ bụ `/tmp/.omniroute/plugins` mgbe usoro ahụ na-ebupụtaghị home ọ bụla — enweghị nchọpụta nkịtị nke mgbanwe a na-ewepụ. A na-edekọ ndekọ edoziri otu ugboro na mmalite dị ka `scanner.dir_resolved` ya na ntinye meriri. Naanị n'akụkụ server: plugins iwu CLI na-edebe `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH` nke ha (nkebi 9). |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`                   | _(empty = disabled)_          | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | Igodo AES maka nzuzo zuru ezu maka SQLite database mgbe a kwụsịrị. Mepụta ya na `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY_VERSION`           | `v1`                          | `scripts/build/bootstrap-env.mjs`, `electron/main.js` | Aha version maka igodo nzuzo. Welie ya elu mgbe emere ntụgharị igodo iji kwado ịtụgharị data ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`               | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/backup.ts`                                | Mgbe etinyere `true`, na-ewepụ ichekwa ndabara/tupu idekọ faịlụ SQLite (dịka models.dev echekwa ọnụego/ehichapụ, ndekọ ntọala). Nchekwa aka na nke tupu mgbake ka na-aga. Ọ naghị ezuru ịkwụsị ntụgharị nchekwa nke migration runner nke chọrọ nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide ma ọ bụ nchedo maka mgbanwe data dị adịgide. A na-edobe nchekwa ndị na-abụghị nke aka ruo ugboro otu kwa nkeji 60. Dashboard **Settings → Storage** nwere ike ịgbanyụọ ichekwa ndabara na-akpaghị aka n'onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLITE_SLOW_MS`                 | _(unset/off)_                 | `src/lib/db/adapters/slowDiagnostics.ts`              | Milliseconds; ọnụahịa dị n'okpuru 1 ma ọ bụ ndị na-abụghị nọmba ga-anọgide gbachiri; na-eweta ntị kacha otu ugboro kwa 10s; ahịrị ndekọ nwere naanị driver, operation, na elapsedMs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CRYPT_KEY`                      | _(unset)_                     | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy alias** maka `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY`. A na-anabata ya dị ka nkwụsị mgbe mgbanwe izizi adịghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_BASE64`                 | _(unset)_                     | `src/lib/db/encryption.ts`                            | **Legacy alias** (nke edobere na Base64) a na-anabata dịka nkwụsị. A na-eme ka ọ doo anya ozugbo tupu ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DB_HEALTHCHECK_INTERVAL_MS`     | _(unset)_                     | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Gbanwee etiti oge nyocha ahụike SQLite (ms) nke na-eme oge ụfọdụ. Mgbe etinyereghị, a na-eji ndabara si na `NODE_ENV`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`       | _(removed)_                   | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | **E wepụtala.** Checkpoint ndụ kwa oge `wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)` nwere ike ime ka nhazi wal-index nke ekerịta ghara ịdị irè ma mee ka usoro daa na SIGBUS (#13973), ya mere onye nhazi oge adịghịzi. Ihe a gbanwere adịghị arụ ọrụ: uru dị mma na-edebere otu oge nkwupụta nkwụsị, ebe `0` ma ọ bụ ịhichapụ na-ejide udo. WAL na-ejikwa PASSIVE checkpoints (n'okpuru) ma a na-ebelata ya site na checkpoint nkwụsị ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_PASSIVE_INTERVAL_MS`        | `300000` (5m)                 | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Gbanwee etiti oge nke `wal_checkpoint(PASSIVE)` nke na-eme kwa oge (ms). Na-eme ka ọnụ ọgụgụ frames WAL na-eche ghara ibu ibu, ka checkpoints na-aga ngwa ngwa ma faịlụ WAL nọ n'ókè n'etiti nkwụsị idegharị. `0` na-egbochi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WAL_GUARD_MAX_MB`               | `256`                         | `src/lib/db/walMaintenance.ts`                        | Mgbe tick PASSIVE hụrụ faịlụ WAL gafere nha a, gbaa `wal_checkpoint(RESTART)` ka WAL maliteghachi n'enweghị idebanye akwụkwọ wal-index edobere. A wepụrụ checkpoints nke truncate-mode ndụ (lee ahịrị `OMNIROUTE_WAL_TRUNCATE_INTERVAL_MS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Tinye na `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji pụọ na usoro mgbe nrụgide akụ dị oke nọgide ruo `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS`, na-ahapụ onye nlekọta (dịka systemd `Restart=always`, ụkpụrụ Docker maka mmaliteghachi) ịmaliteghachi usoro dị ọcha kama inye 503s n'agbanyeghị oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART_AFTER_MS` | `120000` (2m)                 | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                  | Olee ogologo oge nrụgide dị oke ga-anọgide tupu mmechi mmaliteghachi onwe ya pụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_PSI_DISABLED`          | `false`                       | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressurePolicy.ts`            | Tinye na `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji chefuo `/proc/pressure/memory` nke host niile (`psi_some` / `psi_full`) mgbe onye agbụrụ na-eme mkpọtụ na-eme ka 503s pụta ebe usoro a dị mma (#13124). Cgroup na V8 heap guards ka nọ n'ọrụ. PSI nwekwara kwụsịrị ịgụnye dị ka mgbochi kernel zuru oke maka `cgroup_high`: ọnụ ọgụgụ cgroup gafere `memory.high` na-eme ka ọ bụrụ ihe dị oke egwu naanị mgbe ịrị elu `memory.events` na `high`/`max`. E nweghị `memory.events` a na-edobe otu ọnụego ntakịrị.                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SQLJS_WASM_PATH`                | _(auto-detect)_               | `src/lib/db/adapters/sqljsAdapter.ts`                 | Ụzọ kpọmkwem (absolute ma ọ bụ nke metụtara cwd) gaa na `sql-wasm.wasm` mgbe ejiri `sql.js` WASM fallback adapter. A na-achọpụta ya akpaka site na ndabere ngwugwu na nhazi ndị nwere ike ịbụ mgbe etinyereghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`           | `30`                          | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ụbọchị nke nhicha akpaaka na-edebe checkpoints, faịlụ ntinye/mmepụta/njehie e kpọtụrụ aha, na ahịrị nke ọrụ Batch API gụchara (gụchara/dara/kagbuola/kubi oge) tupu ehichapụ ha. Ọ na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ naanị mgbe a gbadoro `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`; ọ kwekọrọ na windo nchekwa mmepụta Batch API nke OpenAI n'onwe ya. Ọ metụtaghị ụzọ `DELETE /api/v1/batches/delete-completed` nke onye ọrụ na-akpali, nke ka na-enweghị ọnọdụ (enweghị ihe nzacha afọ) site n'atụmatụ ya.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BATCH_AND_FILE_AUTO_CLEANUP_ENABLED`      | `false`                       | `src/lib/db/cleanup.ts`                               | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ka nhicha akpaaka hichapụ ọrụ Batch API gụchara (na checkpoints ha) gafere `OMNIROUTE_BATCH_RETENTION_DAYS`, wee hichapụ ọdịnaya BLOB nke faịlụ e buliri gafere `expires_at` nke ha. Ọ gbanyụrụ n'ọnọdụ ndabara: nrụnye ọ bụla dị adị na-edebe data a kpomkwem dị ka ọ dịbu tupu onye ọrụ kwenye. Ọ bụkwa feature flag a nwere ike dezie na dashboard — lee `docs/reference/FEATURE_FLAGS.md` → Runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`            | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`, `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`     | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji leghara nlele ahụ ike DB kpamkpam n'oge mmalite. Ọ bara uru maka ọrụ na-adịghị adị ogologo oge na ule njikọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORCE_DB_HEALTHCHECK`           | `0`                           | `src/lib/db/core.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji manye mee ka loop nlele ahụ ike DB rụọ ọrụ, ọbụlagodi mgbe a ga-eleghara ya n'ụzọ nkịtị (dịka, ọrụ na-adịghị adị ogologo oge).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_POSTINSTALL`               | `0`                           | `scripts/postinstall.mjs`                             | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji leghara warm-up nke native-runtime n'oge `npm install`. Ọ bara uru na nrụnye CI/headless ebe e wurula sqlite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_MIGRATIONS_DIR`                 | _(nchọpụta akpaaka)_          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Kwapụta ụlọ ọrụ (directory) nke onye ọsọ migration na-enyocha. Ọ bara uru mgbe a na-ebuga migrations ejikọtara ọnụ n'ime ewulite omenala.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_EXTRA_MIGRATIONS_DIRS`          | _(adịghị atọ)_                | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner/extraDirs.ts`             | Ụlọ ọrụ migration ndị ọzọ dị ka ndenye `namespace=dir` nke ihe nkewa ụzọ nke usoro kewara (dịka `ee=/opt/app/enterprise/db/migrations`). A na-edekọ faịlụ a hụrụ ebe ahụ dị ka `<namespace>-<number>`, ka nkesa na-ebuga migrations nke ya ghara ịkụkọta na oghere ọnụ ọgụgụ upstream. Ndenye emebi, namespace na-adịghị ire ma ọ bụ ụlọ ọrụ na-apụ na-atụpụ njehie n'oge mmalite kama ịleghara schema n'udo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_PENDING_MIGRATIONS`         | `50`                          | `src/lib/db/migrationRunner.ts`                       | Oke nchekwa maka migrations na-echere n'ọha (#3416). Mmalite na-ekwụsị ọ bụrụ na migrations karịrị ọnụ ọgụgụ a na-echere na DB dị adị (na-echebe tebụl nlekota e hichapụrụ). Welie ya iji weghachite nchekwa ochie; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ nlele ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTALL_UPGRADE_WORKDIR`        | _(`<repo>/.install-upgrade`)_ | `scripts/check/check-install-upgrade.mjs`             | Ụlọ ọrụ ọrụ maka ọnụ ụzọ mwepụta `check:install-upgrade`. Ọ chọrọ ihe dịka 12 GB (osisi nrụnye abụọ nke ~3 GB gbakwunyere tarball), ya mere ọ gaghị agba ọsọ na tmpfs dị obere — na onye ọsọ self-hosted, `/tmp` bụ tmpfs 12 GB nke RAM na-akwado ma ọnụ ụzọ ahụ gwụrụ ya, na-egbutu ngwugwu ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_FLUSH_INTERVAL_MS`        | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Oge nkitị flush (ms) maka onye ede mmefu/ọnụ ahịa jikọtara ọnụ. Ọnụọgụgụ dị ala na-ebelata ijikọta nke nde; ọnụọgụgụ dị elu na-ebelata asọmpi DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SPEND_MAX_BUFFER_SIZE`          | _(ndabara dị na koodu)_       | `src/lib/spend/batchWriter.ts`                        | Oke kacha elu nke ndenye mmefu a bufere tupu a manye flush. Welie ya na ntinye QPS dị elu; belata ya mgbe ncheta nwere oke dị mkpa karịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_FETCH_DEBUG`              | _(adịghị atọ)_                | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                        | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` iji wepụta ndekọ debug `[ProxyFetch]` na ụzọ relay Vercel. Ọ gbanyụrụ n'ọnọdụ ndabara iji zere ịkwafụ ndụmọdụ nhazi ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_BINARY_PATH`         | _(adịghị atọ)_                | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/sync.ts`        | Ụzọ zuru oke nke binary proxy core nke mpaghara (faịlụ a na-akpọ `sing-box`) nke na-enyocha nhazi core emepụtara tupu ọ dochie faịlụ a nabatara. Mgbe a na-atabeghị ya, ọ na-ede `<path>.generated` n'akụkụ faịlụ a nabatara naanị. Gburugburu host naanị: a naghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ nabata ya site na API nlekota, n'ihi na a na-agba binary ahụ ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_CORE_RELOAD_COMMAND`      | _(adịghị atọ)_                | `src/lib/proxySubscription/coreConfig/reload.ts`      | Otú proxy core nke mpaghara si abudata ọzọ mgbe nnọchi config e gosipụtara gụsịrị: usoro argument JSON na-agba ọsọ na-enweghị shell, ma ọ bụ kpọmkwem `external` mgbe onye na-ele anya site n'èzí na-etinye faịlụ ahụ. A naghị echekwa ya na database ma ọ bụ kpughee ya site na API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                    | `false`                       | `src/lib/proxyLogger.ts`                              | Tinye ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji gụnye IP nke client/egress na prefix akaụntụ n'ahịrị ndekọ usoro `[ProxyEgress]` zuru ezu. A na-edebe ya OFF dị ka ndabara ka ndekọ usoro ghara ịgbaapụta IP ma ọ bụ prefix akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG`                          | _(edobeghị)_                  | `bin/cli/commands/quota.mjs`                          | Tinye ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji biipụta nyocha oge nke arịrị ọ bụla (`[omniroute] GET <path> completed in Nms`) sitere na iwu quota nke CLI gaa na stderr.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_PATH`               | _(akpaghị aka)_               | `scripts/dev/healthcheck.mjs`                         | Ụzọ a kapịrị ọnụ nke nlele ahụike container na-enyocha. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, nnwale ahụ na-ewepụta ya site na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`; ịtinye ya na-eme ka ị laghachi n'ime njedebe nlele miri emi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_DEBUG_COMPLETION`               | _(edobeghị)_                  | `bin/cli/commands/completion.mjs`                     | Tinye ka ọ bụrụ uru ọ bụla na-abụghị efu iji wepụta nyocha `[omniroute completion]` sitere na ụzọ cache nke mmecha shell CLI (gụọ/megharia/dee). Ọ na-anọ OFF dị ka ndabara — caches ndị ahụ na-akụda n'ịdị jụụ ka cache furu efu ma ọ bụ rụrụ arụ ghara imebi mmecha tab.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `BATCH_RETRY_DURATION_MS`                  | `86400000` (24h)              | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oge nnwale ọzọ kacha elu maka ihe batch nke ọ bụla (ms). A na-akara ihe ndị gafere oge a dị ka ndị dara ada.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_BASE_MS`                    | `5000`                        | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ngbede isi (ms) maka exponential backoff na nnwale ọzọ nke ihe batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `BATCH_BACKOFF_MAX_MS`                     | `3600000` (1h)                | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Oke kacha elu (ms) maka exponential backoff n'etiti nnwale ọzọ nke ihe batch.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `BATCH_MAX_CONCURRENT`                     | `1`                           | `open-sse/services/batchProcessor.ts`                 | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke batch a na-arụkọrọ ọnụ. Welie ya iji mee ka ọrụ a na-arụ dịkwuo; debe ya n'ala iji zere ọgbaaghara rate-limit.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Tupu ịgbanwe database na-adịgide adịgide dị adị, onye na-agba ọrụ migration na-ebipụta foto zuru ezu
> nke e jiri adreesị ọdịnaya hazie n'okpuru `DATA_DIR/db_backups/`. Mbipụta chọrọ sistemụ faịlụ
> nke na-akwado njikọ siri ike n'otu sistemụ faịlụ nke na-agaghị edeghari, tinyere sịnc faịlụ na-adịgide
> adịgide. Ndị ọbịa POSIX chọkwara sịnc ndekọ; na Windows, Node nwere ike ịjụ handles ndekọ,
> ya mere OmniRoute na-eme ka faịlụ ebipụtara kwụsie ike na diski (flush) wee were sịnc ntinye
> ndekọ dị ka mgbalị kacha mma.
> Ọ bụrụ na `DATA_DIR` a tụrụ agaghị enye nkwa ndị ahụ, mmalite na-akụda n'ọnọdụ mechiri emechi
> tupu etinye migration. Kwagara `DATA_DIR` gaa na olu nwere ihe ndabere ndị ahụ; ekwula iji
> `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP` gafere nchekwa migration.

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                           | Nhazi                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| **Mmepe ime obodo**             | Hapụ ndabara niile. DB nọ na `~/.omniroute/omniroute.db`.                                  |
| **Docker**                      | `DATA_DIR=/data` + tụọ olu na `/data`.                                                     |
| **Nzobe ezo mgbe ọ na-ezumike** | Tinye `STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY` + debekwa ndabere nke igodo! Ịla n'iyi ya = ịla n'iyi data. |
| **CI/Nnwale**                   | `DATA_DIR=/tmp/omniroute-test` — nke na-adịghị adịgide, achọghị nzobe ezo.                 |

---

## 3. Netwọk & Ports

| Variable                                    | Default                              | Source File                                                              | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PORT`                                      | `20128`                              | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Port bụ isi maka Dashboard UI na API endpoints (ọnọdụ otu port).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`                       | _(efu = root)_                       | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/docker/ensure-docker-base-path.mjs`          | Subpath URL maka ijere OmniRoute n'azụ reverse proxy (na-atọ Next.js `basePath`; ntụgharị auth na-amata basePath). Dịka `/omniroute`. Na Docker, a na-agbanye uru ahụ n'oge `docker build` (`ARG OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`); root images e wuru n'ihu nwere ike itinye uru runtime dị iche otu ugboro mgbe container bidoro, tupu Next.js ebido. Tọwụrụ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ origin ọha mmadụ gụnyere otu subpath ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH`           | _(efu = root)_                       | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`                                  | Ihe oyiri nke `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` nke browser na-ahụ, etinyere n'ime n'oge build ka ngosi endpoint dashboard wee gosi `https://host/omniroute/v1` kama `https://host/v1`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `OMNIROUTE_BASE_PATH` mgbe a na-atọghị ya. Wulite ọzọ mgbe ị gbanwere (Next `basePath` bụ n'oge build).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED`                     | _(a tọghị = agaghị etinye n'iframe)_ | `next.config.mjs`, `scripts/build/dashboardEmbed.mjs`                    | Ntinye iframe nke ị họrọ maka ibe HTML. Mgbe a na-atọghị ya, ụzọ ọ bụla na-ebu `frame-ancestors 'none'` + `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. Tọwụrụ ya ka ọ bụrụ `vscode` iji jee ibe ndị ahụ (dashboard, nbanye, akwụkwọ nduzi, ibe mbata) na `frame-ancestors 'self' vscode-webview:` na-enweghị `X-Frame-Options`, ka VS Code Simple Browser wee nwee ike igosi ha (ọnọdụ `dashboardOpen: "editor"` nke OmniCopilot). Ihu API (`/api`, `/v1`, `/v1beta`, `/a2a`, `/healthz`, aliases ọkwa root) na-edebe isiokwu siri ike n'ụzọ ọ bụla. Naanị `vscode` ka a na-amata — `1`/`true` anaghị amalite ya. N'oge build: wulite ọzọ mgbe ị gbanwere (`docker build --build-arg DASHBOARD_ALLOW_EMBED=vscode` maka images; itọ ya na ntinye e wubere n'ihu enweghị mmetụta).                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `API_PORT`                                  | _(a tọghị)_                          | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe a tọrọ ya, ọ na-ejere `/v1/*` proxy API na port dị iche a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `API_HOST`                                  | `0.0.0.0`                            | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Adreesị bind maka port API.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `DASHBOARD_PORT`                            | _(a tọghị)_                          | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Mgbe a tọrọ ya, ọ na-ejere Dashboard UI na port dị iche a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_MAX_CONCURRENT_CONNECTIONS`           | `0` _(agbanyụrụ)_                    | `src/sse/utils/backpressure.ts`                                          | Na-akpụ oke njikọ chat na-aga n'ihu n'otu oge; arịrị ọ bụla karịrị oke ahụ na-enweta `503` na `Retry-After`. Integer dị mma na-amalite nche ahụ; a tọghị/`0` na-agbanyụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_INSTANCE_ID`                     | _(a tọghị)_                          | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | ID kwụsiri ike ma pụrụ iche maka gateway a mgbe a na-ejikọtọ ọnụ ọgụgụ OmniRoute n'usoro. Na-enyere aka nyocha peer-loop na-abata. Mkpụrụedemede ekwenyere: mkpụrụedemede, ọnụọgụ, `.`, `_`, `:`, na `-`; oke kacha elu bụ mkpụrụedemede 64.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_URLS`                       | _(atoghị)_                           | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`     | URL ndị bụ isi OmniRoute nke koma kewara nke nwere ike ịnata `X-OmniRoute-Peer-Trace`. Naanị URL ndị upstream e depụtara n'ụzọ doro anya na-anata metadata peer; ndị ọzụzụ ndị ọzọ niile anaghị emetụta ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PEER_MAX_HOPS`                   | `4`                                  | `src/shared/resilience/peerRouting.ts`                                   | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha ukwuu nke ihe omume OmniRoute ndị e gara n'ụzọ ha n'oge gara aga nke a nabatara na arịrịọ ejikọtara ọnụ (`1`-`32`). Ihe omume ndị arụrụ ugboro ma ọ bụ ego gụsịrị na-eweghachi HTTP `508 Loop Detected`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT`                       | `20130`                              | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka Dashboard na ọnọdụ mmepụta ihe Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROD_API_PORT`                             | `20131`                              | `docker-compose.prod.yml`                                                | Port e bipụtara n'akụkụ host maka API na ọnọdụ mmepụta ihe Docker.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PORT`                            | _(atoghị)_                           | `src/lib/runtime/ports.ts`                                               | Na-akara isi karịa `PORT` mgbe ọ na-agba ọsọ n'ime Electron ma ọ bụ ndị nkịtị ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `LIVE_WS_PORT`                              | `20129`                              | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Port maka sava nleba anya WebSocket ndụ n'oge ndụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `LIVE_WS_HOST`                              | `127.0.0.1`                          | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Adreesị njikọ maka sava WebSocket ndụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0.0.0.0` ka ọ pụta ìhè na LAN (hazikwa `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`). Nnjikọ ndị ahụ na-akwado onwe ha site na kuki nnọkọ dashboard ma ọ bụ igodo API Bearer nke nwere oke nlekọta (`manage`/`admin`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                   | _(atoghị)_                           | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts`                                            | Ebe mmalite ndị ọzọ nke koma kewara nke a kwetara ịmepee WebSocket ndụ. A na-akwatala ebe mmalite dashboard loopback n'ụzọ ndabere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_HOSTS`                     | _(atoghị)_                           | `src/server/ws/liveServerAllowList.ts`                                   | Aha host ndị ọzọ nke koma kewara nke a kwetara maka ebe mmalite WebSocket ndụ. N'adịghị ka `LIVE_WS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` (URL ebe mmalite zuru ezu), ọ na-akpọtụ naanị akụkụ host — ọ bara uru maka ntọala LAN/Tailscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_LIVE_WS_PUBLIC_URL`            | _(atoghị)_                           | `src/hooks/useLiveDashboard.ts`                                          | URL ọha maka WebSocket dashboard ndụ (n'akụkụ ihe nchọgharị). Tọọ ya mgbe ị na-etinye sava WS n'ihu reverse proxy ma ọ bụ Cloudflare Tunnel (dịka `wss://ws.my-ai.com/live-ws`); ihe nchọgharị na-ejikọ ebe ahụ kama `ws://hostname:20132`. A na-ejikwa akụkụ pathname dị ka ụzọ nkwalite WebSocket (ndabere: `/live-ws`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ENABLE_LIVE_WS`                  | `true`                               | `src/server/ws/liveServer.ts` na `scripts/start-ws-server.mjs`           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ sava WebSocket oge ndụ (a na-enye ya aka n'ụzọ ndabere, ejikọtara na loopback). Ọ bụ ihe mgbanwe CI/harness nke na-agbanyụọ script enyemaka WebSocket ndụ kwụ onwe ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE`                       | `30`                                 | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/route.ts`                         | Oke ọnụego relay maka (token, IP) ọ bụla, arịrịọ/nkeji. Nọ na nchekwa, nke ihe omume ọ bụla. `0` ma ọ bụ ọnụ ọgụgụ ọjọọ na-agbanyụọ ọnụ ụzọ nke ojii IP (oke DB nke token ka na-arụ ọrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NODE_ENV`                                  | `production`                         | Isi Next.js                                                              | Na-achịkwa oke ntinye ndekọ, nchekwa nwa oge, ngosipụta nkọwa njehie, na nkwalite Next.js.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK`                   | `1` (Turbopack — ndabara koodu)      | `package.json` / Next.js 16                                              | Turbopack bụ ngwugwu ndabara maka `npm run dev` na `npm run build` (iwu ngwa ngwa 2-3x, nwalee). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji laghachi na webpack na Windows, mgbe ị na-ezute nsogbu njikọta ala / ndakọrịta ngwugwu, **ma ọ bụ na igwe nwere RAM pere mpe** — a maara na ihe owuwu mmepụta Turbopack na ahịrị ụdị Next.js a (16.2.x) na-eri ebe nchekwa dị elu karịa webpack na nnukwu eserese modul (ndozi nchụpụ ebe nchekwa Turbopack nke Next 16.3 akwadobeghị); webpack fallback na-eri ebe nchekwa dị ala karịa. Lee #6409. **Ihe owuwu onyonyo Docker na-adabara na `0` (webpack)**: `Dockerfile` na-ekwupụta `ARG OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=0` ka `docker build .` (Railway na ndị ọbịa ndị ọzọ nwere otu pịa, ndị na-akwado onwe ha) ghara ịnwụ n'ihi OOM na onye na-ewu ụlọ nwere oke ebe nchekwa; gafere `--build-arg OMNIROUTE_USE_TURBOPACK=1` iji wuo onyonyo ahụ na Turbopack. Lee `docs/guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md` → Ihe enyemaka oge owuwu. |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_DB_HEALTHCHECK`             | _(edozibeghị)_                       | `src/lib/db/core.ts` / `src/lib/db/healthCheck.ts`                       | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji fefe nyocha ahụike iguzosi ike n'ezi ihe SQLite na mmalite. Bara uru maka mmalite ngwa ngwa na nnukwu nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `NOTIFY_SOCKET`                             | _(edozibeghị)_                       | systemd (usoro sd_notify)                                                | systemd na-edozi ya mgbe usoro ahụ na-agba n'okpuru otu ọrụ nwere njikọta sd_notify; OmniRoute na-agụ ya (lee `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`) iji zipu ọkwa READY/WATCHDOG. Onye ọrụ anaghị edozi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_SD_NOTIFY`               | _(edozibeghị)_                       | `scripts/dev/systemd-notify.mjs`                                         | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji gbanyụọ systemd sd_notify (Type=notify / WatchdogSec=) ọbụlagodi mgbe ọ na-agba n'okpuru otu systemd. Onye na-agwa ihe bụ no-op na mpụga systemd n'agbanyeghị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK_INTERVAL`          | `3600000`                            | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts` | Oge (ms) maka onye nhazi nyocha ahụike nzuzo nzuzo.
