Nke kacha nta: 10000 (10s).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL`               | `300000`                             | `open-sse/config/constants.ts` / `src/lib/credentialHealth/cache.ts`     | TTL (ms) maka ọnọdụ ahụike nzuzo echekwara nwa oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CREDENTIAL_HEALTH_CHECK` | `false`                              | `src/lib/credentialHealth/scheduler.ts`                                  | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` ma ọ bụ `true` iji gbanyụọ nyocha oge niile nke njikọ ndị na-enye ọrụ. A na-ewepụ ndị na-enye ọrụ ọchụchọ (SEARCH_VALIDATOR_CONFIGS na `src/lib/providers/validation/searchProviders.ts`, dịka ọmụmaatụ `tavily-search`) mgbe niile na nyocha ahụ — "ntule" ha bụ ajụjụ ezigbo ego na-akwụ ụgwọ, yabụ anaghị enyocha ahụike ha na ngụ oge (#9970).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `DEEP_HEALTH_CHECK_ENABLED`                 | `0`                                  | `src/app/api/monitoring/health/route.ts`                                 | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` iji kwe ka onye na-akpọ oku nwere ikike rịọ `/api/monitoring/health?deep=1`, nke na-enyocha elu mmecha otu ugboro kwa TTL. Ndị na-akpọ oku amaghị aha anaghị akpalite nyocha ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVENT_LOOP_STALL_THRESHOLD_MS`   | `5000`                               | `src/lib/healthzLag.ts`                                                  | Oke nkwụsị (ms) maka onye na-edekọ ihe omume mmalite: nkwụsị ogologo karịa nke a na-edekọ otu ahịrị `event loop stalled` na nkwụsị na ebe nchekwa na ọnụọgụ na-aga, ma na-ebuli `eventLoopStall` counter/oke na `/api/monitoring/health`. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ; uru na-enweghị ike ịgụ ma ọ bụ na-adịghị mma na-alaghachi na `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HOST`                                      | `0.0.0.0`                            | `scripts/dev/run-next.mjs`                                               | Adreesị njikọ maka ihe nkesa Next.js dev/start. Na-ebute ụzọ karịa ndabara `0.0.0.0` mgbe edoziri ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HOSTNAME`                                  | `127.0.0.1`                          | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`                                    | Adreesị bind nke onye na-agba Playwright na-eji mgbe ọ na-ebido Next.js. Ọ na-adabara na `127.0.0.1` maka ule hermetic. **Ejila ya maka `omniroute serve`** — jiri `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST` kama (POSIX shells na-edobe `HOSTNAME` na aha igwe n'onwe ha; `.env` enweghị ike ịgafere ya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER_HOST`                     | `0.0.0.0`                            | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                             | Adreesị bind maka `omniroute serve`. Ọ na-ezere esemokwu na variable `HOSTNAME` nke POSIX shell (nke bash/zsh na-edobe na aha igwe mgbe niile). Ọ na-alaghachi na `0.0.0.0` mgbe a na-adobeghị ya. (#6194)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |

### Ụdị Port

```
┌─────────────────────────── Single Port (default) ──────────────────────────┐
│  PORT=20128                                                                 │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://localhost:20128/v1/chat/completions                    │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Split Ports ─────────────────────────────────────┐
│  DASHBOARD_PORT=20128                                                       │
│  API_PORT=20129                                                             │
│  API_HOST=0.0.0.0                                                           │
│  → Dashboard: http://localhost:20128                                        │
│  → API:       http://0.0.0.0:20129/v1/chat/completions                     │
│  Use case: Expose API to LAN while restricting Dashboard to localhost.      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

┌─────────────────────────── Docker Production ──────────────────────────────┐
│  PROD_DASHBOARD_PORT=443   PROD_API_PORT=8443                              │
│  → Maps container ports to host ports in docker-compose.prod.yml.          │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

## 4. Nchekwa & Nkwenye Njirimara

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MACHINE_ID_SALT`                           | `endpoint-proxy-salt`                                                                       | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Salt jikọtara na njirimara ngwaike maka ịchọpụta igwe (machine fingerprinting). Gbanwee ya kwa-ntinye maka ịkewapụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SALT`                        | _(atoghị = salt a họpụtara n'efu kwa-ntinye echekwara na `<DATA_DIR>/cli-token-salt.json`)_ | `src/lib/machineToken.ts`                                | Salt HMAC maka inweta token nkwenye CLI mpaghara. Ịtọ uru a na-atụgharị token CLI niile dị n'igwe ahụ ma na-ebu n'isi n'elu salt echekwara mgbe niile. Lee `docs/security/CLI_TOKEN.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE`                        | `false`                                                                                     | `src/lib/auth`                                           | Na-eto ihe ịrịba ama `Secure` na kuki nnọkọ. **Ga-abụrịrị `true`** mgbe ị na-arụ ọrụ n'azụ HTTPS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `REQUIRE_API_KEY`                           | `false`                                                                                     | API middleware                                           | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, arịrị proxy `/v1/*` niile ga-etinye igodo API bara uru. **Ihe ịrịba ama a anaghị egbochi `GET /v1/models`**, nke na-agbaso ọnọdụ nbanye dashboard (`requireAuthForModels`) kama — ya mere, `401` si `/v1/models` apụtaghị na inference nwere nchekwa. Lee `docs/security/INFERENCE_AUTH_POSTURE.md` (#13695).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL`                      | `false`                                                                                     | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`         | Na-ekwe ka a gosipụta uru igodo API zuru oke na UI Dashboard. Enwere ike ịhazi ya site na Feature Flags Dashboard; ihe ize ndụ nchekwa na instances ndị a kesara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NO_LOG_API_KEY_IDS`                        | _(oghere)_                                                                                  | `src/lib/compliance/index.ts`                            | ID igodo API kewapụrụ site na kọma nke na-agafere ndekọ arịrị (ịrube isi GDPR).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `DEFAULT_RATE_LIMIT_PER_DAY`                | _(atoghị = enweghị oke)_                                                                    | `src/shared/utils/apiKeyPolicy.ts`                       | Ego arịrị kwa ụbọchị nke na-adochi nke a na-etinye na igodo API ndị kọlọm `rate_limits` ha bụ null. Atoghị ma ọ bụ oghere: enweghị oke zobere (#2289, #11017). `0` bụ otu ihe ahụ (enweghị oke). Ọgụgụ dị mma N na-enye N/ụbọchị, 5N/izu, 20N/ọnwa. Uru ndị na-adịghị mma mana ọ bụghị oghere na-alaghachi na windo ochie 1000/ụbọchị, 5000/izu, 20000/ọnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES`                       | `10485760` (10 MB)                                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/bodySizeGuard.ts`                 | Oke nha isi arịrị kacha elu nke a na-anabata. Na-ajụ payload ndị gafere oke a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`           | `262144` (256 KB)                                                                           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Isi arịrị n'ezie ndị ruru ma ọ bụ gafere oke a na-ewere lease nnabata dị arọ nke bụ atomic ma dị n'usoro mpaghara tupu a gbazuo JSON (ụzọ BYTE, gụnyere `POST /v1/responses`). Otụ ụzọ mgbapu ohere dị mma [#10437](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/pull/10437) dị ka nke structure-heavy; `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / [#10110](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/10110) ka na-edebe ya n'oke ka [#7849](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/OmniRoute/issues/7849) ghara ịlaghachi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_BODY_BYTES`        | `52428800` (50 MB)                                                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke siri ike nke ụzọ chat nke a na-amanye megide bytes a gụrụ n'oge nnata ekechiri oke, gụnyere arịrị ọrịa ndị nwere `Content-Length` na-efu, na-ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ na-aghọ aghụghọ; ihe gafere oke na-anata `413`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT`        | _(enweghị ntọ — enweghị oke ọnụ ọgụgụ arịrị ọrịa)_                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | **#503-fanout:** oke ọnụ ọgụgụ arịrị ọrịa (request-COUNT) ochie a ugbu a na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe a tọrọ ya n'ụzọ doro anya. Mgbe a hapụrụ ya ka ọ ghara ịtọ (nke bụ ndabara), nnabata chat dị arọ ka `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` na-achịkwa kama — ngwa ego BYTE a chọpụtara n'akpaghị aka nke e tụrụ site n'oke echekwa n'ezie nke usoro ahụ n'ime **otu usoro** (otu V8 heap). Arịrị ọrịa abụọ `/v1/responses` na-ejikọta nke ruru ihe dị ka token 750k na-agbu heaps ruru ihe dị ka 12 Gi (#7849) — ọ bụ ịdọ aka ná ntị banyere **ngwa ego echekwa**, ọ bụghị oke ngwaahịa siri ike nke 2. Usoro dị mma (heap dị n'okpuru oke ịwepụ) NWERE IKE ịnakwụtara ọtụtụ arịrị ọrịa `/v1/responses` ogologo na-arụkọ ọrụ site na `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM`. Iri ndị ahịa SSE ogologo (40–50) bụ heap + `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES` / #10110, ọ bụghị "oke kacha elu bụ 2". Ịbuli nke a n'enweghị nghọta iji "jiri host rụọ ọrụ" na-akpọtaghachi #7849. Mụbaa **heaps** site na **`DATA_DIR`s N ndị kwụrụ onwe ha** (#11024); ka ọ ghara ịbụ `replicas>1` na otu faịlụ SQLite. |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_INFLIGHT_BYTES`         | _(a chọpụtara n'akpaghị aka)_                                                               | `src/shared/middleware/admissionBudget.ts`               | **#503-fanout:** nhazi dochie maka ngwa ego byte nnata a chọpụtara n'akpaghị aka (25% nke oke echekwa V8/cgroup kacha chịkọọ e kewara site na mgbasawanye nwa oge 8x). Ọnụ ahịa a chọpụtara yana ndị e tọrọ n'ụzọ doro anya na-ejide n'etiti 8 MiB–2 GiB. Ahụ buru ibu karịa ngwa ego na-arụ ọrụ na-adachị ozugbo site na `413 body_exceeds_budget`; asọmpi dị n'etiti ahụ ndị a nwere ike ịjere ozi n'otu n'otu ka na-anọgide dị ka `503` nke a nwere ike ịgaghachi. Ndị ahịa SSE ogologo 40–50 na-arụkọ ọrụ bụ ngwa ego a + heap, ọ bụghị "oke kacha elu bụ 2" siri ike. Gụọ `chatAdmission.maxInflightBytes` / `budgetSource` / `pressureSeverity` na `/api/monitoring/health` tupu ị mee nhazi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEAP_SHED_RATIO`  | `0.75`                                                                                      | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ịwepụ n'oge nrụgide heap (`heapUsed / heap_size_limit`) maka nnabata BYTE na STRUCTURE dị arọ (#10183, #10268, #10437). Arịrị ọrịa dị arọ na-arụkọ ọrụ nke gafere `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` na-anọ naanị a na-ewepụ ya site na `503` nke a nwere ike ịgaghachi mgbe heap dịKWA na ma ọ bụ gafere ọnụ ọgụgụ a; na heap dị mma, a na-anabata ya site na healthy-headroom kama.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_HEALTHY_HEADROOM` | `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` (ndabara `1`)                                          | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ikike ndị ọzọ ekechiri oke maka ụzọ ngwa ngwa heap dị mma (#10437) na STRUCTURE **na** BYTE (`admitChatRequest`, gụnyere ahụ ndị ≥ `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_LARGE_BODY_BYTES`). Enweghị oke a, arịrị ọrịa ọ bụla nke na-arụsi ọrụ ike ma heap ya dị mma gafere nnabata na-enweghị oke kacha elu. Ozugbo ọtụtụ ikwe (leases) na-arụkọ ọrụ ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a site n'ụzọ heap dị mma, arịrị ọrịa ndị ọzọ na-arụsi ọrụ ike na-ada n'otu ụzọ echere-ekechiri-oke/ịwepụ a na-eji n'oge nrụgide heap n'ezie. `0` na-agbanyụ igafe ahụ kpamkpam.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_MESSAGE_COUNT`        | `200`                                                                                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ozi nke na-ekewa arịrị ọrịa chat dị ka ihe dị arọ ọbụlagodi mgbe ahụ ya dị n'okpuru oke byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_TOOL_COUNT`           | `64`                                                                                        | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ngwá ọrụ nke na-ekewa arịrị ọrịa chat dị ka ihe dị arọ ọbụlagodi mgbe ahụ ya dị n'okpuru oke byte.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HEAVY_ESTIMATED_TOKENS`     | `32000`                                                                                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Amụma token kpachara anya dabere n'oke eriri mkpụrụedemede (string) nke na-ekewa arịrị ọrịa dị ka ihe dị arọ; nke a bụ nnọchiteanya ọnụ ahịa nnabata, ọ bụghị tokenization ịgba ụgwọ nke onye na-enye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_HARD_MAX_MESSAGES`          | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                                                                             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`             | Oke akụkọ ihe mere eme nke chat bụ nhọrọ opt-in. A gbanyụrụ ya n'ụzọ ndabara: ọnụ ọgụgụ ozi bụ amụma iwu nkwalite, ọ bụghị àgwà zuru ụwa nile nke arịrị ọrịa, ma itinye oke ebe a na-ajụ mkparịta ụka site na `413` nke ikpeazụ tupu usoro mgbakọ wee mee ka ha nwee ike ịjere ozi. `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_MAX_HEAVY_IN_FLIGHT` yana ịwepụ nrụgide heap na-ejide uto heap oke. Tinye ọnụ ahịa dị mma na nkwalite ndị echekwa ha dị mbelata nke chọrọ oke kacha elu siri ike; ihe gafere oke ahụ na-anata `413` compact-required haziri ahazi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_NONSTREAMING_RESPONSE_BYTES` | `67108864` (64 MB)                                                                          | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/nonStreamingResponseBody.ts` | Oke siri ike maka nzaghachi upstream na-adịghị asọ mmiri (non-streaming) nke a na-ebufe n'ụzọ zuru ezu n'ime echekwa. Mgbe e gafere nke a, a na-akagbu onye na-agụ ihe upstream, arịrị ọrịa ahụ na-adachị ngwa ngwa kama ịmụba eriri mkpụrụedemede na-enweghị oke ruo mgbe heap gwụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARDING_HEADER_BUDGET_BYTES`  | `768`                                                                                       | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore/responseHeaders.ts`          | Ọnụ ọgụgụ byte kachasị a na-ebufe na waya site na headers nzaghachi upstream. Mgbe a gafere ego ahụ, a na-ahapụ headers ndị mkpa ha dị ala (dị ka `x-codex-*` omenala, `x-oai-request-id`) ka ị nọrọ n'ime oke header nke reverse-proxy na-adịkarị. Tọọ ya elu ka ị bufee metadata upstream ndị ọzọ, mana nke na-eme ka nha header nzaghachi mụbaa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CORS_ORIGIN`                               | _(a tọghị)_                                                                                 | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ndepụta a kwadoro CORS oge ochie nke otu origin. | `CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS`                      | _(a tọghị)_                                                                                 | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ndepụta a kwadoro CORS e kewapụrụ na kọma. A naghị-eziga wildcard ọ bụla ma e wezụga mgbe a haziri `CORS_ALLOW_ALL=true` n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CORS_ALLOW_ALL`                            | `false`                                                                                     | `src/server/cors/origins.ts`                             | Ụzọ mgbapu maka mmepe naanị iji zigharịa `Origin` nke browser ọ bụla. Emela ka ọ rụọ ọrụ na ntọala a na-ekekọrịta ma ọ bụ nke mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED`               | `true`                                                                                      | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Aha ọzọ oge ochie. A na-agụ toggle dashboard ya (nke bụ DB override) tupu a gụọ uru gburugburu a; `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` n'otu n'ime ha na-agbanyụ nyocha host otu `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` si agbanyụ, ọbụlagodi mgbe echekwara toggle dashboard nke flag ahụ dị ka off; uru ndị ọzọ na-ahapụ nche ahụ n'aka flags abụọ dị n'okpuru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`     | `false`                                                                                     | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Ọ na-agbanyụ nyocha host nke nche URL outbound, gụnyere mgbochi cloud-metadata (169.254.0.0/16 niile, metadata.google.internal), na ụzọ provider ndị na-eso ọnọdụ nche ahụ: nyocha URL, ịchọpụta model, base URL nke provider-node (gụnyere nodes rerank dị anya), na ule proxy-fallback, tinyere ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ metụtara provider dị ka ịweta image/media n'ebe dị anya. Ọ na-anabatakwa lekwasị webhook nzuzo, ma e wezụga cloud-metadata/link-local endpoints, nke ka na-egbochiri ọbụlagodi mgbe flag a na-arụ ọrụ. Arịrị ọnụ (chat) e zitere site na `BaseExecutor.execute()` ka na-edebe nyocha cloud-metadata ha. Ndị provider mpaghara na-arụ ọrụ na ndabara: `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` (ndabara `true`) anabatala URL mpaghara na LAN na ụzọ nyocha, ịchọpụta, na provider-node; ule proxy-fallback na lekwasị webhook nzuzo na-eleba naanị flag a anya ma na-egbochi host mpaghara/LAN mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ. Ọ na-agbochie `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS=false`. Uru echekwara site na toggle dashboard na-achị karịa mgbanwe a.                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS`       | `true`                                                                                      | `src/shared/network/outboundUrlGuardPolicy.ts`           | Kwere ka URL provider nọrọ na adreesị mpaghara/nzuzo (127.0.0.1, localhost, LAN, oke nzuzo) maka nyocha provider, ịchọpụta model, ọnụ (chat), na ụzọ outbound ndị ọzọ metụtara provider nke ọnọdụ nche ahụ na-achịkwa. **Ndabara `true`** (mpaghara nke mbụ): nche ahụ na-egbochi cloud-metadata endpoints — 169.254.0.0/16 niile tinyere aha host metadata a ma ama (metadata.google.internal, metadata.goog, Alibaba/Azure/Oracle IMDS, fd00:ec2::254). Tọọ `false` maka ọnọdụ ọha-naanị, nke na-egbochikwa host nzuzo na loopback. Nche ahụ na-enyocha hostname ma ọ bụ IP literal dị ka e dere ya, tupu ọ bụla DNS lookup; n'ọnọdụ ọha-naanị, safe-fetch wrapper na-akpesakwa hostname ma jụ azịza nzuzo ọ bụla. `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS=true` na-agbanyụ nyocha ndị a na ụzọ ahịrị ya depụtara. Ndị provider mpaghara e wuru n'ime, dị ka LM Studio, Ollama, vLLM, Llamafile, na Triton, na-agafee nyocha host niile maka nyocha igodo na ọnụ (chat). Uru echekwara site na toggle dashboard na-achị karịa mgbanwe a. (#5066)                                                       |
| `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`               | `false`                                                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/audioProviderNodes.ts`           | Ka ụzọ `/v1/audio/*` (transcriptions, speech, translations) jiri provider node dakọtara na OpenAI nke a na-anọpụ n'èzí localhost. Ọ gbanyụrụ n'ụzọ ndabara — ịchịkọ audio gaa na host dị anya na-agbanwe njirimara egress ma ghaghị ịbụ mkpebi onye na-arụ ọrụ doro anya. A na-anabata nodes loopback/nzuzo (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) mgbe niile, ha anaghịkwa emetụta. (#3963)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES`              | `false`                                                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/_shared/rerankProviderNodes.ts`          | Ka `POST /v1/rerank` (na nzọụkwụ rerank loopback nke memory engine) jiri provider node dakọtara na OpenAI nke a na-anọpụ n'èzí localhost — igwe LAN ma ọ bụ Tailscale peer na-agba TEI, Infinity, vLLM, wdg. Ọ gbanyụrụ n'ụzọ ndabara — ịchịkọ gaa na host dị anya na-agbanwe njirimara egress ma ghaghị ịbụ mkpebi onye na-arụ ọrụ doro anya. A na-anabata nodes loopback (localhost, 127.0.0.1, 172.16-31.x) mgbe niile, ha anaghịkwa emetụta. Base URL nke node dị anya ga-agafekwa amụma URL outbound nke provider (`OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_LOCAL_PROVIDER_URLS` / `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_PRIVATE_PROVIDER_URLS`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_PROVIDER_NODE_HOSTS`       | _(efu)_                                                                                     | `src/shared/network/localNodeHosts.ts`                   | Aha ndị ọbịa (kewapụrụ site na koma ma ọ bụ ohere ọcha) nke ịhazi ụzọ node ndị na-enye ọrụ na-ewere dịka nke obodo — n'ọtụtụ ebe bụ aha ọrụ Docker/Compose nke reranker, TEI ma ọ bụ audio sidecar na netwọkụ Docker nke OmniRoute (`http://reranker:8080/v1`), ka node ahụ ghara ịchọ IP container e dekapụrụ siri ike. Ndị ọbịa edepụtara tozuru oke maka `/v1/rerank`, `/v1/audio/*` na nyocha ahụike obodo na-amụghị `RERANK_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` / `AUDIO_REMOTE_PROVIDER_NODES` maka node dị anya ọ bụla, a na-akpọkwa ha kpọmkwem — ọ dịghị mgbe site na `HTTP(S)_PROXY` ma ọ bụ proxy ejikọrọ e jiri igodo mado. Ha ka na-eziga igodo API nke njikọ ahụ. Naanị aha otu akara na aha `.internal`/`.local` ka a na-anabata; a na-eleghara FQDN ọha, IP literal na aha cloud-metadata anya na ịdọ aka ná ntị. (#14635)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`     | `false`                                                                                     | `src/app/api/auth/login/route.ts`                        | Mgbe OIDC rụrụ ọrụ, gbanyụọ nbanye paswọọdụ ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkwenye njirimara ha naanị site na OIDC Single Sign-On. A na-anabatakwa alias dị larịị `OIDC_DISABLE_PASSWORD_LOGIN`; Ihe ịrịba atụmatụ (Feature Flag) nke Dashboard nke otu igodo ahụ na-edu ụzọ. (#10889)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

### Ndepụta Nlele Ịkwalite Nchekwa

```bash
# Opekempe nchekwa mmepụta:
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true        # Chọrọ HTTPS
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true           # Nyochaa oku proxy niile
ALLOW_API_KEY_REVEAL=false     # Adịghị kpughee igodo na UI
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=https://your.domain.com
MAX_BODY_SIZE_BYTES=5242880    # Oke 5 MB
```

---

## 5. Nhichapụ Ihe Ntinye & Nchedo PII

OmniRoute na-enye nchedo nwere ọkwa abụọ: nyocha ntụnye ọjọọ n'akụkụ arịrịọ na iwepụ PII n'akụkụ nzaghachi.

> **⚠️ Oke ya:** Usoro nchedo ndị a bụ nchọpụta _heuristic nke a na-agba mbọ ime nke ọma_, ọ bụghị firewall zuru oke megide ntụnye ọjọọ na prompt ma ọ bụ sistemụ PII DLP. Ha nwere ike iwepụta nchọpụta ụgha (dịka ịkọpụta prompt persona/RPG na-enweghị nsogbu dịka nke ọjọọ) na enweghị nchọpụta mgbe ihe ọjọọ dị (dịka leetspeak, oghere dị iche iche, na usoro na-abụghị Bekee). Naanị ha ezughị maka irube isi n'ụkpụrụ. Hazie mode ndị ahụ ma nwalee ha megide traffic gị tupu ịdabere na ha.

### N'akụkụ Arịrịọ: Nchedo Megide Prompt Injection

| Variable                          | Nke Ndabara  | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                |
| --------------------------------- | ------------ | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED`         | `true`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Na-eme ka nyocha ozi ndị na-abata maka usoro prompt injection rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                               |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE`            | `warn`       | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Iwu injection: `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = jụ arịrịọ site na 400. `redact` ochie **anaghị** ewepụ ederede injection; jiri `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED` maka idegharị PII dị n'arịrịọ. |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_MODE`            | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/middleware/promptInjectionGuard.ts` | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | `high`       | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Ogo ihe ize ndụ kacha nta nke `MODE=block` ga-ajụ: `high` (ndabara), `medium`, ma ọ bụ `low`. A na-elele usoro `medium` naanị ma ọ bụrụ na e belataghị ya.                           |
| `INJECTION_GUARD_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` | _(ahazighị)_ | `src/shared/utils/injectionSeverity.ts`  | Aha nnọchi ochie maka `INPUT_SANITIZER_BLOCK_THRESHOLD` — otu omume ahụ.                                                                                                             |
| `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`           | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/piiMasker.ts`        | Mgbe ọ bụ `true`, zoo PII n'arịrịọ ndị na-abata (ọ naghị adabere na mode injection).                                                                                                 |
| `CREDENTIAL_REDACTION_ENABLED`    | `false`      | `src/lib/guardrails/credentialMasker.ts` | Zoo usoro API-key / secret-token ndị a ma ama na payload arịrịọ/nzaghachi. A ga-ahọrọ ya ka ọ rụọ ọrụ; ọ na-eme dịka `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED`.                                        |

### N'akụkụ Nzaghachi: Ihe Nhichapụ PII

| Variable                         | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite         | Nkọwa                                                                        |
| -------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION`      | `false`     | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | Nyochaa nzaghachi LLM maka PII gbapụrụ tupu iziga ya nye client.             |
| `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION_MODE` | `redact`    | `src/lib/piiSanitizer.ts` | `redact` = zoo PII, `warn` = dekọọ naanị, `block` = tụfuo nzaghachi ahụ dum. |

### Ihe Nhichapụ Context nke Tokenized-Route na VS Code

| Variable                            | Nke Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ----------------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_VSCODE_SANITIZE_CONTEXT` | `1`         | `src/app/api/v1/vscode/contextSanitizer.ts` | Na-ewepụ context implicit nke active-editor (`editorContext`, `activeEditor`, `currentFile`, `selection`, `openTabs`…) n'arịrịọ `/v1/vscode/[token]/*` ma zoo ọdịnaya faịlụ nwere mmetụta dị elu ndị e tinyere n'ụzọ doro anya. Ọ dị nchebe na ndabara; tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. |

### Ọnọdụ Ojiji

| Ọnọdụ Ojiji                    | Nhazi                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Irube isi n'ụkpụrụ ụlọ ọrụ** | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=block`, `PII_REDACTION_ENABLED=true`, `PII_RESPONSE_SANITIZATION=true` (igbochi injection + izochi PII dị n'arịrịọ/nzaghachi; mode ndị ahụ anaghị adabere ibe ha) |
| **Nlekota naanị**              | `INPUT_SANITIZER_ENABLED=true`, `INPUT_SANITIZER_MODE=warn` — na-edekọ mana ọ naghị egbochi mgbe ọ bụla                                                                                                                 |
| **Ojiji nkeonwe**              | Hapụ ha niile ka ha ghara ịrụ ọrụ — enweghị overhead ọ bụla                                                                                                                                                             |

---

## 6. Iwu Ngwá Ọrụ & Ịhazi Ụzọ

| Ngbanwe                                                     | Nke dabara adaba             | Faịlụ isi iyi                                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `TOOL_POLICY_MODE`                                          | `disabled`                   | `src/lib/toolPolicy.ts`                         | Na-achịkwa ohere LLM nwere ịkpọ ngwá ọrụ/ọrụ. `allowlist` = naanị ngwá ọrụ a depụtara, `denylist` = niile ma e wezụga ndị a depụtara, `disabled` = enweghị mgbochi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_PATH`                              | `./config/payloadRules.json` | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ JSON nke iwu mmegharị payload (ntweaks upstream maka ọ bụla model/protocol).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PAYLOAD_RULES_RELOAD_MS`                         | `5000`                       | `open-sse/services/payloadRules.ts`             | Oge ntinyegharị (ms) maka ịtinyegharị faịlụ iwu payload ozugbo (hot-reload). Nke kacha nta `1000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PREFER_CLAUDE_CODE_FOR_UNPREFIXED_CLAUDE_MODELS` | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Nhọrọ opt-in: zigharịa model IDs `claude-*` dị ọcha sitere n'aka ndị ahịa Claude Code site n'akaụntụ Claude Code OAuth kama ịchọ prefix provider. Prefix provider e kapọrọ n'ụzọ doro anya ka na-achị. Enwere ike ịtọkwa ya site na toggle dashboard dị na ibe provider Claude.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDACT_THINKING`                                 | `true`                       | `open-sse/executors/base/redactThinkingBeta.ts` | Tinye ya `false` iji gbochie OmniRoute ịgbakwụnye beta Anthropic `redact-thinking-2026-02-12` n'onwe ya (blọọgụ echiche dị efu nke naanị signature ka dị). Arịrị ndị nwere `thinking.display: "summarized"` na-awụla ya n'agbanyeghị ihe ọ bụla; beta redact a kparịtara ụka ya na onye ahịa ma ọ bụ opt-in `requestDefaults.redactThinking` dakọtara na CC na-edebe ya. Arịrị Claude OAuth na-edebe beta (ọ bụ akụkụ nke fingerprint Claude Code OAuth) ma e wezụga feature flag `CLAUDE_OAUTH_SUMMARIZED_THINKING_UNREDACTED` nke gbanyere na mbụ na-arụ ọrụ.   |
| `BARE_MODEL_DEFAULT_OVERRIDE_LEGACY`                        | `false`                      | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                    | Omume ochie `resolveBareModelToConnectionDefault` (#4825): jiri `defaultModel` nke njikọ a họọrọ dochie aha model ọ bụla dị ọcha. Adagbụ ya na mbụ, naanị `auto` ma ọ bụ model efu ka a na-edochi; ọ na-ede otu ịdọ aka ná ntị maka ọ bụla abụọ mgbe a gbadoro ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `COMBO_CONCURRENCY_PER_MODEL`                               | `3`                          | `open-sse/services/comboConfig.ts`              | Oke concurrency maka ọ bụla model maka combos round-robin (#9100). E kpochiri semaphore nke combo round-robin na arịrị 3 concurrent maka ọ bụla model n'enweghị ndochi, nke na-etinye okporo ụzọ concurrency dị elu n'usoro n'azụ oke ahụ. A kwadoro ya ka ọ ruo `>= 1`, a machiri ya na `<= 32`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `DISABLE_CONTEXT_WINDOW_CHECKS`                             | `false`                      | `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`                 | Nhọrọ opt-in dị ize ndụ nke na-awụla nyocha context-window / max-input-token mpaghara nke OmniRoute maka arịrị otu model kpọmkwem. Ndị na-enye upstream ka na-amanye oke ha n'ezie; mgbịchị prompt na oke output-token nke model n'onwe ya ka na-arụ ọrụ. Usoro amị amị bụ: ndochi Feature Flags DB > ngbanwe gburugburu ebe ọrụ > nke dabara adaba; adịghị achọ ịmalitegharị.                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`                           | _(atọghị ya)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Akwụkwọ YAML `providers:` n'ahịrị (RIC-738, D4). Mgbe a tọrọ ya (na ma ọ bụ n'enweghị blọọgụ `strategy:`), `/v1/chat/completions` na-atụgharị gaa na ọnụ ọnọdụ self-hosted jikọtara nke dakọtara na OpenAI kama pipeline igwe ojii. Mgbe atọghị ya (nke dabara adaba): ụzọ ahụ na-aga kpọmkwem n'ime pipeline igwe ojii dị adị. Lee `docs/routing/SELF_HOSTED_OPENAI_ENTRY.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS_FILE`                      | _(atọghị ya)_                | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ YAML nke nwere otu akwụkwọ `providers:` dị ka `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, maka ndị mmasị ha bụ faịlụ karịa env var n'ahịrị. Nke ọ bụla n'ime ha na-agbanye ọnụ ọnọdụ self-hosted.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_API_KEY`                             | _(atọghị ya — ụzọ mepere)_   | `open-sse/services/selfHostedEntry.ts`          | Igodo API e kọrọ nke bụ nhọrọ maka ọnụ ọnọdụ self-hosted jikọtara (D5 scaffold, a debebe ya maka usoro quota per-key). Mgbe a tọrọ ya, arịrị ga-agụnye `Authorization: Bearer <key>`. Mgbe atọghị ya: ụzọ ahụ mepere emepe, na-akwado usoro local-provider self-hosted dị adị (ịrụ ọrụ loopback/trusted-network).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`                            | _(ahazighị)_                 | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Akwụkwọ YAML `strategy:` e tinyere n'ahịrị maka injin nhọpụta ụzọ kpebiri nkpebi (M2/RIC-740, D3) — blacklist/whitelist, ọgbaji cooldown, ụgwọ-dị-mkpa, nke na-amata latency, agbụ ndabere. Ọ na-anọchi anya ngọngọ `strategy:` e tinyere n'ahịrị n'ime `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_PROVIDERS`, kwa igodo. Lee `docs/routing/DETERMINISTIC_ROUTING.md`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY_FILE`                       | _(ahazighị)_                 | `open-sse/services/routingStrategies.ts`        | Ụzọ na-eri faịlụ YAML nke nwere otu akwụkwọ `strategy:` ahụ dị ka `OMNIROUTE_SELF_HOSTED_STRATEGY`, maka ndị ebunye ndị na-ahọrọ faịlụ karịa agbanwe gburugburu e tinyere n'ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONVERSATION_TRACKING`                   | _(ahazighị)_                 | `open-sse/services/conversationTracker.ts`      | Tinye `1` iji kwụsị ịnakọta akụkọ mkparịta ụka. `resolveConversationId()` na-eweghachi nsonaazụ a na-esoghị tupu ọ gụọ SQLite ma ọ bụ kọwaa akụkọ ozi, a kpuchirikwa ID nnọkọ ndị onye ahịa nyere. Nhazi nnọkọ nhọpụta ụzọ adịghị agbanwe, a naghịkwa ehichapụ ndekọ ndị dị adị. Maka ndị ebunye ndị na-ejighị nlele mkparịta ụka nke dashboard ma chọọ ka tebụl ntụgharị kwụsị ito.                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_API_KEY`                                   | _(ahazighị)_                 | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Igodo API maka atụmatụ onye nhọpụta ụzọ auto-combo `nadir` nke opt-in, nke a na-eji mgbe `config.nadir.apiKey` nke combo ahụ adịghị ahazi. Nhọrọ: oku ndị na-enweghị igodo na-ada na ọkwa amaghị ama nke Nadir, nke e gbochiri ọnụego kwa IP, n'ihi ya enweghị ike iji ya n'azụ ọnụ ụzọ ekekọrịtara. Ihe nzuzo; họrọ ubi kwa-combo mgbe combos ji akaụntụ dị iche iche.                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_NADIR_BASE_URL`                                  | `https://api.getnadir.com`   | `open-sse/services/autoCombo/nadirStrategy.ts`  | Isi mmalite nke API mkpebi Nadir, nke a na-eji mgbe `config.nadir.baseUrl` nke combo ahụ adịghị ahazi. Ọ dị mkpa naanị maka Nadir nke a na-anabata n'onwe, nke na-edebe isi prompt n'ime netwọk gị. A na-anabata `/v1` nke dị n'ọgwụgwụ ma wepụ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW`                           | `provider`                   | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Windo izu kwa maka ókè ojiji USD kwa igodo API (ịmanye arịrịọ, `GET /api/keys/{id}/usage-limits`, `@@om-usage`). `provider`: izu na-agwụ na mmalitegharị izu mbụ kacha n'etiti njikọ upstream ndị igodo nwere ike iru, ma ọ bụ bụ ụbọchị asaa na-agagharị mgbe ọ dịghị nke a maara. `calendar`: izu mpaghara site na Mọnde 00:00 ruo Mọnde 00:00 na-esote, nke yiri otu maka igodo ọ bụla. A na-atụ aro `calendar` mgbe otu ọdọ akaụntụ upstream nke ọtụtụ igodo nke e kwadoro ego n'otu n'otu na-ekekọrịta. Ọnụ ọgụgụ ndị ezighị ezi na-alaghachi na `provider`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_WEEKLY_WINDOW_TIMEZONE`                  | _(mpaghara oge usoro)_       | `src/lib/usage/apiKeyUsageLimits.ts`            | Mpaghara oge IANA (dịka ọmụmaatụ `Asia/Shanghai`) nke na-akọwa Mọnde 00:00 maka windo izu `calendar`. Mgbe a hazighị ya ma ọ bụ ọ bụghị nke ezi: mpaghara oge nke usoro OmniRoute (`TZ` nke host ma ọ bụ container, nke a na-adịkarị ahazighị, ya bụ Oge Ụwa E Jikọrọ Ọnụ, n'ime onyonyo container). A na-elegharị ya anya na ọnọdụ `provider`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_MAX_TOOLS_LIMIT`                                 | `128`                        | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`                  | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ngwá ọrụ e kwenyere n'otu arịrịọ (ndabara OpenAI). Naanị ọnụ ọgụgụ zuru eke dị mma; ọnụ ọgụgụ ndị a hazighị ma ọ bụ ezighị ezi na-edebe `128`. Onye na-ebubata nke nwere ókè a maara ma ọ bụ nke ọ kwadoro (`grok-cli` 200, `nvidia` 1536) ka na-etinye ókè ya — nke a na-akpali naanị ókè kacha elu nke ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROMPT_EMULATE_TOOLS`                            | _(ọrụ)_                      | `open-sse/executors/syntxChat.ts`               | Ịkpọ ngwá ọrụ nke SYNTX.ai na-emume n'ime prompt: mgbe a hazighị ya ma ọ bụ `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` ọ na-etinye katalọgụ ngwá ọrụ mpaghara n'ime prompt; ọnụ ọgụgụ ọ bụla ọzọ (dịka `0`) na-agafe ntinye ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
