## 7. URL na Mmekọrịta Igwe Ojii

| Agbanwe                                     | Ndabara                                                   | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `BASE_URL`                                  | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL nke akụkụ sava nke ọrụ mmekọrịta ime ga-eji kpọọ `/api/sync/cloud`. Debe nke a dị ka URL loopback/kọnteena ọbụna mgbe e ji proxy mee ka ngwa ahụ dị n'ihu ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CLOUD_URL`                                 | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | URL njedebe mbufe igwe ojii (atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ pụrụ iche).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CLOUD_SYNC_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `12000`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Oge nkwụsị HTTP maka arịrịọ mmekọrịta igwe ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_BUILD_PROFILE`                   | `full`                                                    | Nhazi owuwu Webpack                              | Profaịlụ oge owuwu (tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `minimal` iji wepụ modul nwere ikike kpamkpam na ngwugwu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                  | _mmepụta standalone nke .build/_                          | `scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`          | Mgbanwe nnọchi nke oge owuwu maka ndekọ mmepụta standalone nke usoro idokọta ọnụ mgbe owuwu gasịrị na-eji. Ọ bụghị ntọala oge arụmọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET`               | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                                        | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Ihe nzuzo nkekọrịta a na-eji enyocha mbinye aka HMAC-SHA256 nke nzaghachi Mmekọrịta Igwe Ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRETS`              | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji kwe ka njedebe Mmekọrịta Igwe Ojii dochie nzere mpaghara. Ndabara bụ `false`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_ENFORCE_SIGNATURE`    | `false`                                                   | `src/lib/cloudSync.ts`                           | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji jụ nzaghachi Mmekọrịta Igwe Ojii na-enweghị mbinye aka mgbe a hazighị ihe nzuzo mpaghara (#13679). A na-enyocha mbinye aka dị adị mgbe niile — a na-ajụkwa ya mgbe niile ma ọ bụrụ na edoghị `OMNIROUTE_CLOUD_SYNC_SECRET` — n'agbanyeghị ọkọlọtọ a. Ndabara ga-agbanwe ka mmanye na v3.9.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ZED_IMPORT_LEGACY_ONE_STEP`      | `false`                                                   | `src/app/api/providers/zed/import/route.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `true` iji laghachi n'omume otu nzọụkwụ nke v3.8.5, "bubata ihe niile", na-enweghị nkwenye onye ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                      | `http://localhost:20128`                                  | OAuth, Dashboard, mmekọrịta                      | URL ọha maka OAuth redirect_uri, njikọ Dashboard, na URL ọha ndị emepụtara. Tọọ nke a ka ọ bụrụ URL ọha kwụsiri ike mgbe OAuth callbacks ma ọ bụ njikọ ihe nchọgharị emepụtara ga-eji onye nnabata reverse-proxy ọkọlọtọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_CLOUD_URL`                     | _(ọ tọgbọ chakoo)_                                        | Akụkụ klayent                                    | Oyiri nke `CLOUD_URL` n'akụkụ klayent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL`                       | _(edobereghị)_                                            | `src/shared/services/cloudSyncScheduler.ts`      | Nhọrọ ndabere ochie maka `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_PORT`                          | _(edobereghị — na-eji `PORT` dị ka ndabere)_              | `src/shared/hooks/useDisplayBaseUrl.ts`          | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri ndabere n'akụkụ klayenti maka URL ngosi mgbe a maghị origin ọ bụla (SSR/nnwale); a na-agụ ya tupu `PORT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_BASE_URL`                 | _(edobereghị)_                                            | Onye na-achọpụta origin ọha, URL onyonyo         | Origin OmniRoute nke ihe nchọgharị na-eji, nke nwere mkpa kachasị elu maka imepụta URL ọha na nkwado izi ezi nke origin ihe nchọgharị na-abụghị dashboard. Tọọ nke a mgbe OpenWebUI ma ọ bụ relay ọzọ ji URL ime rute OmniRoute mana ihe nchọgharị onye ọrụ ga-esi na LAN, tunnel, ma ọ bụ origin ọha nweta mgbasa ozi emepụtara. **Etinyela** `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL`           | _(edobereghị)_                                            | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | URL zuru ezu nke manifest plugin provider a na-akpọsa nye klayenti sidecar. Mgbe edobereghị ya, OmniRoute na-ewepụta `/api/v1/provider-plugin-manifest` site na origin arịrịọ ma ọ bụ HOST/PORT.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_PROTOCOL`                 | `http`                                                    | `open-sse/config/providerPluginManifestUrl.ts`   | Protocol a na-eji mgbe a na-ewepụta URL manifest plugin provider site na HOST/PORT na-enweghị origin arịrịọ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `https` n'azụ proxy ọha na-akwụsị TLS mgbe edobeghị `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_MANIFEST_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY`                     | _(edobereghị)_                                            | `src/server/origin/publicOrigin.ts`              | Ụdị ntụkwasị obi nhọrọ maka headers origin ọha ebugharị. Edobereghị = atụkwasịla `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` obi maka mkpebi nchekwa. `true` / `loopback` na-atụkwasị host/proto ebugharị obi naanị site na proxy loopback e tinyere token. `private` / `lan` na-atụkwasịkwa ndị proxy nọ na LAN nkeonwe obi, ma na-enye bridge `API_PORT` ohere idowe `X-Forwarded-For` nke peer dị otú ahụ. N'ebe mmepụta, ọ ka mma ịtọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` kpọmkwem.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                          | _(edobereghị)_                                            | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | URL callback ọha maka ọrụ kie.ai asynchronous. Ndochi nwere mkpa kachasị elu tupu `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL` na `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_KIE_CALLBACK_URL`                | _(edobereghị)_                                            | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Ụzọ ọzọ e si ede `KIE_CALLBACK_URL`. A na-eji ya dị ka ndabere mgbe edobereghị variable bụ isi.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_PUBLIC_URL`                      | _(edobereghị)_                                            | `open-sse/utils/kieTask.ts`                      | Origin ọha a na-eji ahazi URL callback asynchronous. Ndabere nwere mkpa kacha ala maka callback kie.ai; a na-ejikwa ya dị ka URL ọha izugbe maka relay ndị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CROF_USAGE_URL`                  | `https://crof.ai/usage_api/`                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint nchọpụta oke CrofAI nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLINEPASS_USAGE_URL`             | `https://api.cline.bot/api/v1/users/me/plan/usage-limits` | `open-sse/services/usage/clinepass.ts`           | Endpoint oke ClinePass nke awa 5 / kwa izu / kwa ọnwa nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPENCODE_QUOTA_URL`              | `https://opencode.ai/zen/go/v1/usage`                     | `open-sse/services/opencodeQuotaFetcher.ts`      | Endpoint ojiji OpenCode Go gọọmentị nke API key na-eme nkwado njirimara, nke ibe Usage na-eji. Dochie ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS`           | `true`                                                    | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Mepụta headers njirimara OpenCode CLI (User-Agent, x-opencode-client/project, UUID arịrịọ/session) na arịrịọ opencode-go/zen upstream nke klayenti ezipụghị, ka Cloudflare dị na VPS egress wee nabata ha (#6210/#5997). Agbanyere ya na ndabara kemgbe #10571; jiri `false`/`0`/`no`/`off` gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OPENCODE_USER_AGENT`                       | `opencode/1.18.31`                                        | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | User-Agent ndabara a na-eji mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ ma enweghị ngbanwe `<PROVIDER>_USER_AGENT` nke onye na-eweta ọ bụla ahaziri. A na-etinye ya naanị na ndị mmejuputa opencode. N’arịrịọ na-enweghị igodo nke upstream gbochiri, a ga-eji ndabara a dochie uru ahaziri nke na-enweghị `opencode/<version >= 1.17>` kama ịjụ arịrịọ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_FORWARD_ATTRIBUTION`             | `1` (dị ọkụ)                                              | `open-sse/utils/opencodeHeaders.ts`              | Ziga nkụnye isi njirimara-ngwa onye ahịa (`x-session-id`, `x-title`, `http-referer`) nye ndị na-eweta upstream, ka arịrịọ malitere n’ime harness edepụtara wee debe njirimara ya mgbe ọ na-agafe router (mgbochi ndị mbipụta OpenRouter na-adabere na ndị a). `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` na-edobe njirimara registry/operator ma na-ewepụ uru onye ahịa nyere. Nkụnye isi omenala onye ọrụ sistemụ haziri na-enwe mmeri mgbe niile karịa uru onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OPENCODE_CLIENT`                           | `desktop`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye isi `x-opencode-client` a mepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OPENCODE_PROJECT`                          | `global`                                                  | `open-sse/executors/opencode.ts`                 | Uru maka nkụnye isi `x-opencode-project` a mepụtara mgbe `OPENCODE_SYNTHESIZE_CLI_HEADERS` dị ọkụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_IP_LOCK_PROVIDERS`        | _(ahazighị)_                                              | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Otu ndị na-eweta dị irè nke a na-achịkọta ọdịda upstream ha dịka IP ọpụpụ si dị (otu 429 na-akwụsị njikọ ibe ya niile na-ekekọrịta IP ahụ). Mgbe ahazighị ya, ọ na-eji ndabara ọkwa efu `opencode,opencode-go,opencode-cli`; `none`/`off`/`false`/`0` na-agbanyụ mgbasa ọdịda ahụ kpamkpam; ndepụta e kewara na rikoma na-anọchi otu ahụ. A na-achịkọta atụmatụ akwụ ụgwọ dịka akaụntụ na upstream, ya mere ha nwere ike ịgbanyụ ya n’enweghị nsogbu (#9611).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_EGRESS_QUOTA_ROTATION_PROVIDERS` | _(ahazighị)_                                              | `open-sse/config/providerErrorRules.ts`          | Oke ntụgharị ọpụpụ mgbe a jụrụ quota. Mgbe ahazighị ya, ọ na-ewepụ otu onye otu pool nwa oge mgbe 429 mere naanị maka ezinụlọ a na-achịkọta dịka IP nke dị n’elu; `all` na-agbatị ndekọ ahụ ruo ndị na-eweta niile, nke mere na rate limit enwetara site n’aka otu onye otu ga-eme ka nhọrọ na-esote jiri ụzọ ọpụpụ ọzọ. A na-eleghara uru ọ bụla na-abụghị `all` anya. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED` (dị ọkụ na ndabara). A na-eji adreesị ọpụpụ dịka igodo store, ọ bụghị (onye na-eweta, adreesị), ya mere 429 a gbasapụrụ agbasapụ na-akwụsị onye otu ahụ maka ndị na-eweta niile ruo mgbe oge ahụ gwụsịrị (nkeji 5, na-abawanye okpukpu abụọ ruo 15). A na-agụ ya dịka nsonaazụ nzaghachi ọ bụla si dị, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ na-enweghị ịmalitegharị. |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_REQUEST_CONTRACT`       | _(ahazighị)_                                              | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `off` iji kwụsị imegharị body nke arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo (akara streaming na ndepụta tool). A ka na-etinye nkụnye isi. A na-agụ ya n’arịrịọ ọ bụla, ya mere mgbanwe na-amalite ịrụ ọrụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OPENCODE_FREE_TIER_PLACEHOLDER_TOOLS`      | _(ahazighị)_                                              | `open-sse/executors/opencodeFreeTierContract.ts` | Aha tool ndị e kewara na rikoma a ga-ekwupụta n’arịrịọ OpenCode na-enweghị igodo nke na-enweghị nke ọ bụla, mgbe a hụbeghị ihe ọ bụla maka model ahụ. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ na-alaghachi n’otu placeholder a gwara model ka ọ ghara ịkpọ. Ọ kachasị ndenye 32, `[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,63}`; a na-eleghara ndenye na-adịghị irè anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_URL`          | `https://ollama.com/settings`                             | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | URL ntọala Ollama Cloud a na-eji enweta quota site na scraping. Gbanwee ya maka relay / test fixture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_CLOUD_BALANCE_URL`        | `https://ollama.com/api/balance`                          | `open-sse/services/ollamaCloudBalance.ts`        | Endpoint balance upstream Ollama Cloud edekọtara (`GET https://ollama.com/api/balance`, `Authorization: Bearer <API key>`). A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe module na-eburu. A na-ebu ụzọ jiri ya maka quota Ollama Cloud mgbe njikọ ahụ nwere API key; scraper cookie nke ibe ntọala na-anọgide dịka nhọrọ ndabere. Gbanwee ya maka relay / test fixture.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`                       | _(ahazighị)_                                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` a na-eji enweta quota site na scraping ibe ntọala. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OLLAMA_CLOUD_USAGE_COOKIE`                 | _(ahazighị)_                                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var cookie Ollama Cloud `__Secure-session` ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_OLLAMA_USAGE_COOKIE`             | _(edobeghị)_                                              | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Env var kuki `__Secure-session` ọzọ maka Ollama Cloud, nke a na-eji tupu aha mkpirikpi ndị ọzọ. Ọ bụ ozi nzuzo; họrọ oghere Dashboard nke njikọ ọ bụla mgbe ahaziiri ọtụtụ akaụntụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEWHISPERER_BASE_URL`          | `https://codewhisperer.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`           | `open-sse/services/usage.ts`                     | Endpoint oke ojiji CodeWhisperer (AWS Kiro). Gbanwee ya maka relay / fixture nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

> [!IMPORTANT]
> Mgbe ị na-ebunye ya n'azụ reverse proxy (nginx, Caddy), tọọ `NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL` ka ọ bụrụ URL ọhaneze gị kwụsiri ike (dịka ọmụmaatụ, `https://omniroute.example.com`) mgbe callback OAuth ma ọ bụ njikọ ọhaneze emepụtara ga-eji hostname ahụ. Na-enweghị nke a, callback OAuth nwere ike ịda n'ihi na redirect_uri agaghị adaba, njikọ ọhaneze emepụtara nwekwara ike iduga na origin ime container ahụ.
>
> Debe `BASE_URL` dịka URL loopback/container ime maka ọrụ server-na-server. Ejila `Origin` nke ihe nchọgharị ma ọ bụ hostname ọhaneze maka self-fetch ime nke nwere credential.
>
> Ndekọ Dashboard e ji njirimara kwado anaghị achọ URL base ọhaneze kwụ ọtọ: Dashboard na-ezipụ arịrịọ same-origin na-adịghị nchebe yana token CSRF ejikọtara na session. OmniRoute ka na-achịkọta nkwado public-origin n'otu ebe maka njikọta ihe nchọgharị ndị na-abụghị nke Dashboard: a na-ebu ụzọ tụkwasị env var URL ọhaneze akọwapụtara obi; a na-eleghara header `Forwarded` / `X-Forwarded-*` raw anya belụsọ ma agbanyere `OMNIROUTE_TRUST_PROXY` ma jiri token kaa proxy peer dị nso akara dịka nke a tụkwasịrị obi. Ejila ntọala CORS dozie arịrịọ Dashboard same-origin; CORS bụ naanị maka client ihe nchọgharị cross-origin.

---

## 8. Proxy Ọpụpụ

Dufe oku ndị na-enye LLM n'elu site na proxy HTTP ma ọ bụ SOCKS5 maka ịchịkwá ọpụpụ, ịkụzi ụzọ geo, ma ọ bụ izochi IP.

`OMNIROUTE_UPSTREAM_HTTP2_ENABLED` na-abụ `true` dị ka ndabara. Tinye ya na `false`, `0`, `no`, ma ọ bụ `off` iji manye HTTP/1.1 na ndị nkesa Undici (dispatchers) dị na `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts` na `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`: ọpụpụ ozugbo, proxy HTTP(S)/SOCKS, ndị relay, na nnwalegharị socket ọhụrụ ha. Nke a na-echekwa nyocha asambodo TLS, nyocha njirimara proxy, ókè njikọ, na ntọala keep-alive. Malitegharịa OmniRoute mgbe ị gbanwere ya, n'ihi na a na-echekwa ndị nkesa ahụ ma a na-emepụta ndị nnọchi relay na mmalite. Ịgbanwe a anaghị achịkwa wreq TLS fingerprinting ma ọ bụ ụzọ mbufe pụrụ iche nke onye na-enye ọrụ.

| Agbanwe                                  | Ndabara           | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                              | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`                    | `true`            | `open-sse/executors`                           | Mee ka nọchiteanya SOCKS5 proxy rụọ ọrụ maka oku upstream. Jiri `false` wepụ onwe gị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY`        | `true`            | N'akụkụ onye ahịa                              | Ịmata n'akụkụ onye ahịa banyere nnweta SOCKS5.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`             | `true`            | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Okọlọtọ atụmatụ nke na-arụ ọrụ n'ụzọ ndabara (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); nkwusi dashboard DB ka emeri). Ngwakọta proxy na ntụgharị kwa-akaụntụ na-akwụsị ịnyegharị ọzọ onye otu nke dara ọdụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya (nyocha TCP a jụrụ, ma ọ bụ 429 site na ya) ruo oge nke na-agba abụọ mgbe ọ megharịrị, ruo n'oke. `false` na-eweghachi nhọrọ nkịtị.                                                          |
| `SELECTOR_CONTROL_ALLOWLIST`             | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | `src/lib/proxySubscription/selectorGuard.ts`   | Ndepụta-ekwere maka ebumnuche nchịkwa-nhọrọ nke ime-ime gafere loopback (ndị ọbịa ma ọ bụ IP e kewapụrụ site na kọma/ọghere, `*` na-emeghe LAN/private ma ọ bụghị metadata/link-local mgbe ọzọ). A na-ekwe loopback site na http/https n'ụzọ ndabara. Uru dashboard DB na-emeri agbanwe a mgbe edobere ya ma ọ bụghị efu.                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_BASE_MS`                | `300000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oge ntọala (ms) nke mwepụ 429, nke na-agba abụọ mgbe ọ megharịrị. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. A kechiri ya n'ime `1000..3600000`; nke gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke na-agụghị na-alaghachi na ndabara tinyere ịdọ aka ná ntị.                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_QUOTA_429_MAX_MS`                 | `900000`          | `open-sse/utils/proxyRefusalMemory.ts`         | Oke (ms) nke mwepụ 429. A na-agụ ya otu ugboro mgbe mmalite. A kechiri ya n'ime `1000..3600000`; nke gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke na-agụghị na-alaghachi na ndabara tinyere ịdọ aka ná ntị. Mgbe oke dị n'okpuru ntọala, ọ na-alaghachi na ndabara. Ibelata oke ahụ na-ebelatakwa ichefu usoro: ndepụta na-akpochapụ `2 x max` mgbe oge gachara.                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_POOL_SHARED_EGRESS_ORDER`         | `false`           | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`             | Okọlọtọ atụmatụ nke chọrọ itinye aka (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); nkwusi dashboard DB ka emeri). Maka ndị na-enye nke a kenyere kesi n'ịdọba site na adreesị egress, hazie onye otu ngwakọta nke na-ekekọrịta adreesị egress a hụrụ nke onye otu a jụrụ n'oge na-adịbeghị anya n'okpuru ndị otu dị mma. Nhazi naanị, a naghị apụ ya. Ọ chọrọ `PROXY_SKIP_RECENTLY_FAILED`, nke na-emepụta mgbaàmà ọjụjụ ọ na-agụ. |
| `PROXY_WEBHOOK_REBOUND_MS`               | `300000`          | `src/lib/proxyEvents/proxyTransitionBridge.ts` | Windo mbịaru (ms) na-ejide ịdọ aka ná ntị webhook `proxy.set_aside` ugboro ugboro maka onye otu ngwakọta ọ bụla ka nnukwu ọjụjụ ziga otu mmemme. A na-agụ ya mgbe ọ na-emepụta ọ bụla. A kechiri ya n'ime `1000..3600000`; nke gafere oke ma ọ bụ nke na-agụghị na-alaghachi na ndabara na-enweghị mkpọtụ.                                                                                                                        |
| `HTTP_PROXY`                             | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | HTTP proxy maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `HTTPS_PROXY`                            | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | HTTPS proxy maka oku upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ALL_PROXY`                              | _(a na-edobeghị)_ | Ọkọlọtọ Node.js                                | Proxy zuru ụwa ọnụ (na-akwado `socks5://`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_ECHO_URL`               | _(edobeghị)_      | `src/lib/proxyEchoTarget.ts`                   | Na-edobe ebe lekwasị anya echo-IP nke nyocha egress proxy na-eji na otu URL. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, nyocha ahụ na-anwa `api64.ipify.org` tupu `api4.ipify.org` ka a ghara ịkọ tunnels IPv4 naanị dị ka ndị nwụrụ anwụ (#9694).                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `NO_PROXY`                               | _(edobeghị)_      | Node.js ọkọlọtọ                                | Aha host/IP kewapụrụ site na comma iji gafee proxy ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROXY_DISPATCHER_CONNECTIONS` | `32`              | `open-sse/utils/proxyDispatcher.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ sockets kacha elu n'otu oge maka onye nkesa proxy HTTP/SOCKS a kwadoro n'eche. Osimiri SSE dị ogologo dị ka Codex `/v1/responses` chọrọ karịa otu njikọ mgbe ọtụtụ arịrị na-ekekọrịta otu proxy n'ọkwa akaụntụ. A na-edobe oke ụkpụrụ karịa `256`.                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SOCKS_HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `10000`           | `open-sse/utils/socksConnectorWithFamily.ts`   | Oge njedebe handshake SOCKS5 (njikọ) na ms. Welite ya mgbe otu ebe obibi gateway host nwetara concurrency dị elu (dịka ọmụmaatụ arịrị 100 n'otu oge) — handshake n'ezie nwere ike ịgafere 10s n'okpuru pool juru n'afọ ọbụlagodi na a nwere ike iru proxy ahụ, nke ga-apụta na ọzọzọ dị ka `[Proxy Fast-Fail] Proxy unreachable` adịgboroja. Edobere oke na `120000`.                                                             |
| `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN`                        | `false`           | `src/sse/handlers/chatHelpers.ts`              | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), a na-**jụ (fail-closed)** arịrị nke proxy e kenyere ya dara na ịdozi kama ịlaghachi na njikọ kpọmkwem — nke na-egbochi mwepụ IP n'ezie. Tọọ `true` iji weghachi usoro DIRECT fallback ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT`                 | `false`           | `open-sse/executors`                           | Jiri wreq-js ghọọ TLS fingerprint (na-akọ Chrome 124). Na-eboju mgbochi JA3/JA4.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TLS_FINGERPRINT_PROVIDERS`              | _(edobeghị)_      | `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`                 | Ndepụta ndị na-enye ọrụ a nabatara kewapụrụ site na comma maka ụzọ TLS proxied ọhụrụ (`open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts`). TLS kpọmkwem na-edebe omume ochie ya mgbe edobeghị ya; naanị ndị na-enye ọrụ ndị a ka na-agafee ọzara fingerprint Chrome-124. A wepụrụ Groq (`api.groq.com`) ọbụlagodi mgbe edobeghị ndepụta a nabatara, n'ihi na Chrome JA3 na-akpali Cloudflare 1010 ebe undici n'ala na-eru API ahụ.                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_TURNSTILE_IGNORE_TLS_ERRORS`  | `false`           | `open-sse/services/claudeTurnstileSolver.ts`   | Kwe ka ihe ọdịnaya ihe nchọgharị Claude Turnstile Playwright leghara njehie asambodo HTTPS anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                            | Nhazi                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| **SOCKS5 site na ọwa SSH**                       | `ALL_PROXY=socks5://127.0.0.1:7890`, `ENABLE_SOCKS5_PROXY=true`                                                           |
| **Proxy HTTP ụlọ ọrụ**                           | `HTTP_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `HTTPS_PROXY=http://proxy.corp.com:3128`, `NO_PROXY=localhost,internal.corp.com` |
| **Mgbochi fingerprint**                          | `ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true` — chọrọ `wreq-js` (tinyere n'ime ya)                                                        |
| **Egress a na-achịkwa / enweghị ohere kpọmkwem** | Hapụ `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=false` (ndabara). Arịrị na-adaa kpamkpam mgbe proxy adịghị kama ịkwụsa IP site na njikọ kpọmkwem.   |
| **Ochie / dev — kwe ka njikọ kpọmkwem daa**      | `PROXY_FAIL_OPEN=true`. Na-eweghachi omume tupu ịsie ike: a na-eji njikọ kpọmkwem mgbe ịdozi proxy dara.                  |

> **Mara (NVIDIA validation bypass — #3226):** Endpoint nkwenye igodo API nke NVIDIA
> na-akwụsịtụ mgbe a gafere ya site na fetch proxy/TLS-patched zuru ụwa ọnụ (undici dispatcher → 504).
> `src/lib/providers/validation.ts::directHttpsRequest()` na-agafee proxy patch n'echiche
> maka otu oku nkwenye ahụ site na iji `safeOutboundFetch({ bypassProxyPatch: true })`.
> Nke a bụ nnwere onwe e dere akwụkwọ nke nwere oke — ọ **dịghị** metụta egress chat/ojiji.
> Nnwale `tests/unit/proxy-bypass-scope-guard-3226.test.ts` na-edobe oke bypass ahụ.

---

## 9. Mmekọrịta Ngwá Ọrụ CLI

Ọ na-achịkwa otu OmniRoute si achọpụta ma malite CLI sidecars (Claude Code, Codex, wdg.).

| Mgbanwe                           | Ndiizuzu                         | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| --------------------------------- | -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_MODE`                        | `auto`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | `auto` = chọọ PATH nke sistemụ; `manual` = jiri nanị ụzọ e kwupụtara n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_EXTRA_PATHS`                 | _(a tọghị)_                      | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ndepụta PATH ndị ọzọ maka ịchọpụta binary CLI (e ji colon kewapụ ha).                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_CONFIG_HOME`                 | _(a tọghị)_                      | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Dochie ndekọ ụlọ maka ịgụ nhazi CLI (`~/.claude`, `~/.codex`). Ọ ga-abụrịrị absolute ma dịkwa n'ime ụlọ nke usoro (process) — **ma ọ bụ**, n'ime container, ụzọ e jiri bind-mount (nke ahụ bụ otu `/host-home` si arụ ọrụ). Ihe ọ bụla ọzọ ga-adachie na ndekọ ụlọ mbụ. |
| `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`         | `true`                           | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Kwe ka OmniRoute dee faịlụ nhazi CLI (mmelite token, data nnọkọ). Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mee ka ide nhazi CLI ọ bụla daa ada na njehie "writes disabled" doro anya.                                                                                               |
| `CLI_CLAUDE_BIN`                  | `claude`                         | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Claude CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CLI_CODEX_BIN`                   | `codex`                          | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`                 | Ụzọ ahaziri n'ụzọ pụrụ iche gaa na binary Codex CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
