### Nhazi-akpaaka CLI Profile (Auto-Sync)

Feature flags ndị a bụ nke opt-in, ma n'ụzọ ndabara ha adịghị arụ ọrụ. Ị nwekwara ike ịgbanwe ha site na CLI Code dashboard.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara | Faili Isi Iyi                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CODEX_PROFILES`  | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe nhazi model provider gasịrị, ọ na-edekwa ọzọ na-akpaghị aka faili profile `~/.codex/*.config.toml` site na catalog dị ndụ. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ gaghị agbanwe config Codex na-arụ ọrụ/ndabara, auth, ntọala Codex-lb, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ provider.              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AUTO_SYNC_CLAUDE_PROFILES` | `false` | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts` | Mgbe nhazi model provider gasịrị, ọ na-edekwa ọzọ na-akpaghị aka faili profile Claude Code `~/.claude/profiles/<name>/settings.json` site na catalog dị ndụ. Ọ chọrọ `CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES`; ọ gaghị agbanwe config Claude na-arụ ọrụ/ndabara, auth, ma ọ bụ nhọrọ provider. |

### Ihe Atụ Docker

```bash
# Tinye binaries nke host n'ime container ma gwara OmniRoute ebe ha dị:
CLI_EXTRA_PATHS=/host-cli/bin
CLI_CONFIG_HOME=/host-home
CLI_ALLOW_CONFIG_WRITES=true
CLI_CLAUDE_BIN=/host-cli/bin/claude
```

`CLI_CONFIG_HOME` na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe e bind-mounted ụzọ ahụ n'ezie site na host — jikọta ya na mounts dị ka `~/.codex:/host-home/.codex:rw` (lee profaịlụ `host` na `docker-compose.yml`). A na-eleghara ụzọ ọ bụla nke adịghị n'ime ụlọ onye ọrụ container ma ọ bụ bụrụ bind mount anya, n'ihi na ihe e dere ebe ahụ ga-atụfu mgbe a mepụtara container ọzọ.

Image ahụ na-agba ọsọ dị ka `USER node`, n'ihi ya `/root` na-enweghị mount **abụghị** override ziri ezi.

| Varyabụl                                 | Ndabara        | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                   | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------- | -------------- | ----------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTAINER`                    | _(akpaaka)_    | `src/shared/utils/containerEnv.ts`  | Mee ka njirimara container rụọ ọrụ (`1`/`true`) ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ ya (`0`/`false`). Ọ dị mkpa naanị na runtimes nke njirimara akpaaka na-eleghara anya.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_CONTAINER_CONFIG_WRITE` | `false`        | `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` | Kwe ka a dee CLI-tool config n'ime ụzọ container na-enweghị mount na agbanyeghị. Ihe hàtara ya na CLI bụ `--allow-container-write`.                                                                                                                          |
| `DOCKER_UPDATE_TRIGGER_FILE`             | enweghị ntọala | `src/lib/system/autoUpdate.ts`      | Naanị Docker image: dashboard **Melite Ugbu a** na-ede arịrịọ JSON na faịlụ a (kwesịrị ịdị n'ime `DATA_DIR`) ka onye nleba anya n'akụkụ host wee pull ma mepụta container ọzọ. Lee [Ntuziaka Docker](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#dashboard-updates-in-docker). |

### Ndị enyemaka CLI Binary (`omniroute`)

Varyabụl ndị a na-ahazi omume nke `omniroute` CLI binary n'onwe ya (ọ bụghị njirimara sidecar dị n'elu).

| Varyabụl                              | Ndabara                           | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------- | --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_LANG`                      | _(sistemụ)_                       | `bin/cli/i18n.mjs`                      | Mee asụsụ mmepụta CLI n'ike. BCP-47 locale (dịka `en`, `pt-BR`). Ọ na-akagbuo system locale env vars (LC_ALL, LC_MESSAGES).                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_SHOW_LOG`                  | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/runtime/processSupervisor.mjs` | Tinye ya na `1` iji ziga server stdout/stderr na terminal na supervised mode. Ihe hàtara ya bụ `--log` flag na `omniroute serve`.                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_TOKEN`                 | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Token machine-auth etinyere dị ka header `x-omniroute-cli-token`. A na-emepụta ya na-akpaaka na task 8.12.                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `GEMINI_CLI_HOME`                     | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/commands/setup-gemini.mjs`     | Gemini home root na-adịgide adị; ọrụ one-shot na-eji ụlọ nwa oge ekewapụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHILD_SIGNAL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `5000`                            | `bin/cli/commands/run.mjs`              | Oge ebere na milliseconds tupu e ji ike gbuo CLI child a nyela signal.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CLI_SKIP_DEFAULT_DATA_ENV` | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Tinye na 1 site na DATA_DIR kpọmkwem iji gafee faịlụ environment nke data-directory onye ọrụ ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYNC_TOKEN`                | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/commands/sync.mjs`             | Sync token maka `omniroute sync bundle` (`GET /api/sync/bundle` na-anabata naanị sync token, ọ bụghị API key). Ihe hàtara ya bụ `--token`.                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_HTTP_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000`                           | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | HTTP timeout kwa mgbalị (ms) maka arịrịọ CLI → server.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_READY_TIMEOUT_MS`          | `60000`                           | `bin/cli/utils/pid.mjs`                 | Oge kacha ogologo (ms) CLI na-echere server health endpoint tupu ọ pịnye ịdọ aka ná ntị banyere timeout. Ọ bara uru maka cold starts na-adị nwayọ (dịka Windows). Enwere ike ịtọkwa ya site na `--ready-timeout`.                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VERBOSE`                   | `0`                               | `bin/cli/api.mjs`                       | Tinye na `1` iji pịnye nyocha retry/backoff na stderr n'oge iwu CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLUGIN_PATH`               | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/cli/plugins.mjs`                   | Directory ahaziri maka ịchọpụta CLI plugin (`omniroute-cmd-*` packages). Ọ na-akwusi na `~/.omniroute/plugins/` mgbe a na-esetịpụghị ya. Naanị CLI — ọ naghị eru server-side plugin scanner, nke `OMNIROUTE_PLUGINS_DIR` na-ezipụta (ngalaba 2).                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER`        | _(enweghị ntọala)_                | `bin/omniroute.mjs`                     | Gbochie CLI update notification na mmemme ọpụpụ mgbe a tọrọ ya na uru ọ bụla na-abụghị efu. Ọdọ aka ná ntị ahụ ka na-agbanyụrụ n'otu n'otu na CI, quiet mode, na non-text output modes.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCTOR_LIVENESS_URL`       | _(ewepụtara)_                     | `bin/cli/commands/doctor.mjs`           | Liveness URL nke `omniroute doctor` na-enyocha. Nhọrọ `livenessUrl` nke mmemme na-edu ụzọ; ọ bụghị ya, a na-eji uru a dị ka ọ dị. Mgbe a na-esetịpụghị ya, CLI na-ewepụta endpoint `/api/health/degradation` nke dashboard mpaghara.                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_CATALOG_PATH`     | `src/shared/constants/providers/` | `bin/cli/provider-catalog.mjs`          | Ụzọ provider catalog nke iwu CLI provider na-eji. Nhọrọ `catalogPath` nke mmemme na-edu ụzọ. A na-eji ụzọ zuru oke (absolute) dị ka ha dị; ụzọ metụtara (relative) na-akpebi site na project root. Mgbe a na-esetịpụghị ya, CLI na-eji provider constants directory, na faịlụ ochie `providers.ts` dị ka fallback. |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_TEST_MODEL`       | _(ndabara provider)_              | `bin/cli/provider-test.mjs`             | Model fallback zuru ụwa maka ule njikọ CLI provider. Usoro ọganihu bụ model njikọ ahaziri, model override kpọmkwem nye provider, uru a, mgbe ahụ model ule arụnyere n'ime provider.                                                                                                                                |

---

## 10. Agent Ime Ụlọ & Mmekọrịta MCP

| Variable                                        | Default                                             | Source File                                                 | Description                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_BASE_URL`                            | auto-detect                                         | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | URL e kwupụtara nke ngwá ọrụ MCP/A2A ga-eji rụe OmniRoute. Ọ na-ekpochapụ nchọpụta localhost akpaghị aka.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`                             | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | modul MCP/A2A                                               | Igodo API maka oku ngwá ọrụ MCP ime ụlọ na oku nkà A2A.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_ID`                          | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | `open-sse/mcp-server/audit.ts`                              | ID igodo maka nkenye ndekọ nyocha MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ROUTER_API_KEY`                                | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | Ochie                                                       | Aha ọzọ ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_HISTORY_RETENTION_DAYS`          | `30`                                                | `src/lib/a2a/taskManager.ts`                                | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ụbọchị akụkọ ọrụ A2A ka echekwara n'ọdụ data mpaghara tupu nhicha kwa ụbọchị ehichapụ ahịrị. Ọ bụrụ na anahị edobe ya, ọ bụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ, ma ọ bụ `<= 0`, ọ na-alaghachi na `30`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_A2A_MEMORY_HITS`                     | `1`                                                 | `src/lib/a2a/taskExecution.ts`                              | Kill-switch maka atụmatụ nleba anya nke memory-hits A2A. Tọọ ya na `0` iji gafee nchọpụta nlọghachi ncheta maka otu ọrụ kpamkpam; uru ọ bụla ọzọ (gụnyere ịghị edobe ya) na-eme ka ọ nọgide na-arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_ENABLED`                 | `false`                                             | `src/app/api/issue-agent/runs/route.ts`                     | Na-arụ ọrụ njedebe recorded-triage nke Issue Agent offline/mpaghara. Hapụ ka ọ ghara ịrụ ọrụ belụsọ na ị na-agba ọsọ usoro ọrụ recorded-triage mpaghara n'ụzọ doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ISSUE_AGENT_TIMEOUT_MS`              | _(anahị edobe)_                                     | `src/lib/issueAgent/execution.ts`                           | Oge njedebe (ms) maka otu ọsọ recorded-triage nke Issue Agent. E jigidere ya na oke kacha elu ime ụlọ; ọ na-alaghachi na ọdabara e wuru n'ime mgbe anahị edobe ya ma ọ bụ mgbe ọ bụghị nke bara uru.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT`                             | _(ọnọdụ na-arụ ọrụ)_                                | `bin/cli/program.mjs`, `bin/cli/api.mjs`                    | Ọnọdụ/profaịlụ CLI remote-mode maka iwu `omniroute`; ọ na-ekpochapụ ọrụ ọrụ na-arụ ọrụ n'ụlọ echekwara contexts mpaghara. Ọ bụ ihe yiri `--context <name>`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTEXT_KEYCHAIN_DISABLED`           | `0`                                                 | `bin/cli/contexts.mjs`                                      | Na-agbanyụ backend OS-keychain `keytar` nhọrọ maka nzere ọnọdụ CLI. Mgbe a rụrụ ya ọrụ, nzere na-anọgide na `config.json` mode `0600` ma CLI na-ewepụta ịdọ aka ná ntị fallback otu ugboro naanị; e buro ya maka ọrụ headless/container e bu n'uche.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_ENFORCE_SCOPES`                  | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Na-eme ka njikwa ohere dabere na scope rụọ ọrụ na oku ngwá ọrụ MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_SCOPES`                          | _(niile)_                                           | `open-sse/mcp-server/server.ts`                             | Scopes kewapụrụ na kọma: `admin`, `combos`, `health`, `models`, `routing`, `budget`, `metrics`, `pricing`, `memory`, `skills`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_COMPRESS_DESCRIPTIONS`           | `false`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Na-mekaa nkọwa ngwá ọrụ MCP ka ọ dị mkpa tupu a mee serialization nke manifest. Uru ịrụ ọrụ: `1`, `true`, `on`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_DESCRIPTION_COMPRESSION`         | `rtk`                                               | `open-sse/mcp-server/descriptionCompressor.ts`              | Algorithm/profaịlụ mmechi. Uru ịgbanyụ: `0`, `false`, `off`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                | `10000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke ịkwụsị (ms) maka ịgụ njikwa ime ụlọ nke MCP-server (health, resilience, combos, quota, usage).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_MCP_UPSTREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`             | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/mcp-server/fetchTimeout.ts`                       | Oke oge ịkwụsị (ms) maka MCP hops na-echere provider (`route_request`, `web_search`, `web_fetch`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE`                   | `5`                                                 | `src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`                         | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke local-corpus index instances echekwara na nchekwa (LRU, otu maka root directory e tinyere na index ọ bụla). A machiri ya ka ọ dịkarịa ala `1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MODEL_SYNC_INTERVAL_HOURS`                     | `6`                                                 | `src/shared/services/modelSyncScheduler.ts`                 | Oge etiti mmekọrịta model catalog na elekere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_INTERVAL_MINUTES`         | `70`                                                | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oge etiti nyocha rate-limit na quota nke provider.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_SYNC_SPACING_MS`               | `1500`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Oghere (ms) n'etiti ịweta OAuth quota na-esote n'ime bulk sync; a na-eweta njikọ OAuth otu n'otu iji zere ịjụba upstream. `0` na-apụta na nhọrọ a (concurrent).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS`         | `250`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oge etiti kacha nta (ms) n'etiti ịweta upstream quota na-esote n'ụzọ preflight/monitor nke arịrịọ ọ bụla; na-akesa oku netwọk na-agba ọsọ ka ọtụtụ akaụntụ n'otu IP ghara ịjụba upstream. Ejikọrọ ya n'ime ndị na-eweta quota nke Codex (`/wham/usage`), DeepSeek, Bailian (saịtị ịweta abụọ ahụ), OpenCode, na Crof (#6009, #6911). A ka ekpuchịbeghị ụzọ nzaghachi izugbe nke `usage.ts::getUsageForProvider` (github/glm/minimax/nanogpt/xai/etc.) — a na-eso ya iche. Ndakọ cache adịghị emetụta. `0` na-agbanyụọ; a machiri `0..60000`. |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_JITTER_MS`               | `120`                                               | `open-sse/services/quotaFetchThrottle.ts`                   | Oghere random mgbakwunyere (ms) maka arịrịọ ọ bụla, a na-atụle ya na ịweta upstream quota e debere ọ bụla. Ndabara bụ 120; a machiri `0..60000`; `0` na-ewepụ naanị jitter. Tọọ `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_FETCH_MIN_INTERVAL_MS=0` iji gbanyụọ throttle ahụ niile. Jitter anaghị ekwe nkwa mgbochi mmachibido upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `PROVIDER_LIMITS_POST_USAGE_REFRESH_DELAY_MS`   | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/usage/providerLimits.ts`                           | Nkwụsị (ms) tupu ịmegharị oke provider mgbe mmemme ojiji n'ezie gasịrị, na-enye upstream quota API oge ịdekọ oriri.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | nchọpụta akpaghị aka                                | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`             | Ụzọ zuru oke na Chrome ma ọ bụ Edge executable nke sistemụ e ji eme nbanye Adobe Firefly na-arụkọ ọrụ na mmelite dị n'èzí ihuenyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`                 | arụnyere                                            | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Na-edebe IMS na ọnọdụ ihe egwu browser ọhụrụ site na Chrome CDP sessions nke oke akaụntụ. Tọọ ya na `0` iji gbanyụọ mmelite browser.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |

Tọọ `0` iji debe sessions na nchekwa naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`              | `15000`                                             | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                  | Oge izu ike mgbakwunyere na milliseconds mgbe ntinye Adobe nke atọ ọ bụla gara nke ọma.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`            | `8000`                                              | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`                   | Nkwụsị azụ isi na milliseconds mgbe nzaghachi Adobe 408 nke adịgide adịgide; ejikọrọ ya na oghere ntinye n'ofe mbala ise kacha elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES`         | `false`                                             | `src/instrumentation-node.ts`                               | Gbanyụọ ọrụ ndabere niile (sync, ọnụ ahịa, mmelite model). Ọ bar uru maka CI/test.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEADLESS`                            | _(a tọghị)_                                         | `src/lib/system/headless.ts`                                | Mmalite nke proxy-engine naanị: na-agafe subsystem ndị a na-achọghị, `/dashboard` → 404. Oke ala `10000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

Oke ala `5000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_CONNECTION_RECOVERY`         | `false`                                             | `src/lib/quota/connectionRecovery.ts`                       | Gbanyụọ onye nhazi mweghari oge jụrụ oyi njikọ n'ihu (mweghari na-egbu oge na `getProviderCredentials` ka na-arụ ọrụ).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_CACHE_CLEANUP_INTERVAL_MS` | `1800000`                                           | `src/lib/jobs/reasoningCacheCleanupJob.ts`                  | Ugboro nhicha cache echiche (ms). Oke ala `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_MIN_BUDGET`                | _adịghị atọ_ (gbanyụrụ)                             | `open-sse/services/reasoningTokenBuffer.ts`                 | Oke ala nhọrọ maka mmefu mmepụta ụdị ịche echiche: `max_tokens` onye na-akpọ dị na `[256, floor)` na-ebili ruo n'oke ala (oke mmepụta ụdị ahụ na-achịkwa ya). Adịghị atọ = mmefu onye ahịa adịghị ebuli (#9507).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_REASONING_EFFORT_PROBE_PROVIDERS`    | _adịghị atọ_ (gbanyụrụ)                             | `open-sse/services/learnedReasoningEffortCaps.ts`           | Nhọrọ, njirimara ndị na-enye ekwere site na kọma (ma ọ bụ `*`): mgbe `reasoning_effort` 400/422 na-akpọghị otu anabatara, gbalịa ọzọ otu ugboro n'ọkwa dị otu n'ala (anaghị agbada n'okpuru `low`) ma mụta ọkwa ahụ naanị ọ bụrụ na azaa nyocha ahụ (#14895).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CRON`                     | `0 * * * *`                                         | `src/lib/jobs/logExportJob.ts`                              | Nkwupụta Cron (UTC) maka ọrụ mbupụ ndekọ oku nke na-agbapụ ebe mbupụ ndekọ ọ bụla arụnyere ọrụ. Họrọ `*/5 * * * *` mgbe na-ebupụ ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_RUN_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `900000`                                            | `src/lib/logExport/childRunner.ts`                          | Oge elekere mgbidi nwa maka ebe ọ bụla na milliseconds. Oge na-edekọ ihe ọda ma gbuo usoro ahụ na-enweghị ibu batch a kwadobeeghị n'ihu. Uru adịghị mma/ndị na-adịghị dị mma/ndị na-abụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ na-eji ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_EXPORT_CHILD_MAX_OLD_SPACE_MB`   | `1024`                                              | `src/lib/logExport/childRunner.ts`                          | Oke old-space V8 nwa mbupụ Node na MB (ọ bụghị mkpokọta RSS). Ndabara na-ahapụ ohere maka ahịrị 500 nke ihe e ji okpu akpuchie nke nwere ahụ; batch buru ibu nwere ike ịme ka heap nwa gwụcha. Ọdịda na-atụ aro ibelata nha batch ma ọ bụ ịbuli oke a. Uru adịghị mma/ndị na-adịghị dị mma/ndị na-abụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ na-eji ndabara. Mbupụ Bun na-alaghachi n'ime usoro na-enweghị oke a.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONFIG_HOT_RELOAD_MS`                | `5000`                                              | `src/lib/config/hotReload.ts`                               | Oge ntuli (ms) maka ibugharị ọkụ nhazi. Nke dị ala karịa `1000` na-ajụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_REDIS_AUTH_CACHE`            | _(arụnyere)_                                        | `src/lib/db/apiKeys.ts`                                     | Tọọ `1` iji gafere cache nyocha API-key nke Redis na-akwado (na-amanye ịgụ DB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS`           | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/rtk/filterLoader.ts` | Tụkwasị iwu nzacha ọrụ RTK onye ọrụ na-elekọta na-enweghị nyocha mbinye aka siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_LITE_MAX_TOOL_LENGTH`                | `2000`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/lite.ts`                     | Oke mkpụrụedemede maka mbelata nsonaazụ ngwá ọrụ Lite n'ihu mgbe `lite.maxToolLength` adịghị atọ. Oke 256–1000000. Nhazi Dashboard na-emeri env a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKERS`                      | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ndị ọrụ RTK/Caveman synchronous na-arụkọ ọrụ; ọrụ ndị karịrị na-echere FIFO.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                            | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Oge ọrụ ọ bụla na milliseconds. Ndị ọrụ oge ha agwara na-akwụsị ma arịrịọ ahụ na-adaghị emeghị mgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_IDLE_MS`               | `60000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Oge ndụ na-arụghị ọrụ na milliseconds tupu e kwụsị onye ọrụ mgbakọ a na-ejighị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNI_COMPRESSION_WORKER_MAX_OLD_MB`            | `1024`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/compressionWorkerPool.ts`    | Oke heap ọgbọ ochie (old-generation) nke V8 na MB maka onye ọrụ mkpakọ ọ bụla, n'ọnọdụ niile (kacha n'ala `128`). Job gafere ya na-ada emeghe (enweghị mkpakọ) kama ịme ka heap nke gateway too ụba.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_ENABLED`          | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ịgbanwe isi (master switch) nke circuit-breaker per-engine maka T02 stacked-pipeline. **Ịhọrọ ya (emechiri na ndabere)** — mgbe ọ bụrụ na ọ na-arụ ọrụ, engine na-atụfu njehie ugboro ugboro n'ofe arịrị arịrị ka a ga-awụlikwa (fail-open) ruo oge cooldown; mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ = omume ochie byte-identical.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_THRESHOLD`        | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ọdịda nke sotara n'usoro n'ofe arịrị arịrị tupu breaker nke engine emeghe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_PIPELINE_BREAKER_COOLDOWN_MS`      | `30000`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/pipelineEngineBreaker.ts`    | Ọnụ ọgụgụ milliseconds engine emegbere na-anọgide na a wụlikara ya tupu a gbaa nyocha half-open.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_RETRIEVAL_RAMP_FACTOR`         | `2`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ramp nzaghachi retrieval nke T08/H8 CCR: retrieval ọ bụla gara aga nke block echekwara na-ebuli `minChars` ya dị irè n'ụzọ linear (ọdịnaya a na-arụpụtakazi na-ebelata ka a na-akpakọ ya; retrieval `>=3` = agaghị akpakọ ya ọzọ). `1` na-agbanyụ ramp ahụ (binary skip na oke ahụ naanị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_CCR_DURABLE_STORE`                 | `true`                                              | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/ccr/index.ts`        | Ọba nchekwa block CCR na-adigide (#9061). Ọ na-akwado nchekwa in-memory site na SQLite ka block wee lanarị LRU eviction, TTL, mmalite ọzọ, ma ọ bụ retrieve rutere na instance ọzọ. Tọọ `false` iji debe blocks na ncheta naanị. Blocks karịrị 512KB na cloud runtimes na-anọgide na ncheta naanị n'agbanyeghị ihe ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_ENABLED`             | `false`                                             | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Ịgbanwe isi nke prefix freeze usage-observed T08/H5. **Ịhọrọ ya (emechiri na ndabere)** — mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ, system prompt a hụrụ `>=` oke ahụ ka a na-ewere dị ka prefix kwụsie ike nke a nwere ike itinye na cache ma chekwaa ya ka mkpakọ ghara ịmetụ ya, ọbụna maka ndị na-e nyefere nke static cache heuristic na-agaghị (freeze _na-echekwa_ naanị, ọ naghị agbanwe ihe ọ bụla).                                                                                                                                                       |
| `COMPRESSION_PREFIX_FREEZE_THRESHOLD`           | `3`                                                 | `open-sse/services/compression/prefixFreeze.ts`             | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ọhụrụ nke system prompt tupu a were ya dị ka prefix kwụsie ike e sịchiri (frozen).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_BOOTSTRAPPED`                        | `false`                                             | `src/app/(dashboard)/dashboard/page.tsx`                    | Script bootstrap na-atọ ya `true` mgbe ntọala mbụ gasịrị. Ọ na-achịkwa ka a na-ahụ setup wizard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_ALLOW_BODY_PROJECT_OVERRIDE`         | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/executors/antigravity.ts`                         | Ụzọ mgbapụ (escape hatch): kwe ka body arịrị jiri nke ya dochie ubi project Antigravity.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_CREDITS`                           | `off`                                               | `open-sse/services/antigravityCredits.ts`                   | Iwu kredit Google One AI: `off` anaghị etinye kredit ọ bụla, `retry` na-etinye otu ugboro mgbe quota 429 ruru eru gasịrị, `always` na-etinye n'arịrị mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_ALLOW_SIGNATURE_BYPASS`            | `0`                                                 | `open-sse/translator/request/openai-to-gemini.ts`           | Kwe ka onye ntụgharị arịrị Antigravity wụlikwa nkwenye signature arịrị CLI siri ike mgbe upstream jụrụ signatures n'ezie (ọnọdụ debug/antiquated-CLI). Ọnụ ọgụgụ na-abụghị ezero na-agbanye bypass ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `AGY_TOKEN_FILE`                                | `~/.gemini/antigravity-cli/antigravity-oauth-token` | `src/app/api/providers/agy-auth/apply-local/route.ts`       | Jiri nke a dochie ụzọ faịlụ token nke Antigravity CLI (agy) maka mbubata nbanye mpaghara auto-detect.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

### Njikọ OAuth CLI (N'ime)

| Ọgbanwe             | Ihe ndabere             | Faịlụ isi mmalite               | Nkọwa                                             |
| ------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`  | nchọpụta na-akpaghị aka | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | URL sava maka njikọ nyocha CLI↔OmniRoute.         |
| `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`   | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Token nyocha maka njikọ CLI.                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID` | `cli`                   | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | ID onye ọrụ maka nnọkọ njikọ CLI.                 |
| `SERVER_URL`        | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ochie (legacy alias) maka `OMNIROUTE_SERVER`. |
| `CLI_TOKEN`         | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_TOKEN`.                 |
| `CLI_USER_ID`       | _(a tọghị)_             | `src/lib/oauth/config/index.ts` | Aha ochie maka `OMNIROUTE_USER_ID`.               |
