## 11. Ihe Nnweta Ndị Na-enye OAuth

Ihe nnweta e wuru n'ime maka **mmepe localhost**. Maka ndị arụnyere n'ebe dị anya, debanye nke gị na console onye mmepe nke onye na-enye ọ bụla.

| Mgbanwe                                     | Onye na-enye            | Ihe edeturu                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Claude Code (Anthropic) | Onye ahịa ọha — achọghị ihe nzuzo ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_REDIRECT_URI`                  | Claude Code             | Kagbuo URI ntụgharị. Nke dabara: `https://platform.claude.com/oauth/code/callback`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CODEX_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Codex / OpenAI          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | Gemini (Google)         | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `GEMINI_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | Gemini (Google)         | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `KIMI_CODING_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Kimi Coding (Moonshot)  | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `MUSE_CODE_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                 | Muse Code (Meta)        | Nhọrọ kagbuo maka ID onye ahịa device-flow ọha nke Muse CLI. Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịdị iji jiri onye ahịa ọha e tinyere n'ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | Antigravity (Google)    | Chọrọ `_SECRET` kwekọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`           | Antigravity (Google)    | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Onye ahịa ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GHE_COPILOT_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`               | GHE Copilot             | Nhọrọ kagbuo maka ID onye ahịa OAuth nke GitHub Enterprise Copilot. Ọ na-alaghachi na nke dabara ọha nke `GITHUB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` mgbe a na-etoghị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `COPILOT_INTEGRATION_ID`                    | GitHub Copilot          | Nhọrọ kagbuo maka ID njikọ onye ahịa GitHub Copilot a na-eziga na isiokwu `Copilot-Integration-Id` na `Editor-Plugin-Version`. Ọ na-adabara na `copilot-developer-cli`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `WINDSURF_API_KEY`                          | Windsurf / Devin (v3.8) | Igodo API ndabere nke `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-eji mgbe ihe nnweta nke njikọ ọ bụla adịghị. Ọ bụ nhọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                             | Devin CLI (v3.8)        | Ụzọ ahaziri gaa na binary Devin CLI (`devin`). `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts` na-edozi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | ID onye ahịa OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Debanye aha ngwa na `https://gitlab.com/-/profile/applications` site na URI ntụgharị `<NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL>/callback` na scopes `api, read_user, openid, profile, email`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`            | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ihe nzuzo onye ahịa OAuth maka GitLab Duo. Ọ bụ nhọrọ — usoro PKCE achọghị ihe nzuzo. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`                       | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Kagbuo URL ntọala GitLab (GitLab nke onwe na-akwado). Ọ na-adabara na `https://gitlab.com`. Ọ na-alaghachi na `GITLAB_BASE_URL`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `GITLAB_BASE_URL`                           | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ihe nnọchi azụ ọdịnala maka `GITLAB_DUO_BASE_URL`. A na-eji ya mgbe a na-edobeghị ụdị `_DUO_`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                    | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ihe nnọchi azụ ọdịnala maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `GITLAB_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                | GitLab Duo (v3.8)       | Ihe nnọchi azụ ọdịnala maka `GITLAB_DUO_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET` nke `src/lib/oauth/constants/oauth.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                 | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_AUTHORIZE_URL`                 | Qoder                   | Dobee ya iji mee ka Qoder OAuth rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `QODER_OAUTH_TOKEN_URL`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_USERINFO_URL`                  | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID`                     | Qoder                   | —                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `QODER_PERSONAL_ACCESS_TOKEN`               | Qoder                   | Ihe nnọchi azụ igodo API ozugbo (na-agafee OAuth).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`                       | Qoder                   | ID ebe ọrụ maka Qoder CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_QODER_WORKSPACE`                 | Qoder                   | Aha ọzọ maka `QODER_CLI_WORKSPACE`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `QODER_CLI_CONFIG_DIR`                      | Qoder                   | Kwapụọ folda nhazi Qoder CLI (nnọkọ PAT dị iche, na-ezere imebi nbanye ihe nchọgharị).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_CLI_MAX_CONCURRENCY`                 | Qoder                   | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ọrụ qodercli na-agba ọsọ otu oge (mmalite print ma ọ bụ ajụjụ ACP); arịrị ndị ọzọ na-echere n'ahịrị FIFO. Mgbe a na-edobeghị ya (ndabara) = enweghị oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QODER_CLI_QUEUE_TIMEOUT_MS`                | Qoder                   | Ogologo oge arịrị Qoder na-echere ohere qodercli (naanị mgbe edobere oke) tupu ọ daa ada na 429 `qodercli_busy` + `Retry-After`. Ndabara `120000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `QODER_CLI_TRANSPORT`                       | Qoder                   | `print` (ndabara) na-amalite `qodercli --print` maka arịrị ọ bụla; `acp` na-edebe otu usoro `qodercli --acp` na-adịgide adịgide maka PAT ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`             | Qoder                   | Ngwaga ACP: gbuo usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe oge ihe ọnụ dị otu a gachara. Ndabara `600000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_STARTUP_TIMEOUT_MS`          | Qoder                   | Ngwaga ACP: oge ikpeazụ maka qodercli ịza ajụjụ `initialize` nke ACP. Ndabara `60000`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `QODER_CLI_ACP_MAX_SESSIONS_PER_PROCESS`    | Qoder                   | Ngwaga ACP: megharịa usoro qodercli na-adịgide adịgide mgbe ọtụtụ nnọkọ dị otu a gachara. Ndabara `500`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BLACKBOX_WEB_VALIDATED_TOKEN`              | Blackbox Web            | Token `tk` nke frontend iji ziga dị ka `validated` na `/api/chat`. Achọrọ ya mgbe Blackbox na-amanye ịdabara token; ma ọ bụghị, OmniRoute na-atụgharị na UUID enweghị usoro. Lee nsogbu #2252.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_BASE_URL`                    | Nchekwa Vision Bridge   | URL ntọala kwadoro OpenAI maka oku vision-bridge na-abụghị nke Anthropic. Ọ na-adabere na env URL OpenAI ochie ma ọ bụ api.openai.com. Kụzie ya na `/v1` self-loop nke OmniRoute ma ọ bụ endpoint OpenAI-compat ọ bụla (Gemini OpenAI-compat, OpenRouter). Nsogbu #2232. Mgbe URL ahụ bụ `/v1` nke OmniRoute n'onwe ya, sub-request describe na-ezipu `x-omniroute-admission-bypass: internal` ma jiri nzere self-loop e kpebiri nyocha onwe ya (`sk_omniroute` sentinel na ọnọdụ mpaghara, ma ọ bụ `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY` / `ROUTER_API_KEY` — #1350) ka ntinye `REQUIRE_API_KEY=true` wee rụọ ọrụ. |
| `VISION_BRIDGE_API_KEY`                     | Nchekwa Vision Bridge   | Igodo API maka URL dị n'elu. Ọ na-ekpochapụ OpenAI / Google env vars kwa-onye na-ebubara maka oku vision-bridge na-abụghị nke Anthropic. Ụdị Anthropic na-edebe ụzọ igodo Anthropic pụrụ iche ha. Nsogbu #2232.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_VISION_BRIDGE_NEGATIVE_CACHE_MS` | Nchekwa Vision Bridge   | Ogologo oge a na-echekwa nsonaazụ nhazi "enweghị onye nhọrọ a ga-eji", na ms (ndabara `30000`). Ọnụọgụ ndị adịghị irè ma ọ bụ ndị ọjọọ na-adabereghachi na ndabara; `0` na-egbanyụọ cache ọjọọ ahụ. Isi: `src/lib/guardrails/visionBridgeRouter.ts`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

> [!WARNING]
>
> 1. Gaa na [Google Cloud Console → Credentials](https://console.cloud.google.com/apis/credentials)
> 2. Mepụta OAuth 2.0 Client ID (ụdị: "Web application")
> 3. Tinye URL nkesa gị dị ka Authorized redirect URI
> 4. Dochie ọnụọgụ nzere ndị ahụ na `.env`.

## 12. Nkwakọ User-Agent nke Onye Na-enye

Kwakọ `User-Agent` header a na-eziga onye na-enye upstream ọ bụla. Executor base class na-edozi nke a n'ụzọ dynamic n'oge ọrụ (runtime):

```
process.env[`${PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`]
```

> **Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/executors/base.ts` → `buildHeaders()`

| Mgbanwe | Uru ndabere | Oge ị ga-emelite ya |
| -------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT` | `claude-cli/2.1.280 (external, cli)` | Mgbe Anthropic wepụtara ụdị CLI ọhụrụ |
| `CLAUDE_DISABLE_TOOL_NAME_CLOAK` | `false` | `executors/base.ts` + `executors/cliproxyapi.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1`/`true` iji zipu aha ngwá ọrụ nke third-party harness ozugbo (verbatim) ka ọ gaa na Anthropic n'ụzọ abụọ ahụ na-eduga n'Anthropic (native OAuth na CLIProxyAPI). N'ọnọdụ ndabere, executor na-enye aha ngwá ọrụ ndị ọzọ abụghị nke Claude Code aha ọzọ (alias) n'ụzọ a na-achọpụta — na-eji Claude Code canonical mapping ebe ọ dị, ma ọ bụghị PascalCase — ma tụgharịa ha n'azịza ahụ site na `_toolNameMap`, ka harness ndị nwere ngwá ọrụ snake_case ghara ịjụ dị ka ndị ahịa ndị ọzọ a chọpụtara site na fingerprint. Maka idozi mmejọ naanị. |
| `CODEX_USER_AGENT` | `codex-cli/0.155.0 (Windows 10.0.26200; x64)` | Mgbe OpenAI melitere Codex CLI |
| `CODEX_CLIENT_VERSION` | `0.155.0` | Kwakọ ụdị client Codex n'otu nke ya, n'enweghị isi na UA string zuru oke |
| `GROK_CLI_CLIENT_VERSION` | `1.0.41` | Kwakọ `x-grok-client-version` maka inference, mmekọ ụdị (model sync), na device-code OAuth, tinyere Grok CLI User-Agent ndabere maka inference na model sync. Ọ na-anabata SemVer nwere metadata prerelease/build nke ọ bụ nhọrọ, ruru mkpụrụedemede 128 mgbe a wépụrụ oghere (whitespace) gbara ya gburugburu. Ọnụ ọgụgụ ndị a na-atọghị, ndị efu, ndị ezighi ezi, ma ọ bụ ndị nwere line-break na-eji pin e zitere. Nkwakọ User-Agent inference zuru oke dị adị ka o na-ewere ọchịchị mbụ (precedence). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` | `2.1.280` | Kwakọ ụdị Claude Code e kwupụtara n'otu nke ya, n'enweghị isi na `CLAUDE_USER_AGENT`. Anthropic na-akpachi ụfọdụ ụdị (models) n'ọnụ ọgụgụ a (#12417). |
| `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_BUILD_REVISION` | `1e2` | Kwakọ mgbakwụnye mkpụrụedemede atọ (3-character suffix) OmniRoute na-agbakwụnye na `cc_version=` n'ime Claude billing block. Bulie ya n'otu oge na `CLAUDE_CODE_CLIENT_VERSION` — ịgbatị naanị ụdị ahụ na-ekwupụta otu `version.revision` nke ọ bụghị ezigbo binary na-ezipụta. |
| `GITHUB_COPILOT_CLI_VERSION` | `1.0.91` | Kwakọ ụdị Copilot CLI e kwupụtara n'otu nke ya, n'enweghị isi na `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` |
| `GITHUB_USER_AGENT` | `GitHubCopilotChat/0.54.0` | Mgbe GitHub Copilot Chat melitere |
| `ANTIGRAVITY_USER_AGENT` | `antigravity/2.0.1 darwin/arm64` | Mgbe Antigravity IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_USER_AGENT` | `AWS-SDK-JS/3.0.0 kiro-ide/1.0.0` | Mgbe Kiro IDE melitere |
| `KIRO_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` | `kiro-cli` | Kwakọ `clientId` nke Kiro social device-code (id ọha) |
| `KIRO_VERIFY_FULL_CRC` | `false` | Nhọrọ ịbanye (opt-in): nkwenye CRC zuru oke nke ozi ọ bụla n'ọkwa frame n'Kiro event stream (maka ịchọpụta ọwa ndị mebiri emebi) |
| `KIRO_CREDIT_PRICE_USD` | `0.02` | USD maka kredit Kiro ọ bụla; ọ na-agbanwe kredit `meteringEvent` ka ọ bụrụ ọnụ ahịa ziri ezi nke arịrị ọ bụla |
| `KIRO_REQUIRE_CREDITS` | `false` | Mee ka azịza Kiro na-ekwupụta na ọ nweghị nhazi kredit (credit metering) daa ụda, kama ịdekọ ya ka ọ bụ nke a na-adaghị ọnụ (unbilled) |
| `QODER_USER_AGENT` | `Qoder-Cli` | Mgbe Qoder CLI melitere |
| `CURSOR_USER_AGENT` | `Cursor/3.3` | Mgbe Cursor melitere |

> [!TIP]
> Ị nwere ike ịgbakwụnye nkwakọ User-Agent maka **ọ bụla** onye na-enye site na iji ụkpụrụ `{PROVIDER_ID}_USER_AGENT`. Executor na-ewulite aha mgbanwe gburugburu (env var) n'ụzọ dynamic.

---

## 13. Ndakọrịta Akara-Mkpịsịaka CLI

Mgbe agbanyere ya, OmniRoute na-ahazigharị isiokwu HTTP na mpaghara ahụ JSON ka ha kwekọọ kpọmkwem na akara njirimara nke ngwa CLI gọọmentị. Nke a na-ebelata ihe ize ndụ nke ịka akaụntụ gị akara ma na-echekwa IP proxy gị.

**Isi mmalite:** `open-sse/config/cliFingerprints.ts`, `open-sse/executors/base.ts`

### Maka Onye Na-enye Ọrụ Ọ Bụla

| Mgbanwe                  | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                              |
| ------------------------ | -------- | ------------------------------------ |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CODEX`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Codex CLI      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLAUDE`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Claude Code    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_GITHUB`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ GitHub Copilot |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ANTIGRAVITY` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Antigravity    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CURSOR`      | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cursor         |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIMI_CODING` | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kimi Coding    |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KILOCODE`    | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Kilo Code      |
| `CLI_COMPAT_CLINE`       | `=1`     | Na-eṅomi akara arịrịọ Cline          |

### Nke Izugbe

| Mgbanwe          | Mgbalite | Mmetụta                                                                 |
| ---------------- | -------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CLI_COMPAT_ALL` | `=1`     | Gbaa ndakọrịta akara-mkpịsịaka maka ndị na-enye ọrụ **niile** otu mgbe. |

### Mgbanwe njirimara Kimi Coding CLI

| Mgbanwe                 | Nke ndabara          | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                        | Nkọwa                                                            |
| ----------------------- | -------------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KIMI_CLI_VERSION`      | `1.36.0`             | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ụdị Kimi CLI a na-eziga n'oge oku OAuth/API.              |
| `KIMI_CODING_DEVICE_ID` | _(ndabara e jidere)_ | `src/lib/oauth/providers/kimi-coding.ts` | Dochie ID ngwaọrụ Kimi e jidere nke a na-eji n'isiokwu klayenti. |

> [!NOTE]
> Njirimara a na-arụkọ ọrụ na mgbanwe User-Agent (§12). Sistemụ akara-mkpịsịaka na-ahụ maka nhazi isiokwu na nhazi mpaghara ahụ, ebe mgbanwe User-Agent na-ahụ maka eriri UA akọwapụtara. Enwere ike ịgbanye ha abụọ n'adabereghị onwe ha.

---

## 14. Ndị Na-enye Ọrụ Igodo API

Igodo API maka ndị na-enye ọrụ na-eji nyocha njirimara ozugbo. **Nhazi akwadoro:** Dashboard → Providers → Add API Key.

Ịtọ ha site na mgbanwe gburugburu bụ ụzọ ọzọ maka mbugharị Docker ma ọ bụ mbugharị na-enweghị ihu njikwa.

Ụkpụrụ a na-amata: `{PROVIDER_ID}_API_KEY`

| Mgbanwe            | Onye na-enye ọrụ                                     |
| ------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------- |
| `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` | DeepSeek                                             |
| `NVIDIA_API_KEY`   | NVIDIA NIM                                           |
| `JINA_AI_API_KEY`  | Jina AI (Foundation API + Reader ndabere)            |
| `JINA_API_KEY`     | Jina AI (aha ọzọ maka `JINA_AI_API_KEY`)             |
| `GEMINI_API_KEY`   | Gemini (Google AI Studio) embeddings + ndabere nkata |
| `GOOGLE_API_KEY`   | Gemini (aha ọzọ maka `GEMINI_API_KEY`)               |

> [!NOTE]
> E wepụrụ ndenye `${PROVIDER}_API_KEY` kwụ ọtọ maka Groq, xAI, Mistral, Perplexity, Together AI, Fireworks, Cerebras, Cohere, Nebius, na Qianfan na v3.8.0 n'ihi na runtime anaghịzi agụ ha — ndị na-enye ọrụ ahụ na-adabere naanị na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. Hụ ngalaba _Nnyocha: Mgbanwe E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghị Arụ Ọrụ_ dị na njedebe akwụkwọ a maka ụzọ mbugharị.

> [!TIP]
> A na-echekwa igodo ndị edobere site na Dashboard n'ụdị ezoro ezo na SQLite, ha na-ebutekwa mgbanwe gburugburu ụzọ.
>
> **Jina:** embeddings `jina-ai/…`, rerank, classify, segment, na `jina-search` anaghị akwụ ụgwọ igodo env klọsta mgbe njikọ dashboard `jina-ai` (ma ọ bụ `jina-reader` a na-ekekọrịta) dị — `getProviderCredentials` na-ebu ụzọ mejupụta. A na-eji `JINA_AI_API_KEY` / `JINA_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:JINA_AI_API_KEY`. Kaadị Reader (`jina-reader`, `r.jina.ai`) anaghị enye `/v1/embeddings` ma ọ bụ `/v1/rerank`.
>
> **Gemini:** `gemini/gemini-embedding-2` (aha ọzọ `google/gemini-embedding-2`) na-ebu ụzọ jiri njikọ dashboard `gemini`. A na-eji `GEMINI_API_KEY` / `GOOGLE_API_KEY` naanị mgbe enweghị igodo dashboard a pụrụ iji. Ndekọ oku na-egosi ndabere env dịka `connection_id=env:GEMINI_API_KEY`. Okporo data multimodal nke izizi na-eji `x-goog-api-key` megide `:embedContent` / `:batchEmbedContents` — ihe N OpenAI `input` na-aghọ vector N.

---

## 15. Ntọala Timeout

Ọnụ ọgụgụ niile dị na **milliseconds**. Mkpebi etiti dị na `src/shared/utils/runtimeTimeouts.ts`.

### Nkwusiorite Timeout

```
REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (nnọchi izugbe)
├─→ FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS (oku ndị na-enye ọrụ upstream, ndabara: 600000)
│   ├─→ FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   ├─→ TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS)
│   │   └── TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 10000)
│   ├── RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 15000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 0 = gbanyụrụ, a tụrụ aro: 30000)
│   ├── OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS (onwe ya, ndabara: 2)
│   ├── FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 30000)
│   └── FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 4000)
├─→ STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 600000)
├─→ STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (onwe ya, ndabara: 1260000; 0 na-agbanyụ ya)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 80000)
├─→ STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS (na-akpụ oke mgbatị njikere adapte, ndabara: 180000)
└─→ API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS (na-enweta site na REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS, ndabara: 30000)
    ├─→ API_BRIDGE_SERVER_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS (ewepụtara, ndabara: 300000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 60000)
    ├── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 5000)
    └── API_BRIDGE_SERVER_SOCKET_TIMEOUT_MS (ndabara: 0 = gbanyụrụ)
```

| Ọgbanwe                                         | Ọdịnala                                                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| ----------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`                            | _(a tọghị ya)_                                                    | Ụzọ mkpirisi zuru ụwa ọnụ — ọ na-akagbu nhazi ọdịnala nke `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` na `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS` abụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                              | `600000`                                                          | Oge ngwụcha arịrịọ HTTP zuru oke maka oku ndị na-enye ọrụ n'elu (upstream).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS`                        | `600000`                                                          | Oge efu kacha ogologo n'etiti bytes n'elu tupu a kwụsị. Ụdị ndị na-atụle ogologo (extended-thinking) anaghị ekwu karịa sekọnd 90.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `1260000`                                                         | Oge ndụ kacha ogologo nke SSE stream na-arụ ọrụ; anaghị ewegharị ya na bytes n'elu, ọ nweghịkwa njikọ na `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS`. E sitere ya na `timeoutMs` kacha ukwu nke ọ bụla ụdị na ndekọ (registry) (1200000, Codex) tinyere oke 60000, ka ụdị enyere ohere ịgba ego ya zuru oke ghara igbu n'etiti azịza. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_SSE_COMMENTS`                        | _(gbanyụrụ)_                                                      | Ma OmniRoute nwere ike iwepụta akara nkọwa SSE `:` (dịka heartbeat `: keepalive` na ndị na-eso metadata `x-omniroute-*`). A gbanyụrụ ya n'ụzọ ọdịnala (#10524) ebe ndị ahịa kwesịrị OpenAI na-emeso ihe omimi na-agba JSON.parse na akara SSE ọ bụla ma na-akụ (crash) na nkọwa `:`; heartbeat `data:` anaghị emetụta. Tọọ `on`/`true`/`1`/`yes` iji laghachi n'ime ya. `open-sse/utils/sseHeartbeat.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SSE_COALESCE_MS`                               | `0` (gbanyụrụ)                                                    | Nhọrọ onwe: jikọta ihe omume Claude `content_block_delta` ndị so n'otu n'otu (otu block index na otu ụdị delta) a na-eziga ndị ahịa Claude, na-asachapụ o dịkarịa ala kwa N ms. Enweghị mfu; `signature_delta` na ihe omume oke (boundary) anaghị ejikọta ha. Ọ na-emetụta ụdị delta ọ bụla ma e wezụga ma a tọrọ ọgbanwe nke ụdị ọ bụla n'okpuru. `open-sse/utils/sseDeltaCoalesce.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SSE_COALESCE_THINKING_MS`                      | `0` (gbanyụrụ)                                                    | Nchịkwa nke ụdị ọ bụla nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `thinking_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `SSE_COALESCE_TEXT_MS`                          | `0` (gbanyụrụ)                                                    | Nchịkwa nke ụdị ọ bụla nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `text_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SSE_COALESCE_INPUT_JSON_MS`                    | `0` (gbanyụrụ)                                                    | Nchịkwa nke ụdị ọ bụla nke `SSE_COALESCE_MS` maka `input_json_delta`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SSE_FRAME_COALESCE_MS`                         | `0` (gbanyụrụ)                                                    | Nhọrọ onwe: chịkọta SSE frames a na-ezipu n'ime N ms ka ọ bụrụ otu ide (write) nye onye ahịa. Frame mbụ na frames ngwụcha (`[DONE]`, `message_stop`, `error`, `response.completed`/`failed`/`incomplete`) anaghị egbochi; mgbe ịchịkọta dị n'ọrụ, ITL na-atụ oghere ndị a chịkọtara. `open-sse/utils/sseOutputSink.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `SSE_STALL_WATCHDOG`                            | `timer`                                                           | Nhazi nlekota nkwụsị byte (stall watchdog) maka azịza ndị a na-asọ asọ. `timer` na-ewegharị otu timer maka chunk ọ bụla n'elu (ọ na-amụ kpọmkwem na ego nkwụsị); `interval` na-edobe otu nyocha kwa stream na oge `clamp(budget/4, 1s, 5s)`, ka nkwụsị wee kụpụ n'ime ego + oge. `open-sse/utils/streamStallWatchdog.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `STREAM_READINESS_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `80000`                                                           | Oge iji natara ihe omume SSE mbụ nke abụghị ping. Ọ na-anara `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe a tọrọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `STREAM_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `180000`                                                          | Windo njikere ihe omume mbụ na-agbanwe agbanwe kacha ukwu maka arịrịọ stream buru ibu, jupụtara na ngwaọrụ, ma ọ bụ nke nwere echiche elu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_POLICY_ENABLED`           | `true`                                                            | Ọsụ igbu maka heuristic `/goal`. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nchọpụta kpamkpam — anaghị ebuli oge ngwụcha njikere na mweghari stream site na body/headers arịrịọ, na-ebelata ịbawanye oge ngwụcha nke onye ahịa na-achịkwa (timeout amplification).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_READINESS_MAX_TIMEOUT_MS` | `600000`                                                          | Windo njikere ihe omume mbụ kacha ukwu maka ọsọ ndị nkwado `/goal` a chọpụtara ma ọ bụ arịrịọ a manyere site na `x-omniroute-agent-goal`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_AGENT_GOAL_STREAM_RECOVERY`          | `true`                                                            | Kwado mweghari stream n'oge ozugbo maka ọsọ ndị nkwado `/goal` a chọpụtara. Tọọ `false`/`0`/`off` iji gbanyụọ nhọrọ pụrụ iche nke goal. Nke a nwere ike ịGBAKWỤNYE mweghari na nke ọdịnala onye na-arụ ọrụ — ọ naghị akagbu nhọrọ pụ (opt-out) nke `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`/nhazi DB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_DROP_NONSTANDARD_EVENTS`       | `true`                                                            | Wepụ ihe omume SSE `codex.*` ndị abụghị ọkọlọtọ (dịka `codex.rate_limits`) nke na-emebi `responses.stream()` nke OpenAI SDK site na 502. Ọdịnala dị N'ỌRỤ (#11014). Tọọ `0`/`false`/`no`/`off` iji zipụ ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS`                  | _(a tọghị ya)_                                                    | Ụzọ njem Codex app-server nke nhọrọ onwe. WebSocket endpoint (`ws://`/`wss://`) nke sidecar `codex app-server` dị n'ebe ahụ. Mgbe a tọrọ ya tinyere token, a na-ebugharị arịrịọ Codex site na JSON-RPC gaa sidecar kama HTTP Responses API. Enwere ike itọ ya kwa njikọ site na `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerUrl`. `open-sse/executors/codex/appServerConfig.ts` na-eji ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN`            | _(a tọghị ya)_                                                    | Token ikike/bearer a na-enye app-server. Nchịkwa nke njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerToken`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN_FILE`       | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Ụzọ ebe faịlụ na-ejide token ikike nke app-server (site na `codex app-server --ws-token-file`). A na-eji ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_WS_TOKEN` edobeghị. Nchịkwa karịa nke njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerTokenFile`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_CWD`                 | `/tmp`                                                            | Ndekọ ọrụ ebe app-server turn na-agba ọsọ. Nchịkwa karịa nke njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerCwd`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_APPROVAL`            | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Amụma nkwenye e nyefere app-server turn (dịka `never`, `on-request`). Nchịkwa karịa nke njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerApprovalPolicy`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_SANDBOX`             | _(edobeghị)_                                                      | Amụma sandbox e nyefere app-server turn (dịka `read-only`, `workspace-write`, `danger-full-access`). Mgbe edobeghị ya, executor na-adabere na `workspace-write` (nke e siri ike; nke bụ `danger-full-access` na mbụ). Nchịkwa karịa nke njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerSandbox`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_CODEX_APPSERVER_AUTO_APPROVE`        | `false`                                                           | Kweta ajụta nkwenye nke app-server n'onwe ya (ịme command/file/permission na host) na-akpaghị aka. Ọ gbanyụrụ na ndabara — a na-ajụ ajụta ndị ahụ na-akpaghị aka; oku ngwá ọrụ harness anaghị emetụta (ha na-agafe site na `item/tool/call` passthrough dị iche). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`yes`. Nchịkwa karịa nke njikọ ọ bụla: `providerSpecificData.codexAppServerAutoApprove`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `FETCH_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nweta isi azịza.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_FETCH_START_TIMEOUT_CAP_MS`          | `110000`                                                          | Oge ogologo kachasị maka ịchere isi azịza na arịrị ọrụ streaming (ms). `0` na-agbanyụ oke a; `fetchStartTimeoutCapMs` nke provider depee kpọmkwem na-edu n'ihu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `30000` (30s)                                                     | Oge ogologo kachasị ịchere mmalite azịza (ms) maka mgbalị direct no-proxy ọ bụla. Mgbe oge agwụla, a na-anwale ọzọ otu ugboro na socket ọhụrụ; debe `0` iji gbanyụ oke ahụ ma weghara omume gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`     | `300000` (5 nkeji)                                                | Ọnọdụ ala nke mmalite azịza (ms) maka mgbalị direct no-proxy nke host ebumnuche ya bụ nke nzuzo/LAN/loopback (dịka `localhost`, `host.docker.internal`, `192.168.x.x`), ka a ghara ịgbepụ cold start nke Ollama obodo site na ndabara 30s dị n'elu. Ọ na-arụ ọrụ dịka ọnọdụ ala: oge ichere dị irè bụ nke kachasị ukwuu n'etiti uru a na `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS`; `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-adịghị irè na-ewepụ ọnọdụ ala obodo ahụ. Host ndị dị anya anaghị emetụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_RESPONSE_RETRY_TIMEOUT_MS`    | `600000` (10 nkeji)                                               | Oke elu (ms) maka mgbalị RETRY nke socket ọhụrụ mgbe oge mmalite azịza nke mgbalị pooled dị n'elu agwụla (#13703). Ọ na-adị irè naanị mgbe onye na-akpọ etinyela signal oge ọgwụgwụ ya (usoro njikọ/model/provider/`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS` edoziri); signal ahụ bụ ezigbo oke ma ọ na-amalite mbụ n'ụzọ e bu n'obi, yabụ nke a bụ nchekwa nke ukwuu karịa oke larịị — ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị ya, mgbalị ọzọ ahụ ji otu windo mkpirikpi `OMNIROUTE_DIRECT_HEADERS_TIMEOUT_MS` ahụ dị ka mgbalị pooled, wee mee ka model reasoning ndị dị mma ma nke TTFB ha na-adị ngwa ngwa (slow-TTFB) nweta 504. Ọ naghị ekwe ka ọ dị ala karịa ọnọdụ ala larịị dị n'elu; mgbe onye na-akpọ nyereghị signal oge ọgwụgwụ ọ bụla, mgbalị ọzọ ahụ na-edebe ọnọdụ ala larịị ahụ ka ọ ghara ịgbanwe. |
| `FETCH_BODY_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge iji nweta ahụ azịza niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `FETCH_CONNECT_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `30000`                                                           | Oge agwụ maka ịmepụta njikọ TCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `FETCH_KEEPALIVE_TIMEOUT_MS`                    | `4000`                                                            | Oge agwụ maka socket keep-alive na-anọghị n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS`                         | = `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`                                              | Oge agwụ nke proxy TLS fingerprint (wreq-js).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `TLS_FIRST_BYTE_WATCHDOG_MS`                    | `10000`                                                           | Ọ na-agbakọ oge ruo byte mbụ (time-to-first-byte) na ahụ nke wreq-js TLS-fingerprint transport kpọmkwem; `TLS_CLIENT_TIMEOUT_MS` naanị ya enweghị ike ịchụta ahụ kwụsịrị ebe ọ na-edozi ozugbo isi azịza rutere (#12656). Mgbe oge agwụla, ọ na-akagbu wreq reader ma laghachi na direct/proxy dispatcher; `0` na-agbanyụ watchdog ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `RESPONSES_FIRST_BYTE_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `15000`                                                           | Naanị OpenCode executor, ma naanị mgbe flag atụmatụ `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_STALL_ROTATION` dị n'ọrụ (ndabara: gbanyụrụ): ọ na-agbakọ oge ichere byte mbụ nke ahụ nke azịza Responses na-asọ mgbe isi ya gasịrị (#13484). Iyi Responses na-emeghe site na `response.created`, yabụ ịdị jụụ gafere windo a bụ ịkwụsị: a na-ajụ akaụntụ ahụ (cool down) ma arịrị ọrụ ahụ na-agaa akaụntụ ọzọ otu ugboro; ịkwụsị nke abụọ na-adạ ngwa ngwa. `0` na-agbanyụ nche ahụ ọbụlagodi mgbe flag dị n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MS`            | `0` (= gbanyụrụ)                                                  | Naanị OpenCode executor: ọ na-agbakọ oge ichere isi azịza upstream na oku Responses na-asọ mgbe akaụntụ ọzọ ka dị — arịrị ọrụ nọ n'ahịrị na-awụsị ichere ma gaa n'ihu kama ijide windo isi azịza niile. Naanị oku Responses na-asọ ka a na-emetụta (iyi Responses na-emeghe site na `response.created`, yabụ ịdị jụụ ebe ahụ bụ ahịrị, ọ bụghị ịmepụta); chat completions na oku ndị na-asọghị anaghị emetụta, windo ahụ na-ebelata naanị oke elu fetch-start dị irè, ọ naghị agbatị ya. Uru a tụrụ aro mgbe ị na-agbanye ya: `30000`. `0` na-agbanyụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_RESPONSES_HEADERS_WAIT_MAX_ROTATIONS` | `2`                                                               | Naanị OpenCode executor: ọtụtụ ugboro kwa arịrịọ ka ókè ịchere headers dị n'elu nwere ike ịga n'akaụntụ na-esote. Akaụntụ ikpeazụ fọdụrụ na-ejigide windo headers zuru oke mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_AND_RESUME`                      | `false`                                                           | Naanị OpenCode executor: kwụsị arịrịọ ahụ na heartbeat mgbe 429 ndị na-agafe agafe gasịrị ugboro ugboro (ma ọ bụ akara pool-strain ọhụrụ), mesịa kwụgharịa otu ụkwụ e nyere ókè nke ruru akaụntụ 3 n'usoro kama ịgbasa otu niile (#13924). Na ndabara, a gbanyụrụ ya: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa n'akaụntụ na-esote dị ka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_PARK_REPLAY_SERVE_SETASIDE`           | `false`                                                           | Naanị OpenCode executor: mgbe akaụntụ cooldown-ready ọ bụla n'ụkwụ replay park-and-resume ka e tinyere n'akụkụ site n'echeta proxy-refusal, jee ha ozi na agbaghị kama ị ghara ịkwụgharị ihe ọ bụla (#14851, nke na-eso #14750). Na ndabara, a gbanyụrụ ya: ụkwụ replay ahụ dị efu n'ọnọdụ ahụ, dị ka ọ dị tupu #14750.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_READINESS_STALL_RETRY`                  | `false`                                                           | Naanị streaming chat: mgbe body upstream mbụ kwụsịrị tupu ọ mepụta ihe omume a na-eji arụ ọrụ, mee otu mgbalị nke abụọ e nyere ókè site n'otu ụzọ nhazi ahụ na otu ego ịdị njikere ahụ n'enweghị ntaramahụhụ akaụntụ. Na ndabara, a gbanyụrụ ya: body mbụ kwụsịrị na-eme ka arịrịọ daa ada n'enweghị mgbalị ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_RESELECT`                        | `false`                                                           | Naanị OpenCode executor: mgbe 429 sitere n'aka onye na-enye egress-bucketed n'akaụntụ na-enweghị proxy n'okpuru ọnọdụ pool gbara gburugburu, jụọ connection pool maka onye ọzọ maka mgbalị na-esote kama ịmegharị adreesị egress ahụ. Ọ na-edebe usoro, ọ naghị ewepụ: pool gwụrụ na-ejigide omume ugbu a. Na ndabara, a gbanyụrụ ya: 429 ọ bụla na-atụgharị gaa n'akaụntụ na-esote dị ka ọ dị na mbụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OPENCODE_POOL_STRAIN_MARKER_PATH`              | _(a hazibeghị)_                                                   | Naanị OpenCode executor: ụzọ nchịkwa nke akara pool-strain a na-agụ tupu ịkwụsị (`{since, reason, ttl_s}`, nke dabara adaba `/tmp/opencode-pool-strain.json`, #13924). Akara ọhụrụ na-akwụsị n'enweghị ịgụta ọzọ; nke na-adịghị ma ọ bụ nke ochie na-alaghachi n'ọnụ ọgụgụ burst.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `30000`                                                           | Oge ezumike proxy hop maka arịrịọ bridge `/v1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
