| `OMNIROUTE_DEFAULT_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `120000`                                                          | Ihe mgbakwụnye nke `src/shared/utils/fetchTimeout.ts` na-eji mgbe a hazibeghị `FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_PROBE_TIMEOUT_MS`           | `8000`                                                            | Oge ezumike (ms) maka ndị haziri `validationRead` na `modelsProbe` n'ime `src/shared/network/safeOutboundFetch.ts`. Welie ya maka endpoints ndị na-adị nwayọ (Cerebras, Cloudflare AI, Groq) iji gbochie ịgbanwe n'etiti active/error na dashboard. Ọ na-alaghachi na 8000ms maka ụkpụrụ na-ezighi ezi (<1000) ma ọ bụ ụkpụrụ na-abụghị nke ọnụ ọgụgụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`              | `25000`                                                           | Oge ezumike fetch nke relay pụrụ iche n'ime `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). Relay kwụrụ ga-adaba tupu oge ezumike client/agent (~30s) ka ndị na-akpọ hụ ọdịda nke relay pụrụ iche kama oge ezumike upstream izugbe. E nyere ya ókè na `29000` ka ọ na-arụ ọrụ mbụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_RETRY_BACKOFF_MS`                    | `10`                                                              | Backoff mgbalị ọzọ a na-ekekọrịta maka ụzọ direct/relay/proxy retry-otu-ugboro n'ime `open-sse/utils/proxyFetch.ts` (#9158). `0` = mee mgbalị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |

Answer questions directly using search results." na ozi usoro nke onye na-akpọ (`perplexity-web/protocol.ts`). Ka ọ gbanyụrụ n'ụzọ ndabara — Perplexity na-achọ ma ọ bụrụla, ahịrịokwu ahụkwa na-asọpụta n'ime nzaghachi dị ka nkọwa meta maka ndị ahịa koodu. Tọọ `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` iji weghachi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

| `OMNIROUTE_GROK_TLS_GRACE_MS`                   | `10000`                                                           | Ohere oge maka oke njedebe siri ike nke JS, a tinyere n'elu oge njedebe obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                                                           | Oge njedebe arịrịọ wreq-js obodo (`notionTlsClient.ts`); `notion-web` na-ebulili ya kwa-arịrịọ ruo `180000` maka ọmụmụ ogologo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_NOTION_TLS_GRACE_MS`                 | `10000`                                                           | Ohere oge maka oke njedebe siri ike nke JS, a tinyere n'elu oge njedebe obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_BROWSER_POOL`                        | `on`                                                              | Nnọkọ nchọgharị Playwright ekeresịrị maka mkparịta ụka web-cookie nwere nkwado nchọgharị (`browserPool.ts`); tọọ `off` iji gbanyụọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSCURA_BIN`                                   | `auto-detect`                                                     | Ụzọ ebe binary `obscura` dị nke nnọkọ nchọgharị na onye na-eme Cloudflare Playground ji dị ka injin isi (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`); a na-achọpụtara ya na-akpaghị aka site na PATH nke usoro mgbe a na-ahazibeghi ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OBSCURA_CDP_ENDPOINT`                          | _(a na-ahazibeghi)_                                               | Kwalie Obscura na-arụlarị ọrụ (`http://host:port`) kama ịmepụta otu; modul ahụ anaghị enwe usoro (process) ahụ (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OBSCURA_PORT`                                  | `random free port`                                                | Ọdụ ụgbọ (port) doro anya maka `obscura serve` a mụtara; a na-ahọrọ ọdụ ụgbọ n'efu na-akpaghị aka mgbe a na-ahazibeghi ya (`open-sse/services/obscura.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `WEB_COOKIE_USE_BROWSER`                        | `0`                                                               | Họrọ arịrịọ mkparịta ụka web-cookie ka ọ banye n'ụzọ nkwado nchọgharị (`browserBackedChat.ts`); `1` iji mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL`                             | `https://www.kimi.ai`                                             | URL isi maka onye na-eme Kimi Web (kimi.ai Connect-RPC mba ụwa niile) (`kimi-web.ts`); gafee ya naanị maka njedebe mirror/proxy.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `KIMI_WEB_CHAT_URL`                             | `<KIMI_WEB_BASE_URL>/apiv2/kimi.gateway.chat.v1.ChatService/Chat` | Njedebe mkparịta ụka zuru ezu maka onye na-eme Kimi Web (`kimi-web.ts`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOGIN_BROWSER_PATH`                  | _(a chọpụtara na-akpaghị aka)_                                    | Ụzọ ebe ihe Chrome/Edge nke usoro nwere ike ịgba dị maka ịbanye nchọgharị mmekọrịta nke Adobe Firefly (`adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`); na-agafee nchọpụta na-akpaghị aka kwa-OS.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_STANDALONE_DIR`                      | _.build/ ọpụpụ standalone_                                        | Ndejisi oge ịwulite maka ndekọ ọpụpụ standalone nke nzọụkwụ colocation mgbe ịwulite gasịrị na-eji (`scripts/build/colocate-standalone.mjs`); ngwá ọrụ ịwulite, ọ bụghị oge ọrụ (runtime).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |

Mgbalị lekwasị combo na-anakọta oge njedebe arịrịọ upstream e kpebiri (`FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`, ma ọ bụ
`REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` mgbe ọ na-enye ndabara fetch). Tọọ `targetTimeoutMs` n'ime combo, ndabara
combo, ma ọ bụ ndejisi onye na-enye naanị iji mee ka fallback combo gaa ngwa; a na-ekpochi ụkpụrụ
karịa oge njedebe upstream ugbu a ruo oge njedebe upstream.

`comboTimeoutMs` bụ mmefu oge elekere mgbidi (wall-clock) dị iche nke combo dum n'ofe lekwasị failover.
Hapụ ya ka ọ ghara ịhazi ma ọ bụ `0` iji debe ịgagharị na-enweghị oke (nkwụsị njide nke nkeji iri
a koodụrụsi ike n'ime `comboPredicates.ts` ka na-emetụta). Ụkpụrụ dị mma na-anọchi anya
mgbaba nchekwa ahụ maka combo. Debe `comboTimeoutMs` ka ọ gaa ogologo karịa `targetTimeoutMs` ka
failover wee nwee oge mgbe lekwasị mbụ na-agwa nwayọ.

### Ọnụ Ọgụgụ Circuit Breaker

Nhazi circuit breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye. Ndabara na-egosipụta ụkpụrụ e buru ibu e jiri kemgbe v3.6 maka njikọ 500+.

| Agbanwe                                                       | Ndabara   | Faịlụ isi mmalite                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------------------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_THRESHOLD`                   | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oke ọdịda nke usoro maka ndị na-enye OAuth tupu breaker agbasa.                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_OAUTH_RESET_MS`                    | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oghere nrụzigharị (ms) maka breaker nke onye na-enye OAuth.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_THRESHOLD`                 | `12`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oke ọdịda nke usoro maka ndị na-enye API-key.                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_API_KEY_RESET_MS`                  | `30000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oghere nrụzigharị (ms) maka breaker nke onye na-enye API-key.                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_THRESHOLD`                   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oke ọdịda nke usoro maka ndị na-enye mpaghara (Ollama, LM Studio, ...).                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_CIRCUIT_BREAKER_LOCAL_RESET_MS`                    | `15000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Oghere nrụzigharị (ms) maka breaker nke onye na-enye mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `10`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: ọdịda n'ime oghere ahụ tupu onye na-enye OAuth niile abanye na cooldown.                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `900000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: oghere ịgụ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-enye OAuth.                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: cooldown (ms) ozugbo o ruru oke onye na-enye OAuth.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`      | `5`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-enye OAuth na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`     | `8`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ mmulite nrịgo resetTimeout kacha elu nke onye na-enye OAuth.                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_OAUTH_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT`   | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-enye OAuth na-arịgo mgbe gburugburu mepere emepe ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`        | `15`      | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: ọdịda n'ime oghere ahụ tupu onye na-enye API-key niile abanye na cooldown.                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`        | `1800000` | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: oghere ịgụ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-enye API-key.                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`              | `600000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: cooldown (ms) ozugbo o ruru oke onye na-enye API-key.                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_DEGRADATION_THRESHOLD`    | `7`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-enye API-key na-abanye na DEGRADED mgbe ọdịda ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_MAX_BACKOFF_MULTIPLIER`   | `4`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ mmulite nrịgo resetTimeout kacha elu nke onye na-enye API-key.                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_API_KEY_BACKOFF_ESCALATION_COUNT` | `3`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Onye na-enye API-key na-arịgo mgbe gburugburu mepere emepe ruru ọnụ ọgụgụ a.                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_THRESHOLD`          | `2`       | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: ọdịda tupu onye na-enye mpaghara niile abanye na cooldown.                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_FAILURE_WINDOW_MS`          | `300000`  | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: oghere ịgụ ọdịda na-agagharị (ms) maka ndị na-enye mpaghara.                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_PROVIDER_BREAKER_LOCAL_COOLDOWN_MS`                | `60000`   | `open-sse/config/constants.ts`       | Breaker nke ọkwa onye na-enye: cooldown (ms) ozugbo o ruru oke onye na-enye mpaghara.                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`                               | `60000`   | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts` | Cooldown nke igodo ọ bụla (ms) nke ntụgharị API-key nwere ọtụtụ igodo na-etinye mgbe upstream 429 enweghị `Retry-After` (#14573). Uru ndị na-erughị efu ma ọ bụ ndị na-abụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara. |
| `PIN_DROP_BACKOFF_LEVEL`                                      | `2`       | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Omimi backoff nke ebe ahụ ka a na-ewere onye na-enye pin cache ọnọdụ dị ka nke na-adịghị ike na-adịgide, ma a tụfuo pin ahụ maka failover.                                                                       |
| `PIN_DROP_GRACE_MS`                                           | `20000`   | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`         | Oghere mgbochi-flap (ms) na-anabata cooldown nke oge dị mkpirikpi na-agafe tupu a tụfuo pin cache ọnọdụ.                                                                                                         |

### Ọnọdụ

| Ọnọdụ                                | Nhazi                                                   |
| ------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------------- |
| **Ịmepụta koodu na-aga ogologo oge** | `REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS=900000` (15 nkeji)                  |
| **Ịmachị oke ndụ stream niile**      | `STREAM_ACTIVE_TIMEOUT_MS=1260000` (21 nkeji)           |
| **Ọdịda ngwa ngwa maka API mmepụta** | `API_BRIDGE_PROXY_TIMEOUT_MS=10000`                     |
| **Ụdị echiche gbatịrị agbatị**       | `STREAM_IDLE_TIMEOUT_MS=300000` (5 nkeji n'etiti nkewa) |

## 16. Ndekọ

Sistemụ ndekọ na-ede ma na stdout ma na faịlụ ndekọ ndị a na-atụgharịgharị. A na-agụ nhazi niile site na `src/lib/logEnv.ts`.

| Mgbanwe                                   | Ndabara                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| ----------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_LEVEL`                           | `info`                     | Ọkwa ndekọ kacha nta: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_FORMAT`                          | `text`                     | Ọdịdị mmepụta: `text` (nke mmadụ nwere ike ịgụ) ma ọ bụ `json` (ahaziri ahazi).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_TO_FILE`                         | `true`                     | Dee ndekọ na faịlụ yana stdout.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `APP_LOG_FILE_PATH`                       | `logs/application/app.log` | Ụzọ faịlụ ndekọ (n'ihe metụtara mgbọrọgwụ prọjektị ma ọ bụ `DATA_DIR`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILE_SIZE`                   | `50M`                      | Ogo faịlụ kachasị tupu ntụgharị. Ọ na-anabata: `50M`, `1G`, `512K`, ma ọ bụ bytes nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                  | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe faịlụ ndekọ ngwa ndị a tụgharịworo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `APP_LOG_MAX_FILES`                       | `20`                       | Ọnụọgụ ndabere faịlụ ndekọ ndị a tụgharịworo kachasị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`                 | `7`                        | Ụbọchị ole a ga-edowe ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ/oku na ebe nchekwa data.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CALL_LOG_MAX_ENTRIES`                    | `10000`                    | Ọnụọgụ ndenye ndekọ oku kachasị na buffer dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `CALL_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`                | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `call_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ENABLE_REQUEST_LOGS`                     | _(edoghị ya)_              | Manye ndekọ arịrịọ zuru ezu ka ọ gbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ, na-akagbu ntọala dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MAX_PENDING_REQUEST_AGE_MS`              | `3600000` (1 elekere)      | Ogologo oge kachasị maka ndenye ndekọ arịrịọ nọ n'ọrụ ma bụrụ nke a hapụrụ tupu nhicha n'ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_CAPTURE_STREAM_CHUNKS` | `false`                    | Chekwaa iberibe stream na pipeline artifacts mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`. Nhọrọ nke onye ọrụ ga-eme (`true`) — agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji chekwaa ohere diski.                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CALL_LOG_PIPELINE_MAX_SIZE_KB`           | `512`                      | Ogo artifact ndekọ oku pipeline kachasị na KB mgbe `call_log_pipeline_enabled=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LOGS_TABLE_MAX_ROWS`               | `100000`                   | Ahịrị kachasị na tebụl SQLite `proxy_logs` tupu ebelata ha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_LOG_INCLUDE_IPS`                   | `false`                    | Tinye IP ndị ahịa/egress na prefix akaụntụ na ndekọ console `[ProxyEgress]`. Ihe ndekọ proxy-log nke dashboard/ebe nchekwa data na-edowe nkọwa niile.                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_LOG_FIRST_CHUNK_TIMING`            | `false`                    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `"true"` ma ọ bụ `"1"` iji dekọọ `first_chunk_ms` (site na mbido izipu ruo na byte mbụ bara uru nke body) n'ahịrị proxy-log. Ọ na-etinye body upstream ọ bụla n'ime stream passthrough umengwụ ma na-eme patch n'ahịrị ahụ ozugbo byte ahụ rutere; agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara ka response gafee n'enweghị mgbanwe. |
| `APP_LOG_ROTATION_CHECK_INTERVAL_MS`      | `60000` (1 nkeji)          | Ugboro ole `src/lib/logRotation.ts` na-enyochagharị nha faịlụ ndekọ nọ n'ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CHAT_LOG_TEXT_LIMIT`                     | `65536`                    | Ogologo string kachasị a na-edowe na chat log artifacts (ndabara bụ 64 KB).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CHAT_LOG_ARRAY_TAIL_ITEMS`               | `128`                      | Ọnụọgụ ihe array a na-edowe site na ngwụcha mgbe a na-ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_DEPTH`                      | `6`                        | Omimi ntinye kachasị tupu ebipụ chat log payloads.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_OBJECT_KEYS`                | `80`                       | Object keys kachasị a na-edowe na chat log payloads (0 = enweghị oke).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `CHAT_LOG_MAX_BODY_KB`                    | `1024`                     | Nha body arịrịọ/response dum (KB) tupu e jiri nchịkọta nkịtị dochie ya kama clone zuru ezu. Welie nke a ma ọ bụrụ na mkparịta ụka agentic ogologo na-egosi placeholder kama ozi ndị ahụ n'ezie na dashboard.                                                                                                                     |
| `CHAT_DEBUG_FILE`                         | `false`                    | Mgbe ọ bụ true, `serializeArtifactForStorage` na-amafere mbipụ dabere na nha. Maka debug naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## 17. Nkwalite Ebe Nchekwa

| Agbanwe                       | Ndabara                                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| ----------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`         | _auto_ (bare metal); **`1024` na foto Docker**                                                              | **A na-atụ aro** oke V8 heap Docker/standalone (MB). Mgbe a na-esetịghị ya, a na-atụle ya n'ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe (~35% nke RAM sistemu, a machibidoro ya na `[512, 4096]`); `512` bụ naanị ala kacha ala mgbe a na-apụghị ịgụ ngụkọta ebe nchekwa. Na `run-standalone.mjs` (Docker CMD), a na-agbakwụnye uru **a kapịrị ọnụ** dị ka `--max-old-space-size` ma ọ **na-merie** flag NODE_OPTIONS heap na-emegbu ya (V8 last-flag). `omniroute serve` ka na-ahọrọ NODE_OPTIONS heap dị adị (#5238). Esetịla ha abụọ na ọnụ ọgụgụ dị iche iche — usoro ahụ na-edekọ ịdọ aka nántị na-akpọ aha uru abụọ ahụ na onye mmeri. **Foto Docker gọọmentị na-esetị `1024` mgbe niile, yabụ ntụle anaghị arụ ọrụ ebe ahụ.** Coding-agent `/v1/responses` chọrọ `8192`–`12288` gbakwụnye ohere cgroup — lee [Ntuziaka Docker — runtime RAM](../guides/DOCKER_GUIDE.md#runtime-ram-for-coding-agents). |
| `OMNIROUTE_RSS_PRESSURE_MB`   | _auto_ (`max(3072, 2 × OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB)`; ọ na-adịghị arụ ọrụ mgbe a na-esetịghị `OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB`) | `open-sse/utils/resourcePressure.ts`                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | Fuse process-RSS zuru oke (MB) maka ebe nchekwa native/external nke V8 `heapUsed` na-apụghị ịhụ; n'elu ya, arịrị ọhụrụ na-enweta 503 `resource_pressure`. RSS na-adịkarịghị adata mgbe mmụba gasịrị, yabụ jikọta ya na `OMNIROUTE_PRESSURE_SELF_RESTART`. |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`       | `50`                                                                                                        | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke ndenye system prompt e cache-ara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`      | `2097152` (2 MB)                                                                                            | Ogo ngụkọta kacha elu nke cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `PROMPT_CACHE_TTL_MS`         | `300000` (5 nkeji)                                                                                          | TTL nke ndenye cache prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE`     | `100`                                                                                                       | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke nzaghachi temperature=0 e cache-ara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES`    | `4194304` (4 MB)                                                                                            | Ogo ngụkọta kacha elu nke cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `SEMANTIC_CACHE_TTL_MS`       | `1800000` (30 nkeji)                                                                                        | TTL nke ndenye cache semantic.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CORPUS_CACHE_SIZE` | `5`                                                                                                         | Mgbọrọgwụ corpus mpaghara nke na-edobe index dị ndụ na nchekwa n'otu oge (`src/lib/localCorpus/configured.ts`). LRU: na oke ahụ, a na-ewepụ index nke mgbọrọgwụ kacha nso e ji mee ihe n'oge na-adịbeghị anya ma wughachie ya na ajụjụ ya na-esote. A machibidoro ya na kacha nta nke `1`; uru na-abụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ na-alaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `STREAM_HISTORY_MAX`          | `50`                                                                                                        | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke mmemme stream na-adịbeghị anya na buffer nlele ndụ Dashboard.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT`      | _(a na-esetịghị ya)_                                                                                        | Nchịkọta ogologo context zuru ụwa ọnụ (tokens) maka onye na-enye ọ bụla nke na-enweghị agbanwe `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`. Mgbe e setịpụrụ ya, ọ **na-anọchi** uru catalog, nchịkọta dashboard na registry maka ndị na-enye ndị ahụ; mgbe a na-esetịghị ya, OmniRoute na-eji yinye ya e wuru n'ime, nke ikpeazụ ya bụ `128000`. Lee [Ogologo context per-provider](#per-provider-context-length-context_length_provider).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `USAGE_TOKEN_BUFFER`          | `100`                                                                                                       | Ohere token mgbakwunye e chekwara mgbe a na-esocha oke ojiji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

### Ogologo context per-provider (`CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`)

`open-sse/services/contextManager.ts` (`getEnvOverride()` / `resolveTokenLimit()`) na-agụkwa ụdị
**per-provider** nke a rụrụ aha ya site na ID onye na-enye na oge ọrụ, yabụ a na-apụghị
edepụta ya ahịrị site na ahịrị na tebụl dị n'elu ma ọ bụ na `.env.example`.

**Aha.** `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>`, ebe `<PROVIDER>` bụ provider ID e dere n'ụkwụ edemede ukwu (uppercase), ebe a na-eji `_` dochie mkpụrụedemede ọ bụla nke na-abụghị `A-Z` / `0-9`. Provider ID ahụ bụ nke izugbe mgbe e doziri alias (akụkụ `provider` nke eriri `provider/model` mgbe e doziri alias dị mkpirikpi). Ọdụ (nodes) OpenAI- ma ọ bụ Anthropic-compatible ndị e mepụtara omenala na-eji node ID ha mepụtara, ya mere UUID bụ akụkụ nke aha mgbanwe ahụ. Ọnụọgụ ahụ bụ ọnụọgụ zuru eke dị mma nke tokens; ihe ọ bụla ọzọ (oghere efu, `0`, nke ọdịda, nke na-abụghị ọnụọgụ) a na-ahapụ ya, ka nhazi ahụ gaa n'usoro na-esote.

| Provider ID                                                 | Aha mgbanwe                                                                |
| ----------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| agentrouter                                                 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER                                                 |
| openrouter                                                  | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENROUTER                                                  |
| openai-compatible-chat-0f8e2c1a-5b7d-4e3f-9a6c-2d1b8e4f7a90 | CONTEXT_LENGTH_OPENAI_COMPATIBLE_CHAT_0F8E2C1A_5B7D_4E3F_9A6C_2D1B8E4F7A90 |

**Ọnọdụ mbụ.** Maka ihe mmekọ `provider/model`, usoro mbụ nke na-enye ọnụọgụ bụ nke na-eri:

1. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_<PROVIDER>` maka provider ahụ.
2. `CONTEXT_LENGTH_DEFAULT` (mbochichi gburugburu zuru ụwa ọnụ).
3. Windo nke model ọ bụla: mbochichi ọdịnaya e chekwara (e tọrọ na dashboard, ma ọ bụ reconciler nchọpụta pin-nyere ya naanị ya, lee `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`), ma ọ bụghị otu a, ọnụọgụ katalọgụ e jikọtara / models.dev.
4. `defaultContextLength` nke registry provider ahụ.
5. Nyocha aha model (`claude` 200000, `gemini` 1000000, `gpt` / `o1` / `o3` / `o4` / `codex` 400000).
6. Ọnọdụ mbụ e wuru n'ime maka provider ọ bụla, mgbe ahụ `128000`.

Usoro gburugburu abụọ ahụ na-emetụta **provider niile**: model ọ bụla nke provider ahụ na-enweta oke otu ahụ, ha na-eri mbochichi dashboard nke model ọ bụla. Mgbe naanị otu model nke provider chọrọ windo dị iche, jiri mbochichi nke model ọ bụla nọ na dashboard kama.

**Oke metụtara.** Usoro a na-akwalite nyocha windo ọdịnaya n'oge arịrịọ (jụ `context_length_exceeded` / "Input exceeds context window" n'ime `open-sse/handlers/chatCore.ts`), mmefu mbẹda prompt na oke ọdịnaya combo. `context_length` nke `GET /v1/models` na-akwasa nwere ike ịbụ ihe si na metadata katalọgụ ma ọ bụ registry, a naghị ekwe nkwa na ọ ga-egosipụta mbochichi gburugburu ahụ.

**Ọmụmaatụ.** Provider nke model ya na-anabata windo token 1,050,000 ebe katalọgụ na-akọ 128,000:

```dotenv
CONTEXT_LENGTH_AGENTROUTER=1050000
```

Malitegharịa server mgbe ị gbanwere ya: a na-agụ ọnụọgụ ahụ site na gburugburu usoro server. Na **ngwa desktọpụ**, server na-arụ ọrụ dị ka usoro nwa (child process), ọ hụghị mgbanwe sistemu e tọrọ mgbe e malitere ngwa ahụ; tinye akara ahụ na faịlụ `.env` nke ngwa ahụ na-ebu (usoro ọchọ dị na [Ntuziaka Electron: Ọchọ faịlụ gburugburu](../guides/ELECTRON_GUIDE.md#environment-file-lookup)), wee mechie ngwa ahụ kpamkpam ma malite ya ọzọ.

### Mbẹda

| Mgbanwe                               | Nke mbụ | Nkọwa                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_RTK_TRUST_PROJECT_FILTERS` | e tọghị | Tụkwasị `.rtk/filters.json` nke ọrụ ahụ obi na-enweghị hash `.rtk/trust.json`. Jiri ya naanị na mmepe obodo a na-achịkwa. |

### Injin Echeta (atụmatụ 21)

### Ọnụ ego event-loop nke echeta, nkà, na mmelite token (#10349)

OmniRoute bụ **otu usoro Node**. Mwepụ/inyeghachị echeta, itinye nkà, na mmelite token provider na-agba ọsọ na **otu event loop ahụ** dị ka `GET /healthz` na dashboard. Ha abụghị worker thread.

| Ọrụ                       | Koodu                              | Nke mbụ                                     | Njikwa onye ọrụ                                                                                                            |
| ------------------------- | ---------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| Mwepụ / inyeghachị echeta | `src/lib/memory/`                  | Dashboard **memoryEnabled** (nke mbụ bụ on) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory**. Enweghị kill switch env dị iche karịa ịgbanyụ atụmatụ ahụ na ntọala.                        |
| Itinye nkà                | `src/lib/skills/injection.ts`      | Dashboard **skillsEnabled** (nke mbụ bụ on) | Gbanyụọ **Settings → Memory/Skills** (`skillsEnabled`). Njikwa sandbox dị n'okpuru na-eke oke ọrụ naanị mgbe e tinyewo ya. |
| Mmelite token             | `src/sse/services/tokenRefresh.ts` | On maka providers OAuth/web e jikọtara      | Kwụsịnjikọ provider ahụ ma ọ bụ hapụ ka tokens dị irè; enweghị env `TOKEN_REFRESH=0` taa.                                  |

Ọ bụrụ na `/healthz` na-ebu nwayọ na igwe dị jụụ, gbanyụọ echeta + nkà mbụ, wee lelee ibu katalọgụ/mbẹda (#10303, #9685). Atụmatụ ndị a na-ahapụ ohere n'ebe `await` mana ha ka na-asọ mpi maka otu eri (thread) ahụ.

Oyi embedding, ụlọọrụ vector na njikwa reranking maka usoro ncheta na-adịgide adịgide (`src/lib/memory/`).

| Mgbanwe                              | Nhazi dabara                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------ | --------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_TTL_MS`      | `300000` (nkeji 5)          | TTL maka cache embedding dị n'ime nchekwa (kwa signature source/model/dim).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `MEMORY_EMBEDDING_CACHE_MAX`         | `1000`                      | Ọnụ ọgụgụ entries LRU kacha elu echekwara na cache embedding.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_TRANSFORMERS_MODEL`          | `Xenova/all-MiniLM-L6-v2`   | HF repo id maka pipeline MiniLM mpaghara opt-in `@huggingface/transformers` (~23 MB int8, ~400 MB RAM).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_MODEL`                | `minishlab/potion-base-8M`  | HF repo id maka embedder lookup-table potion/Model2Vec kwụrụ otu ebe. A na-ebudata ya nanị mgbe a chọrọ ya n'ime ndekọ cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `MEMORY_STATIC_CACHE_DIR`            | `<DATA_DIR>/embeddings`     | Ndekọ ebe a na-eche faịlụ model potion kwụrụ otu ebe. Ọ dabeere n'okpuru `DATA_DIR` mgbe a hazighị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HF_HUB_ENDPOINT`                    | `https://huggingface.co`    | Ịdochie base URL Hugging Face Hub nke `staticPotion.ts` na-eji (dị ka mirror endpoint maka ntọlite air-gapped).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `MEMORY_VEC_TOP_K`                   | `20`                        | Top-K dabara nke `sqlite-vec` brute-force vector search dị n'ime `src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts` na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `MEMORY_RRF_K`                       | `60`                        | Kọnstantị Reciprocal Rank Fusion `k` maka wepụta hybrid FTS5 + vector (usoro sqlite-vec).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `VECTOR_STORE_DISABLE_VEC`           | `false`                     | Oghere nnwale/nchọpụta na `getVectorStore()` (`src/lib/memory/vectorStore.ts`): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, ọ na-amanye vector store ka ọ bụrụ `null` (na-emeghe gburugburu ebe cloud/WASM na-enweghị `sqlite-vec`), na-ebelata wepụta nchekwa ka ọ bụrụ ọchọ ọkwa okwu FTS5. Ahapụla ya ka a hazighị ya na mmepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTION_API_KEY`                     | _(a hazighị)_               | Igodo API maka backend Notion (nke preset backend a maara nke `genericBackend.ts` na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `NOTION_API_URL`                     | `https://api.notion.com/v1` | Base URL maka Notion API (enwere ike ịdochie ya maka ndị ọzọ Notion self-hosted).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_KEY`                   | _(a hazighị)_               | Igodo API maka Obsidian Vault backend (nke preset backend a maara nke `genericBackend.ts` na-eji).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OBSIDIAN_API_URL`                   | `http://localhost:27123`    | Base URL maka Obsidian Vault API (enwere ike ịdochie ya maka vault dị anya).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED`         | `false`                     | Ịgba ọkụ isi nke TV6 typed memory decay. **Opt-in (n'ụzọ dabara gbanyụrụ)** — sweep ahụ **na-ehichapụ** memories rụrụ arụ. Mgbe ọ gbanyụrụ, `access_count`/`last_accessed_at` bụ telemetry dị ọcha na ọ dịghị ihe a na-ehichapụ mgbe ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_EPISODIC_DAYS`   | `30`                        | TTL (ụbọchị) mgbe nke ahụ memory `episodic` a naghị eji eme ihe na-arụ arụ. `0` na-eme ka episodic nwee mgbochi. Ụdị durable (`factual`/`procedural`/`semantic`) na-enwe mgbochi mgbe niile. Elekere arụ arụ na-ebu `last_accessed_at` n'ala ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ACCESS_IMMUNITY` | `3`                         | Memory etinyere `>=` ugboro a na-enwe mgbochi megide arụ arụ n'agbanyeghị ụdị. `0` na-agbanyụ mgbochi ohere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_SWEEP_INTERVAL`  | `0` (agbanyụrụ)             | Interval (sekọnd) maka sweep arụ arụ kwa oge nhọrọ na `src/lib/memory/typedDecay.ts`. `0`/a hazighị = ọ dịghị sweep kwa oge. Opt-in ugboro abụọ: ọ chọkwara `MEMORY_TYPED_DECAY_ENABLED=true`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRICT_SYSTEM_PROVIDERS`  | _(a hazighị)_               | ID providers kewapụrụ na-comma (anaghị ele ukwu/nta anya) nke na-anabata ozi `system` **nanị na index 0** (`src/lib/memory/injection.ts`). Maka ndị a, splice nchekwa nke dị n'etiti array nke cache na-echebe adịghị nchebe na mkparịta ụka multi-turn, ya mere a na-ejikọ/tinye nchekwa n'ihu dị ka ozi system nke mbụ. Ọ dabeere na nanị `xiaomi-mimo`/`mimo`; gbatie ya maka endpoints OpenAI-compatible self-hosted (dị ka Qwen3.5+/3.6) nke template chats ha na-amanye otu mgbochi ozi-system-naanị-n'ihu ahụ. |

### Ihe Nlereanya Docker RAM dị ala

`128` bụ dashboard naanị. Agents nka na-ede koodu na heap a na-enwe `FATAL ERROR` n'oge `/v1/responses` ogologo. Ejila ihe nlereanya a mee ihe dị ka gateway Claude/Codex/Grok.

```bash
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=128
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=20
PROMPT_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=524288        # 512 KB
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_SIZE=25
SEMANTIC_CACHE_MAX_BYTES=1048576     # 1 MB
STREAM_HISTORY_MAX=10
```

| Mgbanwe                 | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta        | Nkọwa                                            |
| ----------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------ | ------------------------------------------------ |
| `PRICING_SYNC_ENABLED`  | `false`       | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Mmekọrịta ọnụahịa oge ọbụla a na-ahọrọ ịgbalite. |
| `PRICING_SYNC_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.              |
| `PRICING_SYNC_SOURCES`  | `litellm`     | `src/lib/pricingSync.ts` | Isi mmalite data ndị rikoma kewara.              |

## Mmekọrịta Arena ELO

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ---------------------------- | ------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_ENABLED`     | `true`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                     | Mmekọrịta ELO nke bọọdụ ndị-isi Arena AI n'oge ụfọdụ, nke a pụrụ ịhazi site na Dashboard Feature Flags ma ọ bụ jiri `false` kwụsị ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `MODELS_CATALOG_PREFIX_MODE` | `dual`        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/models/catalog.ts` | Ụdị prefix a na-eji maka id ụdịdị na `GET /v1/models`. `dual` na-akpọsa ma prefix alias dị mkpụmkpụ ma prefix provider canonical maka ụdịdị ọ bụla (ndakọrịta azụ — ọ fọrọ nke nta ka ọ bụrụ na ọ na-eme katalọgụ ahụ okpukpu abụọ); `alias` na-ewepụta otu id maka ụdịdị ọ bụla; `canonical` na-ewepụta naanị prefix provider-id zuru ezu (providers nke alias ha bụrịrị id canonical na-edobe otu ndenye ha). Clients nwere ike iji `?prefix=alias` gbanwee ya maka arịrịọ ọ bụla. Lee [API_REFERENCE](API_REFERENCE.md#model-id-prefixes-prefix). |
| `ARENA_ELO_SYNC_INTERVAL`    | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/arenaEloSync.ts`                                                            | Oge dị n'etiti mmekọrịta na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

## Provider PromptQL Playground (Na-abụghị nke Gọọmentị/Nnwale)

Njikọ nnọkọ GraphQL e ji reverse engineering mepụta maka prompt.ql.app (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-arụtụ aka na endpoints playground ọha; gbanwee naanị maka deployment PromptQL nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị/ọzọ.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara                                                            | Faịlụ Isi Mmepụta                                                       | Nkọwa                                                          |
| ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROMPTQL_GRAPHQL_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.prompt.ql.app/promptql/playground-v2-hge/v1/graphql` | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint GraphQL e ji arụ ọrụ nkata/nnọkọ.                     |
| `PROMPTQL_CREDITS_ENDPOINT`  | `https://data.pro.ql.app/v1/graphql`                               | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`, `open-sse/services/usage/promptql.ts` | Endpoint GraphQL e ji ajụ banyere itule kredit/ojiji.          |
| `PROMPTQL_TOKEN_REFRESH_URL` | `https://auth.pro.ql.app/ddn/project/token`                        | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Endpoint e ji anwa ime ka token dị ọhụrụ.                      |
| `PROMPTQL_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`   | `180000`                                                           | `open-sse/executors/promptql.ts`                                        | Oge kachasị (ms) iji na-enyocha `thread_events` tupu oge agwụ. |

Àkwà mmiri nnọkọ e mepụtara site na reverse engineering maka hyperagent.com (`src/shared/constants/providers/web-cookie.ts`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-atụ aka na endpoint ịgba ụgwọ/ojiji ọha; gbanwee ya naanị maka nrụnye HyperAgent nke ị na-akwado n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nrụnye ọzọ.

Nnyocha ego USD nkeonwe na ojiji Kilo Pass maka onye na-eweta Kilo Code. Ọ bụ nhọrọ — ndabara na-atụ aka na Kilo API ọha; gbanwee ya naanị maka relay/ngwa nnwale. Nkwenye njirimara na-eji token nnweta OAuth dị ugbu a nke njikọ ahụ.

| Ngbanwe        | Ndabara               | Faịlụ Isi                             | Nkọwa                                                           |
| -------------- | --------------------- | ------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `KILO_API_URL` | `https://api.kilo.ai` | `open-sse/services/usage/kilocode.ts` | URL ntọala eji enweta ego Kilo Code nkeonwe na ojiji Kilo Pass. |

## Onye Na-eweta Weebụ Adobe Firefly (Nke Na-abụghị nke Gọọmenti/Nnwale)

Mmegharị nnọkọ nke ihe nchọgharị na-achịkwa maka onye na-eweta weebụ Adobe Firefly
(`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`, `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`,
`open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`). Ọ bụ nhọrọ — a haziri ndabara niile maka nrụnye
desktọpụ nkịtị.

> **E wepụrụ ya na #9255.** E ji ọrụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị Playwright dochie sistemụ Chrome ochie e jikọrọ na CDP (adobeFireflyChromeRuntime.ts), ya mere ntọala njikwa ya adịghịzi. Ngbanwe
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_ CDP_PORT / VISIBLE / HEADED / PING / FORCE_RESTART, tinyere
> ADOBE_FIREFLY_LOGIN_WAIT_MS na ADOBE_FIREFLY_FORTER_WAIT_MS, anaghị agụ ebe ọ bụla n'ime
> codebase — ịtọ ha enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.

| Ngbanwe                              | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                                       | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------ | ---------- | ----------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`      | `0`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts` | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `1` maka Chrome headless n'ezie (a maara na ọ naghị arụ ọrụ maka generate; maka debugging naanị).                         |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`      | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Ịhọrọ ịgbanye/gbanyụọ ikpo ihe nchọgharị ọkụ tupu oge eruo. `0` na-akwụsị ikpo ọkụ tupu oge eruo (mgbake 408 n'etiti batch ka na-arụ ọrụ). |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`         | `1`        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji kwụsị ichekwa nnọkọ Adobe Firefly na diski.                                                                       |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`    | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere kacha nta (ms) a na-amanye n'etiti submit ndị na-esochi ibe ha, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                     |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`   | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`      | Oghere mgbakwunye (ms) a na-etinye mgbe batch gara nke ọma, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS` | _(edoghị)_ | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyClient.ts`       | Oge nchere ntọala (ms) tupu izipu arịrịọ generation, nke na-anọchi ndabara arụnyere.                                                       |

## 19. Mmekọrịta Model (Dev)

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara       | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| ----------------------------------- | ------------- | ---------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_ENABLED`           | _(edobeghị)_  | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Nchịkwa siri ike maka mmekọrịta ọnụahịa models.dev. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya = soro Ntọala > AI (`modelsDevSyncEnabled`). `0`/`false`/`off`/`no` **na-emeri DB** ma na-amafere ma mmekọrịta oge niile ma nyocha SQL/JSON nke `getModelsDevPricing()` (mgbake mgbe dashboard rapaara n'otu event loop ahụ). `1`/`true`/`on`/`yes` na-amanye ka mmekọrịta rụọ ọrụ. Nchekwa/nhichapụ ọnụahịa ka na-akpọ `backupDbFile("pre-write")`, nke anaghị eme ihe n'okpuru mmachi nke nkeji 60 ma ọ bụ `DISABLE_SQLITE_AUTO_BACKUP`. |
| `MODELS_DEV_SYNC_INTERVAL`          | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/modelsDevSync.ts`         | Ogologo oge n'etiti mmekọrịta katalọgụ model n'oge mmepe, na sekọnd.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CONTEXT_WINDOW_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` | `86400` (24h) | `src/lib/contextWindowResolver.ts` | Ogologo oge (na sekọnd) maka ihe mmezi context-window nke na-emezi onwe ya (5004): ọ na-edobe windo ndị provider kwupụtara site na nchọpụta `/models` dịka mgbanwe `auto:discovery` mgbe ha dị iche na katalọgụ. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya. Ọ na-eji data emekọrịtalarị ọzọ (enweghị fetch ọhụrụ); ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-edegharị mgbanwe `manual`.                                                                                                                                                            |
