## 20. Ntọala pụrụ iche nke Provider

| Variable                                       | Ndabere                                       | Faịlụ isi                                                                          | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OPENROUTER_CATALOG_TTL_MS`                    | `86400000` (24h)                              | `src/lib/catalog/openrouterCatalog.ts`                                             | TTL cache katalog ụdị OpenRouter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `MODEL_CATALOG_INCLUDE_NAMES`                  | `true`                                        | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                   | Gụnye ogige `name` ndị kwesịrị ekwesị maka ngosipụta n'ime nzaghachi `/v1/models`. Gbanyụọ ya maka ndị ahịa na-atụ anya naanị ID.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CATALOG_BUILD_TIMEOUT_MS`                     | `8000` (8s)                                   | `src/app/api/v1/models/catalogCache.ts`                                            | Oke nke oge ichere cold-path maka mbulite ọzọ katalog `GET /v1/models` jikọtara ọnụ (#12627). A na-agụta ahịrị ndị a na-etinyebeghị timestamp dị ka nke gara aga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60s)                                 | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Ogologo oge ka a na-eji nzaghachi `GET /api/usage/analytics` nke na-adị arọ ọzọ maka otu eriri ajụjụ (#15629). `0` na-agbanyụ cache.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`     | `1000` (1s)                                   | `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`                                          | Naanị nzaghachi analytics ndị ruru opekata mpe oge a iji wuo ka a na-etinye n'cache, ka obere instances na-ahụ data ọhụrụ mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_CACHE_METRICS_MIN_COMPUTE_MS`       | a hazibeghị (gbanyụrụ)                        | `src/app/api/cache/route.ts` via `src/lib/usage/analyticsResponseCache.ts`         | Opt-in (#16361). Mgbe a haziri ya na nọmba zuru ezu na-abụghị nke ọjọọ N, a na-enye akụkụ akụkọ ojiji nke `GET /api/cache` (ngụkọta prompt-cache + trend) site na cache nzaghachi analytics a kesara mgbe ọ ruru opekata mpe N ms iji wuo, maka windo `trendHours` a machiri n'oke, maka `OMNIROUTE_ANALYTICS_CACHE_TTL_MS` (akụkọ ihe mere eme nwere ike igbu oge ruo TTL ahụ; `DELETE /api/cache` na-akagbu ya). Nke a na-ahazibeghị ma ọ bụ nke ezighi ezi = a na-agụ akụkọ ojiji ozugbo na arịrị ọ bụla.                                  |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`                   | `120000`                                      | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Oge ichere kacha elu maka ọrụ mmepụta ihe oyiyi NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `NANOBANANA_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `2500`                                        | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration.ts`                                             | Ugboro a na-akpọtụ ọrụ NanoBanana.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SUBMIT_BASE_DELAY_MS`           | `8000`                                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyUpscale.ts`                                         | Nkwụsị isi maka exponential backoff nke ịnyefe ọzọ nke Adobe Firefly upscale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `AWS_REGION`                                   | _(a hazibeghị)_                               | `src/lib/providers/validation.ts`, `open-sse/handlers/audioSpeech.ts`              | Mpaghara a na-eji wuo endpoint AWS Bedrock (Kiro, audio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| `AWS_DEFAULT_REGION`                           | _(a hazibeghị)_                               | `src/lib/providers/validation.ts`, `open-sse/handlers/audioSpeech.ts`              | Nzọpụta mgbe a na-ahazibeghị `AWS_REGION`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CLOUDFLARE_ACCOUNT_ID`                        | _(a hazibeghị)_                               | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-ai.ts`                                              | ID akaụntụ maka Cloudflare Workers AI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CLOUDFLARE_PLAYGROUND_CHROME_PATH`            | _(a hazibeghị)_                               | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`                                      | Ụzọ binary Chrome desktọpụ zuru ezu maka onye na-agba ọrụ Cloudflare AI Playground, a na-eji ya mgbe nlele fingerprint headless gbochiri Chromium Playwright jikọtara ọnụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CODEBUDDY_SYSTEM_PROMPT_MAX_LEN`              | ∅ (∞) — _(a hazibeghị = enweghị oke ogologo)_ | `open-sse/executors/codebuddy/shared.ts`                                           | Oke ogologo nhọrọ maka system prompts nke onye na-akpọ zitere CodeBuddy (CN na International). N'ụzọ dabere, naanị prompt nke na-ebu akara njirimara onye nnọchi coding ka a na-anọchi na nke na-enweghị ike (ihe nzacha ọdịnaya Tencent na-ajụ ha); a na-edebe prompts ogologo. Ọnụ ọgụgụ zuru ezu > 0 na-anọchi anya system prompt ọ bụla karịrị mkpụrụedemede ole na ole ahụ; nke a na-ahazibeghị, `0` ma ọ bụ uru na-abụghị nọmba zuru ezu na-agbanyụ oke ahụ. Nnochi ọ bụla na-edekọ iwu nke rụrụ ọrụ (akara njirimara ma ọ bụ ogologo). |

Volume data ya nwere credentials OAuth/API nke provider, yana image ekpinyere enweghị override `api-keys` nke data-plane dabere na env (naanị `config.yaml` e mount-ara), ya mere `0.0.0.0` ji ekpughe ọrụ nwere credentials n'elu LAN dum.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |

| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                                   | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Nnabata bind/connect nke ọrụ Dario etinyere (naanị loopback dị ka ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                        | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Pọtụ ọrụ Dario etinyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `DARIO_HOST`                                   | `127.0.0.1`                                   | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Nnabata bind/connect nke ọrụ Dario etinyere (naanị loopback dị ka ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DARIO_PORT`                                   | `3456`                                        | `open-sse/executors/dario.ts`                                                      | Pọtụ ọrụ Dario etinyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `LOCAL_HOSTNAMES`                              | _(efu)_                                       | `open-sse/config/providerRegistry.ts`                                              | Aha nnabata ndị ọzọ kewapụrụ site na rikọ nke a na-ahụ dị ka "local" (aha ọrụ Docker, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |

`ENABLE_CC_COMPATIBLE_PROVIDER` bụ naanị maka relay ndị ọzọ na-anabata naanị ndị ahịa Claude Code.
OmniRoute na-edegharị arịrị ka relay ndị ahụ nabata ha. Ọ bụrụ na ị chọrọ naanị iji
Claude Code CLI, ma ọ bụ ị maghị nke ọma ihe relay ndị a bụ, debe nke a ka ọ gbanyụsịrị ma
tinye provider Anthropic-compatible nkịtị kama ya.

---

## 21. Ahụ Ike Proxy

| Mgbanwe                                           | Ndabara                   | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                                                                             | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `PROXY_FAST_FAIL_TIMEOUT_MS`                      | `2000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                  | Oge njedebe nke nyocha ahụ ike fast-fail.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `PROXY_LATENCY_WINDOW_HOURS`                      | `3`                       | `src/lib/db/proxies.ts`                                                                   | Windo oge (awa) maka ịgbakọ nkezi latency nke proxy ndị a họpụtara na atụmatụ pool e kwalitere maka latency.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS`                       | `30000`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                  | TTL nke cache nsonaazụ nyocha ahụ ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_UNHEALTHY_CACHE_TTL_MS`             | `2000`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth.ts`                                                                  | TTL cache maka nyocha ahụ ike proxy ndị dara ada. Mee ka nke a dị mkpirikpi karịa `PROXY_HEALTH_CACHE_TTL_MS` ka oge njedebe proxy nke na-adịru oge n'okpuru ibu ọrụ dị elu wee nwaa ọzọ ngwa ngwa na-amachibidoghị fast-fail maka proxy ndị nwụrụ n'ezie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_ENABLED`                            | `true`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Tinye `false` iji gbanyụụ onye nhazi oge ahụ ike proxy n'azụ nke na-enyocha proxy ndị e debanyere aha oge oge.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_INTERVAL_MS`                        | `600000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Oge nkwụsị nke onye nhazi oge ahụ ike n'azụ na ms (nke kacha nta bụ `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RELAY_INTERVAL_MS`                  | `21600000`                | `src/lib/proxyHealth/relayCadence.ts`                                                     | Oge nlele na ms maka proxies edge-relay (`deno`/`vercel`/`cloudflare`), nke nlele ha na-akpọte serverless isolate a na-akwụ ụgwọ (kacha nta `60000`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_RECOVERY_INTERVAL_MS`               | `600000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Oge recovery-pass na ndabere na ms: ugboro ole a na-emepụtara proxies nke adịghị ike na mbụ nlele ọzọ ka proxy nke eweghachiri laghachi na rotation na-enweghị ịmalitegharị. Ụgwọ dị n'okpuru `60000` na-alaghachi na ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL`                           | `https://httpbin.org/ip`  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                      | Ebumnuche nlele iru eru nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-eji. Kụzie ya na URL nke ime ụlọ/nke onwe iji zere ndabara ọha.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_CONCURRENCY`                   | `10`                      | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                      | Ọnụ ọgụgụ nlele e bidoro n'otu oge na batch ọ bụla, nke scheduler na endpoint `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test` na-ekekọrịta. O dị ala na 1 ma o kacha elu 50.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_STAGGER_MS`                    | `100`                     | `src/lib/proxyHealth/probeTarget.ts`                                                      | Nkwụsịtụ na ms n'etiti mmalite nlele abụọ n'ime batch. N'enweghị ya, batch niile na-apụ n'otu oge, egress IP a na-ekekọrịta nwekwara ike ịkpasu ike target nke a machibidoro oke (rate-limited). Tọwanye ya na `0` iji gbanyụ oge oge ahụ; o kacha elu 5000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_USE_PROVIDER_TARGET`                | `true`                    | `src/lib/proxyHealth/providerProbeTarget.ts`                                              | Tọwanye "false" iji kwụsị ịlele ezigbo ọbịa nke provider a họpụtara maka proxy (`GET /models`, enweghị igodo API) wee jiri `PROXY_HEALTH_TEST_URL` mgbe niile.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `PROXY_HEALTH_AUTO_DEACTIVATE`                    | `false`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/statusPolicy.ts`                                                     | Mgbe ọ bụ `false` (ndabara), nlele iru eru akpaghị aka (scheduler + bọtịnụ "Test All" nke `/api/settings/proxies/auto-test`) bụ **na-agụ naanị (read-only)** ma ọ bụghị ide status proxy — naanị onye na-arụ ọrụ ka na-ahọrọ active/inactive, ka nlele na-adịghị kwụsie ike ghara ịhapụ proxy a họpụtara (#6246). Tọwanye `true` iji weghachite omume ochie nke test-and-set.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `FLUSH_EMPTY_RETRY_ENABLED`                       | `false`                   | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Okooko atụmatụ nnabata n'onwe (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); dashboard DB override na-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-emegharị streaming turns efu a tụgharịrị site na ụzọ credential nkịtị (ruo n'ọnụ ọgụgụ `STREAM_RECOVERY.EMPTY_TURN_RETRY_MAX` nke mmegharị) kama igosi 200 efu ma ọ bụ 502 nke ọdịnaya efu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `EMPTY_STREAM_SIBLING_RECOVERY_ENABLED`           | `false`                   | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Okooko atụmatụ nnabata n'onwe (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); dashboard DB override na-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-akwado nlele ọdịnaya stream efu nke Claude/Codex ma na-eji retry akpaala oke dị ugbu a na njikọ ọzọ tozuru oke mgbe `empty_content` turn gasịrị, na-enweghị cooldown ma ọ bụ ịgọchi model.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_LATE_OUTPUT_PROBE_ENABLED`                | `false`                   | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Okooko atụmatụ nnabata n'onwe (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); dashboard DB override na-emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-aga n'ihu na-ahọrọ otu chunk a zigara n'ime 16 mgbe 64 mbụ gasịrị ka mmepụta a hụrụ n'anya na-adị n'ihu na-akọ stream dị ka ọ nwepụtara mmepụta (5xx naanị mgbe mmepụta gasịrị ka na-anọ na request-scoped kama ịbụ model-only lockout).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `PROXY_POOL_EGRESS_OBSERVATION`                   | `false`                   | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Okọlọtọ atụmatụ nke nhọrọ (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ndakwasị DB nke dashboard ka emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-egosi nchọpụta egress pool dị naanị maka ịgụ n'okpuru pool proksi na dashboard (IP egress dị iche iche, njikọ, na ọnụ ọgụgụ kachasị ukwuu a hụrụ n'azụ otu IP n'ime awa 24 gara aga, sitere na ndekọ proksi). A naghị eji ya eme ihe maka ịhazi ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_ENABLED`                   | `false`                   | `src/shared/utils/featureFlags.ts`                                                        | Okọlọtọ atụmatụ nke nhọrọ (lee [FEATURE_FLAGS.md](./FEATURE_FLAGS.md); ndakwasị DB nke dashboard ka emeri). `true` (ma ọ bụ `1`, `yes`) na-anabata adreesị ndị a hụrụ nke nwere ụbọchị nke onye ọrụ ziputara maka onye ọ bụla nke pool ma jikọta ha na ịgụ akwụkwọ ndekọ (journal) maka ngosi na usoro pool.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `PROXY_OPERATOR_EGRESS_WINDOW_HOURS`              | `24`                      | `src/lib/db/proxyOperatorEgress.ts`                                                       | Oge nchọpụta n'awa maka ahịrị egress nke onye ọrụ ziputara (a na-agụ ya na ọgụgụ ọ bụla; achọghị ịmalitegharị). O nwere ókè 1..168; ọ bụrụ na ọ gafere ókè, ọ bụghị nke ọnụ ọgụgụ, ma ọ bụ oghere, ọ na-adabere azụ na 24 na otu ịdọ aka ná ntị nke usoro dum, ọ dịghị mgbe ọ na-atụpụ mmejọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`                               | `false`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Tọọ `true` ka onye nhazi oge wee wepụ proksi na-akpaghị aka mgbe ọdịda otu n'otu ugboro ugboro gasịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`                         | `3`                       | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Ọdịda otu n'otu tupu onye nhazi oge ewepụ proksi na-akpaghị aka (mgbe `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_AUTO_DISABLE`                              | `false`                   | `src/lib/proxyHealth/scheduler.ts`                                                        | Tọọ `true` ka onye nhazi oge wee gbanyụọ proksi n'ụzọ dị nro (ọkwa `dead`, a naghị ehichapụ ya) mgbe ọdịda otu n'otu ugboro ugboro gasịrị, kama iwepụ ya. Nhọrọ na-ebighị mbibi nke `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE`: proksi na-apụ ngwa ngwa na ngwọta pool/rotation (ihe nzacha ọkwa ndụ nke ngwọta scope-pool na-eji apụtaghala ya) ma a na-akpaliri ya ọzọ na-akpaghị aka ozugbo ọ malitere ịgafe nleba anya ọzọ. Ọ na-ekekọrịta ókè `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE_AFTER`. Ọ bụrụ na okọlọtọ abụọ ahụ bụ `true`, `PROXY_AUTO_REMOVE` ka emeri.                                                                                                            |

Ọ na-agbanyụọ n'oge ndabara: a na-enyocha proksi ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `PROXY_PASSIVE_WINDOW_MS`                         | `300000`                  | `src/lib/proxyHealth/passiveVerdict.ts`                                                   | Windo na ms nke traffic mmepụta ihe nke ịgafepụ na-anọdụ ala na-ele (nwere ókè `60000..599999`). Ọ dị mkpirikpi karịa oge nhicha niile nke nkeji iri ka ọdịda nke na-adị nso nso a wee nwee isi, kama ka a belata ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_CONTROL_PLANE_PROXY_DIRECT_FALLBACK`   | `false`                   | `src/shared/constants/featureFlagDefinitions.ts`                                          | Kwe ka usoro OAuth na nkwenye onye na-enye ihe gafee proksi kpịrịrị wee jikọọ ozugbo mgbe nlele mbụ ịrụ ụgwụ proksi dara. Usoro ọchịchị dị irè bụ ndakwasị DB nke Feature Flags > mgbanwe gburugburu ebe > ndabara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_WAIT_MS`                          | `30000` (30s)             | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts`                                                          | Ego oge **queue-wait** ndabara: oge arịrịọ nwere ike ichere oghere provider ma nọrọ n'ahịrị (queue) tupu a jụọ ya, na-erughị upstream. A na-akpochapụ timer ahụ ozugbo ọrụ ahụ malite ịrụ — a na-akpụ ịrụ ọrụ n'oke iche site na `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`. Env na-edebe naanị ndabara: `resilienceSettings.requestQueue.maxWaitMs` echekwara, na mgbe ahụ `rateLimitOverrides.maxWaitMs` nke per-connection, nke ọ bụla na-emeri ya.                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `RATE_LIMIT_EXECUTION_MAX_WAIT_MS`                | `600000` (10 min)         | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                   | Oke elu maka oge arịrịọ nabatara nwere ike ịnọ n'ịrụ ọrụ tupu ndebe rate-limit ya agwụ — ekewapụrụ ya na ego queue-wait ka mmalite fetch na-adị arọ na gateways na-abụghị nke incremental ghara ịgwụ oge (time out) (#12027).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `RATE_LIMIT_MAX_QUEUE_DEPTH`                      | `0` (disabled)            | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                   | Oke nnabata queue: jụ na 429 `queue_full` ozugbo ọnụ ọgụgụ arịrịọ a nọlarị n'ahịrị. `0` = enweghị oke (ndabara).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `RATE_LIMIT_AUTO_ENABLE`                          | _(unset)_                 | `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`                                                   | Manye ngwa nchekwa rate-limit auto-enable on/off n'agbanyeghị ntọala Dashboard echekwara (`resilienceSettings.requestQueue.autoEnableApiKeyProviders`). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji manye on, `false`/`0`/`off` iji manye off. Mgbe ọ na-arụ ọrụ (ndabara), a na-echekwa njikọ API-key na-arụ ọrụ na rate limit na-akpaghị aka. Ịdebe `rateLimitProtection: false` per-connection na-agbupụ nchekwa kpọmkwem ma ọ bụghị ịpụ na ngwa nchekwa a; ịdebe env var a na `false` bụ usoro zuru ụwa ọnụ iji rụọ njikọ API-key na-enweghị rate-limit queues.                                                                              |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`                       | _(unset → off)_           | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                            | Nleba cooldown provider/connection n'ofe arịrịọ zuru ụwa ọnụ nke bụ opt-in. OFF dabara adaba (na-akpọkọta Connection Cooldown / Provider Circuit Breaker). Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on` iji mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MIN_MS`                        | `5000`                    | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                            | Cooldown kacha nta (ms) tupu a nwaghachie provider/connection dara ada. Ọ na-abawanye n'ụzọ exponential ka ụdị dara ada na-esote ibe ya na-amụba. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_MAX_MS`                        | `300000` (5 min)          | `open-sse/services/providerCooldownTracker.ts`                                            | Oke cooldown kacha elu (ms) tupu a nwaghachie provider/connection dara ada n'agbanyeghị ihe ọ bụla. A na-eji ya naanị mgbe `PROVIDER_COOLDOWN_ENABLED`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED`                         | _(unset → off)_           | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (seed) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (logic)   | **Gịnị ọ bụ:** mweghachi doro anya nke streams upstream egbupụrụ n'etiti (free-claude-code port). Ọ na-ejide windo SSE mmalite ruo `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS` (750 ms) ka a megharịe ma nwaghachie ịgbụsị _pre-commit_ — nke na-eme tupu byte ọ bụla eruo client — n'enweghị ka a hụ ya. **Mgbe ị ga-emee ya:** upstreams na-adịghị kwụsi ike nke na-ebupụ 0-byte ugboro ugboro na mmalite stream; hapụ ya OFF ọ bụrụ na ị nweghị ike ibu ruo 750 ms mgbakwunyere na time-to-first-token na stream ọ bụla. Ọ na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`. Ọ na-akụ mkpụrụ ntọala Resilience echekwara; ntọala Dashboard na-emeri ozugbo edebere ya. |
| `STREAM_RECOVERY_MIDSTREAM_ENABLED`               | _(ahazibeghị → gbanyụrụ)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` (mkpụrụ) → `open-sse/services/streamRecovery.ts` (lojik) | **Gịnị:** ịga n'ihu n'etiti iyi (Fase 4.4) — mgbe ịchụsịbe _post-commit_ gasịrị (bytes eruola n'aka onye ahịa), rịọ ọzọ site na ederede akụkụ ahụ dị ka assistant prefill ma jikọta suffix na-efu. Naanị iyi ederede dị larịị dakọrọ na OpenAI; anaghị amalite mgbe oku ngwá ọrụ ka na-efe efe. **Mgbe ị ga-akwalite ya:** mmepụta ogologo ndị na-ekubi n'etiti azịza ma ị nabatara na ọdụ eweghachitere ga-abata n'otu ibu kama token-site-token. Nweere onwe ya site na `STREAM_RECOVERY_ENABLED` (profaịlụ ihe egwu dị iche). Na-anabata `true`/`1`/`on`.                                                                          |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`              | _(ahazibeghị → gbanyụrụ)_ | `src/lib/resilience/settings.ts` → `open-sse/services/throughputWatchdog.ts`              | Onye nche mmepụta bara uru nke iyi na-arụ ọrụ, nke a na-ahọrọ itinye aka. Na-achọpụta iyi ndị na-aga n'ihu iziga chunks ma ka na-anọ n'okpuru ọnụego mmepụta assistant ahaziri; heartbeats, ihe omume ojiji, deltas efu, na usoro ngwá ọrụ/ịchẹ echiche anaghị agụnye dị ka ọganihu. Dị iche na oge ụgwụ nke ihe na-adịghị arụ ọrụ na nke oge ịkacha emecha siri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WARMUP_MS`            | `30000`                   | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Oge amara tupu ntụlele nnyefe, oke ya bụ 0–600000 ms.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_WINDOW_MS`            | `30000`                   | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Window mmepụta bara uru na-akpali, oke ya bụ 1000–600000 ms; a chọrọ otu window zuru ezu tupu ịkwụsị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_BYTES_PER_SECOND` | `4`                       | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Ọnụego byte mmepụta assistant UTF-8 kacha nta (nnọchi anya token nchebe), oke ya bụ 1–1000000.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `STREAM_THROUGHPUT_WATCHDOG_MIN_USEFUL_BYTES`     | `1`                       | `src/lib/resilience/settings/normalize.ts`                                                | Nlereanya mmepụta bara uru na-abụghị efu kacha nta nke a na-ewere dị ka ihe a ga-atụle, oke ya bụ 1–1000000 bytes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS`                          | `3000`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Oge nkwụsịtụ (ms) n'etiti nlele ahụike token nke onye na-enye na mmalite.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MIN_MS`                       | `500`                     | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Jitter randomized kacha nta (ms) agbakwunyere n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nlele ahụike token nke onye na-enye, iji gbochie ịgbaji (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `HEALTHCHECK_JITTER_MAX_MS`                       | `5000`                    | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Jitter randomized kacha ukwu (ms) agbakwunyere n'elu `HEALTHCHECK_STAGGER_MS` n'etiti nlele ahụike token nke onye na-enye, iji gbochie ịgbaji (Issue #1220).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `HEALTHCHECK_BATCH_SIZE`                          | `20`                      | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`                                                             | Nha otu nke nlele otu oge maka sweep nlele ahụike token mmalite; ụkpụrụ buru ibu na-enyocha njikọ ndị ọzọ n'otu oge, ụkpụrụ obere na-ebelata ibu ịgbaji (Issue #7875, regression nke #7719).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `REQUEST_RETRY`                                   | `2`                       | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                  | Ọnụ ọgụgụ ịgbalị ọzọ akpaghị aka na nzaghachi cooldown metụtara model tupu ịweghachi njehie nye onye ahịa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `MAX_RETRY_INTERVAL_SEC`                          | `30`                      | `src/sse/services/cooldownAwareRetry.ts`                                                  | Oge ezighi ezi kacha elu (sekọnd) dị n'etiti mbọ nkwagharị cooldown. Uru a na-egbochi ya n'agbanyeghị `Retry-After` si n'aka upstream.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `HEADROOM_URL`                                    | `http://localhost:8787`   | `src/lib/headroom/detect.ts`                                                              | URL nke nnọchi proxy nchekwa-token Headroom. Usoro ndụ dashboard (`api/headroom/*`) na-amalite `headroom-ai` CLI obodo na loopback dabere na ndabara; jiri override ya naanị iji chọpụta nnọchi sidecar Docker si n'èzí.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Ọnụọgụ nhazi maka mweghachi iyi (stream-recovery)

A na-eji constants e hibere n'ime koodu n'ime
`open-sse/config/constants.ts` (`STREAM_RECOVERY`) mee omume njide (holdback) nke mweghachi nhazi, e gosipụtara ha ebe a dị ka ntụaka —
ịgbanwe ha chọrọ ndezi koodu, ọ bụghị env var:

- `STREAM_RECOVERY.HOLDBACK_MS = 750` — ogologo oge e ji jide windo SSE mmeghe
  ka a nwee ike ịkwagharị mbelata mmalite (early truncation) tupu e nyefee byte ọ bụla n'aka onye ahịa (client).
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.BUFFER_MAX_BYTES = 65536` — oke siri ike n'elu windo a na-ejide; nyefee
  (flush + passthrough) ozugbo ọnụọgụ bytes a chịkọtara, n'agbanyeghị oge (timer).
- `STREAM_RECOVERY.EARLY_RETRY_MAX = 4` — ọnụọgụ kacha elu nke imeghari imeghe iyi upstream
  n'uzo a na-adịghị ahụ anya ka njide (holdback) ka bụ nke a na-enyebeghị (uncommitted).

> **Ọnụ oke sliding-window nke onye na-enye ọ bụla (enweghị env var):** ihe _mgbada_ nke
> ọnụ oke sliding-window nke onye na-enye ọ bụla si FCC bịara dị na koodu
> (`open-sse/services/providerDefaultRateLimit.ts`, jikọrọ site na
> `open-sse/services/rateLimitManager.ts`) mana ọ na-abịa na **maapụ ndabara dị efu**
> ma nwee **enweghị env var onye ọrụ** taa — a na-arụ ya ọrụ naanị site na ihe nnwale (test hook) /
> ndezi koodu. E bu n'obi ka ọ ghara ịnọ na tebụl dị n'elu. Ihe na-achịkwa ọsọ relay
> kwa-`(token, IP)` nke _nwere_ bọtịnụ nhazi bụ `RELAY_IP_PER_MINUTE` (§3 Network & Ports).

---

## 22. Nchọpụta na ndozi nsogbu

> [!CAUTION]
> Ngbanwe ndị a na-emepụta **mmepụta zuru ezu** ma nwee ike ikpughe data dị nzuzo. **Agbanyela ha mgbe ọ bụla na gburugburu mmepụta.**

| Ngbanwe                               | Ndabara                                    | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                                                     | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CURSOR_DEBUG`                        | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Tọọ `1` iji gbanye ndekọ zuru ezu nke Cursor executor (iberibe SSE a tụgharịrị, wdg.).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG`                 | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Aha ọzọ nke `CURSOR_DEBUG` nke dakọtara na nsụgharị ochie.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `CURSOR_DUMP_FILE`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ụzọ faịlụ nhọrọ nke na-anata iberibe Cursor raw a tụgharịrị mgbe `CURSOR_DEBUG=1`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_STREAM_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge njedebe nke stream mgbe ọ naghị arụ ọrụ (ms) maka Cursor executor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CURSOR_KV_GRACE_MS`                  | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Oge mgbaghara (ms) mgbe composer kv_after_text soft terminator gasịrị ma ọ bụrụ na byte ka dị na buffer — nke a na-enye oku ngwaọrụ exec_mcp nke na-esote oge iji mezue frame ya.                                                                                                                                             |
| `CURSOR_TOOL_DIRECTIVE`               | agbanyere (`!== "0"`)                      | `open-sse/executors/cursor.ts`                                                                        | Ntuziaka tool-commit nke na-eme ka composer-2.5 kpọọ ngwaọrụ n'ụzọ a pụrụ ịdabere na ya. Tọọ `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_SYSTEM_INSTRUCTION_APPEND` | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/translator/request/claude-to-openai.ts`, `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts` | Ederede system prompt nke onye ọrụ sistemụ kọwara, nke a na-agbakwunye na system message MGBE ntụgharị gasịrị (ntinye mgbe ntụgharị gasịrị), ma rute n'ụzọ codex/Responses na `/v1/messages`. A na-ejikwa ya dị ka mbido ntuziaka a na-ewepụ na ngọngọ system preamble ndị e weghachiri. Hapụ ya ka edoghị ya iji gbanyụọ ya. |
| `OMNIROUTE_STRIP_SYSTEM_PREAMBLE`     | `0` (agbanyụrụ)                            | `open-sse/translator/response/openai-to-claude.ts`, `open-sse/utils/directivePreambleStripper.ts`     | Tọọ `1` iji wepụ ngọngọ system-prompt preamble ndị e weghachiri site na mmalite nke stream openai→claude. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara — usoro nyocha ndị ahụ dabere n'ụdị ederede Bekee ma na-agbanwe payload nzaghachi, ya mere nzaghachi nke malitere n'ụzọ ziri ezi site na ngalaba dị otu ahụ ga-efunahụ ya.                  |
| `CURSOR_IMAGE_FETCH_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `15000`                                    | `open-sse/utils/cursorImages.ts`                                                                      | Oge njedebe nbudata maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ntinye ọhụhụ `image_url` dị n'ime ime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CURSOR_STATE_DB_PATH`                | _(a na-enyocha)_                           | `open-sse/utils/cursorVersionDetector.ts`                                                             | Dochie ọchụchọ Cursor IDE state DB a na-eji achọpụta nsụgharị IDE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_CLI_VERSION`            | _(chọpụta / kpọgide)_                      | `open-sse/utils/cursorAgentCliVersion.ts`                                                             | NJ build nke Agent CLI (`YYYY.MM.DD-<hash>`) maka `x-cursor-client-version: cli-…` na Agent Run.                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `CURSOR_AGENT_BIN`                    | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ụzọ gaa na Cursor Agent binary a na-eji emepụta onyonyo. Mgbe edoghị ya, handler na-eji `providerSpecificData.agentBin`, mesịa PATH.                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CURSOR_IMG_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `210000`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka onyonyo ọ bụla (ms) maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor Agent.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MAX_CONCURRENT`           | `2`                                        | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ụzọ njikwa ọrụ na-aga n'otu oge n'oche a na-ekekọrịta maka ọrụ onyonyo Cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CURSOR_IMG_MODEL`                    | arịrịọ / `auto`                            | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/cursorAgentImage.ts`                                     | Dochie `--model` nke Cursor CLI maka ọrụ onyonyo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `2000`                                     | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Ugboro nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `UC_IMAGE_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `60000`                                    | `open-sse/handlers/imageGeneration/providers/ucImage.ts`                                              | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka njedebe nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta onyonyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_INTERVAL_MS`           | `3000`                                     | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Ugboro nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (uncensored.com) (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `UC_VIDEO_POLL_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `300000`                                   | `open-sse/handlers/videoGeneration/providers/ucVideo.ts`                                              | Oge elekere zuru ezu maka njedebe nyocha nsonaazụ mmepụta vidiyo UC (ms).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |

| `OMNIROUTE_LOG_REQUEST_SHAPE`         | agbanyụrụ (a ga-ahọrọ iji `"1"` gbanye ya) | `src/app/api/v1/chat/completions/route.ts`                                                            | Dekọọ akara content-type/length maka payload nkata buru ibu mgbe atọrọ `"1"`. Agbanyụrụ ya na ndabara iji belata mkpọtụ ndekọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `DEBUG_RESPONSES_SSE_TO_JSON`         | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji dekọọ nkọwa ntụgharị Responses API SSE→JSON.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `DEBUG_CLAUDE_NONSTREAM`              | _(edoghị ya)_                              | `open-sse/handlers/responseTranslator.ts`                                                             | Tọọ `true` iji gosipụta iberibe textContent efu n'ụzọ ntụgharị nzaghachi Claude (naanị maka nchọpụta nsogbu).                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `NEXT_PUBLIC_OMNIROUTE_E2E_MODE`      | _(edoghị ya)_                              | Ngwa nnwale E2E                                                                                       | Tọọ `true` iji gbanye ọnọdụ nnwale E2E (nkwenye e mere ka ọ dị mfe, njikọ nnwale).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |

## 23. Njikọta GitHub

Kwe ka ndị ọrụ nwee ike ịkọ nsogbu ozugbo site na Dashboard.

| Ihe Mgbanwe           | Ndabara      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------- | ------------ | ------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_REPO`  | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | Ebe nchekwa n'ụdị `owner/repo`.                                                                                                                    |
| `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN` | _(edobeghị)_ | `src/app/api/v1/issues/report/route.ts`           | GitHub Personal Access Token nwere ikike `issues:write`.                                                                                           |
| `GITHUB_TOKEN`        | _(edobeghị)_ | nhazi mbido nke nsogbu / ihe enyemaka cloud agent | Token nnweta GitHub izugbe a na-eji dị ka ndabere maka `GITHUB_ISSUES_TOKEN`, nke ihe enyemaka cloud agent dị na `src/lib/cloudAgent/*` na-ejikwa. |

---

## Ọnọdụ Mbuga

Maka ntuziaka SRE nke backend relay (omume ts/bifrost/auto, ebe a ga-edowe 9router ma e jiri ya tụnyere CLIProxyAPI, na atụmatụ ndabere maka oke ọsọ mbufe), lee [Atụmatụ Backend Relay](/docs/reference/RELAY_BACKEND_STRATEGY.md).

### Mmepe Obodo Dịkarịsịrị Mfe

```bash
JWT_SECRET=$(openssl rand -base64 48)
API_KEY_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)
INITIAL_PASSWORD=dev123
PORT=20128
NODE_ENV=development
```

### Mmepụta Docker

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
INITIAL_PASSWORD=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
DATA_DIR=/data
PORT=20128
API_PORT=20129
NODE_ENV=production
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://localhost:20128
OMNIROUTE_MEMORY_MB=8192
CORS_ORIGIN=https://your-frontend.example.com
```

### Enweghị Njikọ Ịntanetị / CI

```bash
JWT_SECRET=test-jwt-secret-for-ci
API_KEY_SECRET=test-api-key-secret-for-ci
INITIAL_PASSWORD=testpass
NODE_ENV=production
OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_BACKGROUND_SERVICES=true
APP_LOG_TO_FILE=false
```

### VPS nwere Proxy Ntụgharị (nginx + Cloudflare)

```bash
JWT_SECRET=<emepụtara>
API_KEY_SECRET=<emepụtara>
STORAGE_ENCRYPTION_KEY=<emepụtara>
PORT=20128
AUTH_COOKIE_SECURE=true
REQUIRE_API_KEY=true
NEXT_PUBLIC_BASE_URL=https://omniroute.example.com
BASE_URL=http://127.0.0.1:20128
CORS_ORIGIN=https://omniroute.example.com
ENABLE_TLS_FINGERPRINT=true
CLI_COMPAT_ALL=1
```

---

## 24. Ebe Nnwale Nchekwa Skills (v3.8.0+)

Oke na ntọala nchekwa ndị a na-etinye mgbe framework Skills (`src/lib/skills/`) na-eme automation ndị ọrụ kọwara n'ime gburugburu e kewapụrụ maka nnwale.

| Ihe Mgbanwe                       | Ndabara                                               | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| --------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------ | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `10000` (10 s)                                        | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke oge wall-clock maka oge mmejuputa nke ọ bụla nke koodu skill dị na sandbox. Nke a bụ oke siri ike; a ga-akwụsị ihe ọ bụla gafere ya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_MS`     | _(na-eji `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS`` dị ka ndabere)_ | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Oke oge maka nhazi mmejuputa skill n'ogo dị elu. Tọọ ya karịa `SKILLS_SANDBOX_TIMEOUT_MS` iji kwe ka usoro ọrụ nwere ọtụtụ nzọụkwụ rụọ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `SKILLS_MAX_FILE_BYTES`           | `1048576` (1 MB)                                      | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị skill nwere ike ịgụ site na otu faịlụ ọ bụla dị na sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `SKILLS_MAX_HTTP_RESPONSE_BYTES`  | `256000` (250 KB)                                     | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ọnụ byte kachasị a ga-edekọ site na otu nzaghachi HTTP ọ bụla n'ime skill.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `SKILLS_MAX_SANDBOX_OUTPUT_CHARS` | `100000`                                              | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Oke siri ike nke mkpụrụedemede stdout/stderr a na-eweghachi site na otu ọkpụkpọ sandbox.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED`  | `false`                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Tọọ `1`/`true` iji kwe ka njikọ netwọk na-apụ apụ site n'ime sandbox. Ndabara ya bụ **nkewapụrụ** maka nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `SKILLS_ALLOWED_SANDBOX_IMAGES`   | _(efu)_                                               | `src/lib/skills/builtins.ts`                                       | Ndepụta onyonyo container ndị e kwere maka mmejuputa sandbox, nke rikoma kewara. Ọ bụrụ na ọ tọgbọ chakoo, ọ pụtara na ọ bụ naanị ndabara arụnyere n'ime ka a ga-eji.                                                                                                                                                         |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_DOCKER_IMAGE`     | _(ndabara arụnyere n'ime)_                            | `src/lib/skills/`                                                  | Onyonyo container a na-eji mgbe a na-emepụta sandbox Docker na-akwado. Gbanwee ya iji kpọchie onyonyo ntọala omenala e wusiri ike.                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `SKILLS_SANDBOX_RUNTIME`          | `auto`                                                | `src/lib/skills/sandbox.ts`, `src/lib/skills/containerProvider.ts` | Runtime container maka idobe skill na sandbox: `auto` \| `docker` \| `apple` \| `wsl` \| `orbstack` \| `podman`. `auto` na-ahọrọ runtime arụnyere kacha mma dabere na OS nke host (Apple Container/OrbStack na macOS, WSL Container na Windows, Podman na Linux), ma na-eji Docker dị ka ndabere ma ọ bụrụ na ndị ahụ adịghị. |

> [!CAUTION]
> Ime ka `SKILLS_SANDBOX_NETWORK_ENABLED=true` rụọ ọrụ na-emepe ụzọ egress site na koodu skill ọ bụla. Jikọtara ya na `OUTBOUND_SSRF_GUARD_ENABLED=true` yana amụma `CORS_ORIGIN`/proxy siri ike n'ebe mbugote ọtụtụ ndị na-eji ọnụ.
