## 25. Oke Provider, Ọgba, Nchekwa Ndabere & Ihe Ndị Ọzọ Runtime

Njedebe oke provider, ọgba netwọk (Tailscale, Ngrok, MITM debug proxy), pool ọpụpụ 1Proxy, nchekwa ndabere database na obere mgbanwe kpọmkwem maka atụmatụ ọ bụla nke oyi executor ma ọ bụ script na-akpọ.

| Mgbanwe                                        | Ndabere                                                                     | Faịlụ Isi                                                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| ---------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_URL`                                    | `redis://localhost:6379`                                                    | `src/shared/utils/rateLimiter.ts`                                         | Eriri njikọ Redis maka backend rate limiter.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_HOST`                     | _(host mmepụta)_                                                            | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Dochie host a na-eji ịweta quota coding-plan Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ALIBABA_CODING_PLAN_QUOTA_URL`                | ewetara site na host                                                        | `open-sse/services/bailianQuotaFetcher.ts`                                | Ndochi URL quota zuru ezu maka Alibaba Bailian.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_COOKIE`                            | _(ahazighị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Cookie session console maka gateway quota Token Plan nkeonwe Qwen Cloud / Model Studio (API key inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo isi arịrịọ `Cookie` dum — ọ nwere `login_qwencloud_ticket` — site na oku `api.json` ọ bụla na `cs-data.qwencloud.com` na home.qwencloud.com › Billing › Subscription (F12 › Network). Ihe nzuzo ma nwere oke session; họrọ ubi `qwenCloudCookie` Dashboard maka njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_CLOUD_SEC_TOKEN`                         | _(ahazighị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Ndochi `sec_token` nke aka maka gateway console Token Plan. Ihe nzuzo; mgbe a na-ahazighị ya, ihe oweta na-edozi ya site na HTML dashboard site na iji cookie ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_HOST`                         | `https://cs-data.qwencloud.com`                                             | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | Ndochi host gateway maka ihe oweta quota Token Plan nkeonwe (dịka `bailian-singapore-cs.alibabacloud.com` maka console Model Studio).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `QWEN_TOKEN_PLAN_DASHBOARD_URL`                | `https://home.qwencloud.com/`                                               | `open-sse/services/qwenTokenPlanQuotaFetcher.ts`                          | URL Dashboard a na-eji edozi `sec_token` site na HTML e banyere.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_CONSOLE_COOKIE`                   | _(ahazighị)_                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Cookie session console maka API quota Token Plan Xiaomi MiMo (`platform.xiaomimimo.com/api/v1/tokenPlan/usage` + `tokenPlan/detail` — API key inference enweghị ike ịgụ ya). Detuo isi arịrịọ `Cookie` dum — ọ nwere `api-platform_serviceToken` na `userId` — site na arịrịọ ọ bụla na `platform.xiaomimimo.com` na ibe Token Plan (F12 › Network). Ihe nzuzo ma nwere oke session; họrọ ubi `xiaomiMimoConsoleCookie` Dashboard maka njikọ ọ bụla.                                                                                         |
| `XIAOMI_MIMO_MONTHLY_TOKEN_LIMIT`              | `4100000000`                                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/xiaomi-mimo.ts`                                  | Mmefu ego kwa ọnwa (Credits) maka ihe mgbazuge Xiaomi MiMo nke OmniRoute na-esochi, nke e ji arụ ọrụ mgbe a debeghị kuki console ọ bụla. Nke mbụ ya bụ 4.1B nke Lite tier; debe ya na Credits nke atụmatụ ahụ (Standard 11B / Pro 38B / Max 82B). Nkwalite `providerSpecificData.monthlyTokenLimit` nke njikọ ọ bụla na-meri env var a.                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_VISION_FE_PATH`             | `/costing-balance/free-quota-image-video`                                   | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Nkwalite ụzọ console front-end maka iweta oke vision/media nke Alibaba Model Studio free-tier.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_MULTIMODAL_FE_PATH`         | `/costing-balance/free-quota-multimodal`                                    | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Nkwalite ụzọ console front-end maka iweta oke multimodal nke Alibaba Model Studio free-tier.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_AUDIO_FE_PATH`              | `/costing-balance/free-quota-audio`                                         | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierQuotaFetcher.ts`                        | Nkwalite ụzọ console front-end maka iweta oke ụda nke Alibaba Model Studio free-tier.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `ALIBABA_FREE_TIER_ALLOWLIST_PATH`             | _(a debeghị)_                                                               | `open-sse/services/alibabaFreeTierAllowlist.ts`                           | Ụzọ nhọrọ gaa na nkwalite JSON mpaghara maka ndepụta anabatara nke ụdị ederede Alibaba free-tier nke e wuru n'ime. Ọ na-adabere na `$DATA_DIR/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`, mgbe ahụ `config/alibaba-free-tier-allowlist.json`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CONTEXT_RESERVE_TOKENS`                       | `1024`                                                                      | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Token ndị e debere maka mmepụta mmechi mgbe a na-agbakọ mmefu prompt.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CONTEXT_KEEP_LATEST_IMAGES`                   | `2`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/contextManager.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ọgụgụ onyinyo inline kacha ọhụrụ a ga-edebe mgbe a na-ewepụ ndị ochie ka ha dabere n'ime windo context (#8560).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `MODEL_ALIAS_COMPAT_ENABLED`                   | na-arụ ọrụ                                                                  | `open-sse/services/model.ts`                                              | Mee ka ọ rụọ ọrụ ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ oyi nkwadobaye alias ụdị ochie nke ndị client ochie na-eji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_EMERGENCY_FALLBACK`                 | na-arụ ọrụ                                                                  | `open-sse/services/emergencyFallback.ts`                                  | Debe `false` (ma ọ bụ `0`) iji gbanyụ ihe mgbazuge mberede maka mmecha mmefu ego nke na-ekpegharị arịrị dara ada gaa na ụdị efu `nvidia`/`openai/gpt-oss-120b`. Usoro nke dị irè bụ nkwalite Feature Flags DB > env var > nke mbụ; ọ bụrụ na ọ dịghị, ọrụ ahụ na-adabere na uru env ojii.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `COMMAND_CODE_CALLBACK_PORT`                   | _(a debeghị)_                                                               | `src/app/api/providers/command-code/auth/shared.ts`                       | Port mpaghara e ji eme callbacks ụdị OAuth site na ihe nyocha Command Code CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `COMMAND_CODE_VERSION`                         | `0.33.2`                                                                    | `open-sse/executors/commandCode.ts`                                       | Uru a na-eziga dị ka `x-command-code-version` header nye Command Code upstream. Kagbuo ya iji bulie ụdị CLI.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `COMMANDCODE_API_URL`                          | `https://api.commandcode.ai`                                                | `open-sse/services/usage/command-code.ts`                                 | URL ntọala maka Command Code usage/quota upstream nke telemetry nke smartphone quota-fetcher na-eji. Kagbuo ya maka Command Code API nke onwe onye na-akwado ma ọ bụ ọzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |

A tọghị ya → 503-with-fallback. A na-ewepụ slash ikpeazụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `BIFROST_PORT`                                 | `8080`                                                                      | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                           | Port nke ọrụ Bifrost etinyere n'ime nke a na-elekọta na-ejikọta (`127.0.0.1:<port>`) mgbe OmniRoute na-elekọta ndụ Bifrost sidecar. Ndabara bụ `8080`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `LLMLINGUA_PORT`                               | `20135`                                                                     | `src/lib/services/bootstrap.ts`                                           | Port nke ọrụ mkpakọ LLMLingua a na-elekọta na-ejikọta (`127.0.0.1:<port>`). Ndabara bụ `20135`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `LLMLINGUA_BASE_URL`                           | `http://127.0.0.1:20135`                                                    | `open-sse/services/compression/engines/llmlingua/index.ts`                | Base URL maka daemon microservice mkpakọ HTTP LLMLingua obodo. Ndabara bụ `http://127.0.0.1:20135`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `LLMLINGUA_MODEL`                              | `atjsh/llmlingua-2-js-tinybert-meetingbank`                                 | `src/lib/services/installers/llmlingua.ts`                                | Njirimara model Hugging Face nke sidecar LLMLingua a na-elekọta na-ebulo (a na-enye ya env usoro nwa).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BIFROST_API_KEY`                              | _(a tọghị ya)_                                                              | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Igodo API maka Bifrost gateway (a na-eziga ya dị ka `Authorization: Bearer ...`). Ọ bụrụ na a tọghị ya, route ahụ na-atụ anya na arịrị ahụ na-ebu igodo OmniRoute API ziri ezi; igodo a bụ maka nyocha ikike n'akụkụ gateway naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `BIFROST_STREAMING_ENABLED`                    | `true`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Mgbe ọ bụ eziokwu, ụzọ Bifrost sidecar na-asọpụta nzaghachi laghachi site na SSE gafere ọnụ ụzọ (gateway) kama onye na-ahụ maka streaming TS. Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `0` iji manye nzaghachi JSON na-abụghị nke a na-asọpụta gafere ọnụ ụzọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `BIFROST_TIMEOUT_MS`                           | `30000`                                                                     | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Oge ngwụsị maka arịrịọ ọ bụla mgbe a na-eziga ozi gaa ọnụ ụzọ Bifrost (ms). Mgbe oge gwụrụ, ụzọ ahụ na-alọghachi ụzọ TS relay site na header `X-Bifrost-Fallback`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_KEY`                        | _(adịghị atọ)_                                                              | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrost/route.ts`                  | Aha ọzọ maka `BIFROST_API_KEY` (nke ndị script na-agụ gburugburu (env) site na `OMNIROUTE_*` na-eji). `BIFROST_API_KEY` na-ewere ọkwa mbụ mgbe abụọ ahụ tọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND`                      | `ts` / `auto`                                                               | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Relay backend maka `/api/v1/relay/chat/completions`: `ts \| bifrost \| auto`. `ts` = TypeScript relay (ndabara mgbe ahazibeghị Bifrost); `auto` na-ahọrọ Bifrost mgbe atọrọ `BIFROST_BASE_URL` ma `BIFROST_ENABLED` ≠ `0`, nwekwara fallback TS akpaghị aka ma ọ bụrụ na a na-apụghị iru sidecar ahụ; `bifrost` na-amanye Bifrost (siri ike, enweghị fallback). Auth/rate-limit/injection-guard/allowlist na-agba ọsọ mbụ na ụzọ Next mgbe niile. Nzaghachi na-ebu `X-Routing-Backend` / `X-Routing-Fallback` / `X-Routing-Fallback-Reason`. |
| `RELAY_ROUTING_BACKEND`                        | _(adịghị atọ)_                                                              | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/routingBackend.ts`                 | Aha ọzọ a nabatara maka `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` (otu ụkpụrụ `ts \| bifrost \| auto` ahụ). `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND` na-ewere ọkwa mbụ mgbe abụọ ahụ tọrọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_BIFROST_FAILURE_COOLDOWN_MS`        | `5000`                                                                      | `src/app/api/v1/relay/chat/completions/bifrostCooldown.ts`                | Oge izu oyi (ms) mgbe hop nke Bifrost sidecar dara ada na ọnọdụ `auto` tupu relay anwaa sidecar ọzọ; ọ na-agafe kpọmkwem n'ụzọ TS ka oge izu oyi ahụ na-aga, mgbe ahụ ọ na-enyocha ọzọ. `0` na-agbanyụ ya. Ọ na-arụ ọrụ naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_RELAY_BACKEND=auto`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`                           | _(adịghị atọ)_                                                              | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ gaa asambodo PEM TLS iji jee ozi `omniroute serve` site na HTTPS (hà na `--tls-cert`). Ọ ga-abụ n'otu na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`; sava nke onwe ya ga-agwụcha TLS na otu listener ahụ (`wss://` na-arụ ọrụ n'agbanweghị). Adịghị atọ → HTTP dị larịị. Inye naanị otu n'ime cert/key, ma ọ bụ ụzọ a na-apụghị ịgụ, na-ede ịdọ aka ná ntị ma na-anọgide na HTTP.                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TLS_KEY`                            | _(adịghị atọ)_                                                              | `bin/cli/commands/serve.mjs`                                              | Ụzọ gaa igodo nzuzo PEM TLS maka HTTPS nke `omniroute serve` (hà na `--tls-key`). Ọ ga-abụ n'otu na `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`. Lee `OMNIROUTE_TLS_CERT`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED`            | `0`                                                                         | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Ọhịa isi maka ụzọ `/api/local/*`. Mgbe adịghị atọ ma ọ bụ `0`, ụzọ `/api/local/*` niile na-alọghachi 503 na production. Ọ ga-abụ `1` na nkwadebe na-abụghị loopback iji kwalite onye mmbido Redis na ndị mmalite ọrụ obodo 1-click ndị yiri ya. Nchekwa ugboro abụọ na nchịkọta nchebe ụzọ `isLocalOnlyPath()` (`LOCAL_ONLY_API_PREFIXES` na `src/server/authz/routeGuard.ts`).                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_TOKEN`              | _(adịghị atọ)_                                                              | `src/lib/security/localEndpoints.ts`                                      | Bearer token maka ndị na-akpọ `/api/local/*` na-abụghị nke nọ na loopback (dịka ngwa desktọpụ). Mgbe atọrọ, arịrịọ sitere na IP na-abụghị loopback ga-ebu `Authorization: Bearer <token>`. Achọrọ ya mgbe `OMNIROUTE_LOCAL_ENDPOINTS_ENABLED=1` na nkwadebe na-abụghị loopback.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `REDIS_PORT`                                   | `6379`                                                                      | `docker-compose.yml`                                                      | Ọnụ ụzọ nnabata maka sidecar Redis nke compose.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ọgụgụ njehie `502` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS` dị mkpa tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ. `1` (ndabara) na-atụgharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-aga n'ihu (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `502` wee ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_502_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400`                      | `false`                                                                     | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Nhọrọ n'aka (ekwuchịrị na ndabara): mgbe ọ bụ `true`, njehie `400` dị larịị (arịrị ọjọọ) na-akpalitekwa ntụgharị akaụntụ. Nke a na-agbakwụnye naanị — ọ naghị egbochi omume engine dị adị ebe `400` bu ederede rate-limit/quota ka na-atụgharị n'agbanyeghị flag a.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`               | `1`                                                                         | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Ọnụ ọgụgụ njehie `400` n'ime `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS` dị mkpa tupu a tụgharịa akaụntụ ahụ (a na-eletanụ ya naanị mgbe `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_ON_400=true`).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_WINDOW_SECONDS`          | `120`                                                                       | `open-sse/services/rotationConfig.ts`                                     | Windo na-aga n'ihu (sekọnd) nke a na-agụ njehie `400` wee ruo `OMNIROUTE_ROTATE_400_THRESHOLD`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |

### Onye Nhazi Warmup Claude

Warmup nke cron na-akwalite maka njikọ Anthropic OAuth ndị họọrọ n'onwe, ka windo rate-limit awa ise wee mepee site n'arịrị a haziri dị mfe kama site n'arịrị mbụ n'ezie (#8848). Ekwuchịrị onye nhazi ahụ ma e wezụga na `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED` bụ eziokwu **ma** e kaara njikọ ahụ aka na `settings.claudeWarmup.connections`; ndepụta njikọ efu pụtara na ọ dịghị ihe na-ekpo ọkụ ọbụlagodi na env var ahụ na-arụ ọrụ.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                     | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ------------------------------ | --------------------------- | ---------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_ENABLED`     | _(a tọghị → gbanyụrụ)_      | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ịgbazi isi maka nhazi oge warmup. Ọ na-anabata `1`/`true`/`yes`/`on` (anaghị ele anya n'etiti ukwu na obere edemede, a na-ewepụ oghere). Uru ọ bụla ọzọ, ma ọ bụ na a tọghị ya, na-ahapụ nhazi oge ahụ na ọgbụgba. |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CRON`        | `0 7 * * *`                 | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Nkwupụta cron nke mpaghara ise maka tick warmup, nke a na-eleba na `America/Los_Angeles` (mpaghara oge nrụọrụ weebụ Anthropic) n'agbanyeghị elekere nnọchi anya.                                                   |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_CONCURRENCY` | `3`                         | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ọtụtụ njikọ ka a na-akpọ n'otu oge n'otu tick. A na-akpachi ya n'etiti `1`-`10`; uru na-abụghị ọnụ ọgụgụ na-adaghachi na `3`.                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_WARMUP_MODEL`       | `claude-3-5-haiku-20241022` | `src/lib/warmupScheduler.ts` | Ụdị e ji arịrị ọ bụla warmup. Gbanwee ya naanị ma ọ bụrụ na ndabara adịghị n'atụmatụ gị; họrọ ụdị kacha dị ọnụ ala nke ka na-emeghe windo ahụ.                                                                     |

### Nzukọ VNC Nbanye Ihe Nchọgharị & Alias Data-Dir

Chromium+VNC e tinyere na container nke e ji achụpụta njirimara nbanye ihe nchọgharị na-arụrụta (`/api/vnc-session`), tinyere alias `DATA_DIR` nke ochie. Ha bụ nhọrọ niile — ndabara VNC na-ezube na oyiyi `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` nke e jikọtara ọnụ, ma a na-agbanwe ha naanị maka oyiyi container nke onwe, ọnụ ụzọ, ma ọ bụ nhazi ịgba ndụ ya.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                                                                  | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local`                                           | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Mkpado oyiyi Docker maka container nbanye Chromium+VNC. Wuo `docker/vnc-browser/chromium` ma ọ bụ dụzie nke a na oyiyi nke onwe.                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                                                                 | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Binary oge ọrụ container nke e ji amalite container VNC (dịka tọọ ya na `podman`).                                                                                                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụ ụzọ VNC/noVNC nke a kpughee n'ime container.                                                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                                                                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụ ụzọ Chrome DevTools Protocol n'ime container.                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ ndekọ profaịlụ Chromium n'ime container.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | `$HOME/.omniroute/browser-login-profiles`                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ nnọchi anya na-ejide profaịlụ nbanye ihe nchọgharị nke e chekwara.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000` (nkeji iri)                                                     | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ọdabara ịrụghị ọrụ (ms) tupu a chụpụ nzukọ VNC na-adịghị arụ ọrụ.                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000` (nkeji iri atọ)                                                | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oke siri ike (ms) na ndụ nke otu nzukọ VNC.                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                                                                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụ ọgụgụ kacha elu nke nzukọ VNC na-arụ ọrụ n'otu oge.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ọdabara (ms) ichere ka ihe nchọgharị container dị njikere maka CDP.                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                                                                  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge ọdabara (ms) maka ịchịkọta nzukọ/cookies nke a chụpụtara mgbe nbanye gasịrị.                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | `--remote-debugging-port=9222 --no-first-run --no-default-browser-check` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọzọ ihe ọnụ ọgụgụ nke a nyefere Chromium nke container.                                                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`                                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ntanetị Docker pụrụ iche nke container nbanye VNC na-esonye (#12571) kama ọlọ njikọ ndabara, ka ndị container nwannne ya agaghị eru ọdụ ụgbọ njikọ CDP ya.                                             |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(a tọghị)_                                                              | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | **Alias nke ochie** maka `DATA_DIR`, nke a na-eleba anya naanị mgbe ma `DATA_DIR` ma `OMNIROUTE_DATA_DIR` atọghị. Ọ na-achọta cache nzukọ web-thread Notion (`<dir>/notion-web-thread-sessions.json`). |

## 26. Ngwá Nnwale & E2E

Nke a bụ ihe `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`, `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs`,
`scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`, na `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs` na-eji. Hapụ ihe ọ bụla dị n'okpuru ka ọ bụrụ nke a na-ejibeghị na ndepụta mmepụta (production).

| Variable                               | Nke dabara adaba                    | Faịlụ isi                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| -------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_BOOTSTRAP_MODE`         | `auth`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Ọnọdụ bootstrap nke E2E (`auth`, `fresh`, `reuse`) maka onye na-agba ọsọ Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_E2E_PASSWORD`               | na-akwụghachi na `INITIAL_PASSWORD` | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Okwuntughe nchịkwa etinyere na gburugburu Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_LOCAL_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanye ihe nrụlele ahụike obodobere na-agba ọsọ n'oge Playwright na-agba ọsọ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_DISABLE_TOKEN_HEALTHCHECK`  | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Gbanye usoro ihe nrụlele ahụike token OAuth n'oge nnwale.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_HEALTHCHECK_SKIP_PROVIDERS` | _(a na-ejibeghị ya)_                | `src/lib/tokenHealthCheck.ts`             | Ndị na-enye ọrụ a kpọsara site na koma nke a na-ewepụ na nlele mmelite token a na-eme tupu ọ bụla (dịka `codex,openai`). Nhọrọ a họọrọ karia ịgbanye ihe nrụlele ahụike dum — ndị na-enye ọrụ nwere TTL dị mkpirikpi na-aga n'ihu na-emelite ọhụrụ ebe ndị na-enye ọrụ cascade ka na-anọ na-nhọrọ na-aza ọzụzụ naanị. |
| `OMNIROUTE_HIDE_HEALTHCHECK_LOGS`      | `true`                              | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Mechie mkpọtụ ihe nrụlele ahụike na stdout nke Playwright.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_PLAYWRIGHT_SKIP_BUILD`      | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/run-next-playwright.mjs`     | Wụlie mmepụta mmepụta Next.js tupu Playwright amalite (nkwalite CI).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_SKIP_UNINSTALL_HOOK`        | `0`                                 | `scripts/build/uninstall.mjs`             | Wụlie hook iwepụ nke OmniRoute (nke CI na-eji iji nwee `node_modules` ka ọ dị mma).                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ECOSYSTEM_SERVER_WAIT_MS`             | `180000`                            | `scripts/dev/run-ecosystem-tests.mjs`     | Oge echi (ms) maka sava ịdị ahụike tupu a malite ịgba nnwale usoro ihe / ụkpụrụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_URL`                   | `http://127.0.0.1:20128/login`      | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | URL nke ngwá smoke nke Electron na-atụ anya na ngwa a na-ebu na-enye.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_TIMEOUT_MS`            | `45000`                             | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Oge ime kaadị dum (ms) tupu ngwá smoke a kwụsị ịnwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_SETTLE_MS`             | `2000`                              | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ọzụzụ ime kaadị (ms) ka ibe a na-ebu gburugburu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_APP_EXECUTABLE`        | _(na-akpali onwe ya)_               | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Ụzọ doro anya gaa na executable nke Electron a na-ebu.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_DATA_DIR`              | _(tmpdir)_                          | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Nchịkọta data maka ọsọ smoke nke Electron.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_KEEP_DATA`             | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Debe `1` iji debe ntuziaka data smoke ahụ mgbe ọgba ahụ gasịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_STREAM_LOGS`           | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | Debe `1` iji bufee ndekọ Electron na stdout n'oge ọgba ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ELECTRON_SMOKE_COLD_RESTART`          | `0`                                 | `scripts/dev/smoke-electron-packaged.mjs` | #7592: malitegharịa megide otu dir data ahụ wee kwenye na mwepụta nke abụọ họrọrọ onye ọkwọ SQLite obodo.                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `CLI_DEVIN_BIN`                        | _(nchọpụta PATH)_                   | `open-sse/executors/devin-cli.ts`         | Gafere ụzọ binary Devin CLI ahụ.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |

### Pipeline ntụgharị asụsụ Docs

`scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs` na-eji ya (iwu `npm run i18n:run` ahụ).
A naghị edobe variables isii ahụ niile n'ụzọ izizi — debe ha na `.env` naanị na ngwá
ndị kwesịrị ịgba onye ntụgharị docs ahụ.

| Variable                                 | Izizi             | Faịlụ isi mmalite                        | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------------- | ----------------- | ---------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_URL`          | _(a naghị edobe)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | URL isi kwadoro OpenAI maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ ahụ.                                                                                                                               |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_API_KEY`          | _(a naghị edobe)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Token Bearer maka backend ntụgharị asụsụ ahụ (anaghị edekọ ya).                                                                                                                       |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_MODEL`            | _(a naghị edobe)_ | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | ID ụdị, dịka `gpt-4o-mini` ma ọ bụ `cx/gpt-5.4-mini`.                                                                                                                                 |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_FALLBACK_MODELS`  | _(a naghị edobe)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Usoro ụdị nhọrọ kewapụrụ site na comma nke a na-anwale n'usoro mgbe ụdị bụ isi dara oda (429 quota, 401/402, 5xx, oge agwụla, netwọk); anaghị anwale arịrị aghụghọ (400/413/422) ọzọ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_TIMEOUT_MS`       | `60000`           | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Oge agwụla arịrị ọ bụla na milliseconds.                                                                                                                                              |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_REASONING_EFFORT` | _(a naghị edobe)_ | `scripts/i18n/lib/translate-backend.mjs` | Njikwa reasoning nhọrọ, nke a na-eziga azụ dị ka ọ dị mgbe edobere ya.                                                                                                                |
| `OMNIROUTE_TRANSLATION_CONCURRENCY`      | `4`               | `scripts/i18n/run-translation.mjs`       | Arịrị ntụgharị asụsụ nhazi mgbe a na-agba ọtụtụ faịlụ / locales.                                                                                                                      |

## 27. Nri Radar (Nnabata n'Onwe Gị)

Mgbakwunye nhọrọ nke ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED na-achịkwa (agbanyụrụ na ndabara — ọkọlọtọ njirimara
a na-agbanye ma ọ bụ gbanyụọ site na Settings/DB, ọ bụghị env var; lee
[docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md#flag-radar_enabled-default-off)).
Ngbanwe anọ mbụ dị n'okpuru bụ nhọrọ iji dochie ntọala maka nri a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara,
yana usoro igodo ndị nkwado. Nke ise, `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`, bụ njikọ dị iche na-enweghị uru ndabara nke na-eduga na
ogwe ọrụ nzuzo nke onye nwe ya. Lee [docs/frameworks/RADAR.md](../frameworks/RADAR.md) maka akwụkwọ zuru ezu
banyere modul ahụ na
[usoro ịgbalite ya site na mmalite ruo na njedebe na nhazi a na-eduzi](../frameworks/RADAR.md#end-to-end-activation-and-guided-setup).

A naghị eji environment variable ahazi ngwa ọgụgụ ọkwa Home/Changelog izugbe,
ọ dịghịkwa adabere na ọkọlọtọ njirimara RADAR_ENABLED. Ọ na-eji naanị GET agụ URL `news.json`
nke repository ọha, nke akọwapụtara na `src/shared/utils/releaseNotes.ts`;
ID nke ihe a jụrụ ka dị na nchekwa mpaghara nke ihe nchọgharị.

| Variable                      | Ndabara                                      | Faịlụ Isi Mmalite                                            | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `RADAR_FEED_URL`              | `https://radar.omniroute.online`             | `src/lib/radar/{sync,referralsSync,offersSync,intelSync}.ts` | URL ntọala nke katalọgụ, ntụnye, onyinye ndị nkwado, na nri Intel ndị a bịanyere aka na ha iche iche na-ekekọrịta. Dochie ya ka ọ na-atụ aka na ọrụ a nabatara n'onwe gị ma ọ bụ nke e kewapụtara. |
| `RADAR_FEED_PUBKEY`           | _(igodo ndabara e debere kpọmkwem)_          | `src/lib/radar/pinnedKeys.ts`                                | Igodo ọha Ed25519 (base64-DER SPKI ma ọ bụ PEM) e ji enyocha mbinye aka nri sitere na nri ahaziri iche.                                                                                            |
| `RADAR_CONTRIBUTOR_CLAIM_URL` | `https://radar.omniroute.online/auth/github` | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Abụ m onye nyere aka" na-emepe (usoro ịnata igodo onye nkwado site na GitHub OAuth).                                                                                     |
| `RADAR_SUPPORTER_PLANS_URL`   | `https://radar.omniroute.online/planos`      | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | URL nke bọtịnụ dashboard "Kwado ọrụ ahụ" na-emepe (ibe ịkwụ ụgwọ/atụmatụ).                                                                                                                         |
| `RADAR_ADMIN_URL`             | _(edoghị ya)_                                | `src/lib/radar/links.ts`                                     | Njikọ nzuzo nke ogwe ọrụ maka naanị onye nwe ya. A chọrọ HTTPS, ma e wezụga maka nnyefe SSH loopback nke HTTP; uru edoghị ma ọ bụ nke na-ezighi ezi anaghị emepụta ihe nchọgharị ọ bụla.           |

---

## Nnyocha: Variable E Wepụrụ / Ndị Na-adịghịzi Arụ Ọrụ

Variable ndị a pụtara na ụdị `.env.example` ndị gara aga, mana ha **enweghị ntụaka runtime ọ bụla** na codebase dị ugbu a. E wepụla ha:

| Mgbanwe                                                                                                                                                                         | Ihe kpatara ya                                                                                                                                 |
| ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `STORAGE_DRIVER=sqlite`                                                                                                                                                         | Faịlụ isi mmalite ọ bụla anaghị agụ ya. SQLite bụ naanị draịva a na-akwado — ọ dịghị mkpa ịhọrọ nke ọ bụla.                                    |
| `INSTANCE_NAME=omniroute`                                                                                                                                                       | Ọ dị na dọkụmentị ochie/ụkpụrụ env mana anaghị eji ya n'oge arụmọrụ. Ọ nwere ike ịlaghachi na njirimara ọtụtụ-instance n'ọdịnihu.              |
| `SQLITE_MAX_SIZE_MB=2048`                                                                                                                                                       | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. E nweghị mmachi e ji aka tinye n'ogo nchekwa data.                                                  |
| `SQLITE_CLEAN_LEGACY_FILES=true`                                                                                                                                                | E zoghị aka na ya n'ime koodu isi mmalite. O yikarịrị ka ewepụla nhicha faịlụ ochie.                                                           |
| `CLI_ROO_BIN`                                                                                                                                                                   | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts`.                                                                                        |
| `CLI_KIMI_CODING_BIN`                                                                                                                                                           | E debanyeghị ya na `src/shared/services/cliRuntime.ts` (Kimi Coding na-eji OAuth, ọ bụghị faịlụ CLI binary).                                   |
| `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_ID` / `IFLOW_OAUTH_CLIENT_SECRET`                                                                                                                           | E zoghị aka na ha ebe ọ bụla n'ime koodu isi mmalite.                                                                                          |
| `CEREBRAS_API_KEY` / `COHERE_API_KEY` / `FIREWORKS_API_KEY` / `GROQ_API_KEY` / `MISTRAL_API_KEY` / `NEBIUS_API_KEY` / `PERPLEXITY_API_KEY` / `TOGETHER_API_KEY` / `XAI_API_KEY` | Ewepụrụ ha na v3.8.0. Runtime anaghịzi agụ env vars ndị a — nzere na-abịa site na Dashboard / `data/provider-credentials.json` / DB ezoro ezo. |
| `CURSOR_PROTOBUF_DEBUG`                                                                                                                                                         | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Cursor executor na-eji `CURSOR_DEBUG` / `CURSOR_STREAM_DEBUG` (lee §22).                                                 |
| `CLI_COMPAT_KIRO`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na v3.8.0. Kiro dị na `CLI_COMPAT_OMITTED_PROVIDER_IDS` — toggle ya enweghị mmetụta ọ bụla.                                         |
| `QIANFAN_API_KEY`                                                                                                                                                               | Ewepụrụ ya na stubs API key ndị ọzọ anaghị eji eme ihe na v3.8.0.                                                                              |

### Ndozi Uru Ndabara

| Mgbanwe                   | Uru `.env.example` Ochie | Ndabara Koodu N'ezie | Ihe Edoziri                                                   |
| ------------------------- | ------------------------ | -------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `APP_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS`  | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |
| `CALL_LOG_RETENTION_DAYS` | `90`                     | `7`                  | ✅ Ewepụrụ uru na-eduhie eduhie; e depụtara `7` dị ka ndabara |

### Mmegharị config OpenCode (ngwaọrụ ad-hoc)

`scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` na-eji ya emegharị `opencode.json`
nwere uru `limit.context` na `limit.output` ziri ezi, nke e wetara site na instance
OmniRoute na-arụ ọrụ. Ọ dịghị nke ọ bụla n'ime ndị a achọrọ maka arụmọrụ nkịtị — script a
bụ naanị ngwaọrụ ndị mmepe.

| Mgbanwe            | Ndabara                  | Faịlụ Isi                                 | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                   |
| ------------------ | ------------------------ | ----------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_URL`    | `http://localhost:20128` | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | URL ntọala nke instance OmniRoute a ga-ajụ maka `/v1/models`.                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_KEY`    | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API maka nkwenye njirimara megide endpoint OmniRoute `/v1/models`. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OPENCODE_API_KEY`.            |
| `OPENCODE_API_KEY` | _(edobeghị)_             | `scripts/ad-hoc/regen-opencode-config.ts` | Igodo API ụdị OpenCode (`sk-...`) nke a na-ede n'ime `opencode.json` e mepụtaghachiri. Ọ bụrụ na edobeghị ya, ọ ga-eji `OMNIROUTE_KEY`. |

### Ngwa nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị (ngwa ad-hoc)

`scripts/compression-eval/index.ts`, CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị, na-eji ya.
Achọghị ya maka ọrụ nkịtị — ọ bụ naanị ngwa ndị nrụpụta.

| Mgbanwe                      | Ndabara    | Faịlụ Isi                           | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| ---------------------------- | ---------- | ----------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_EVAL_CREDENTIALS` | `{}` (efu) | `scripts/compression-eval/index.ts` | Ozi nzere JSON nke onye njikwa nyere maka provider nke CLI nnwale mkpakọ na-anọghị n'ịntanetị na-anwale (a na-eji `JSON.parse` atụgharị ya). Hapụ ya n'edoghị maka nnwale na-enweghị mmejuputa. |

### Oge Nchọgharị VNC

`src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts` na-eji ha ahazi oge Chromium na-enweghị interface eserese nke dabere na Docker maka ndị provider akpaaka nchọgharị. Ha niile bụ nhọrọ — e gosiri ndabara n'okpuru.

| Mgbanwe                               | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ Isi                                   | Nkọwa                                                                         |
| ------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_DOCKER_BIN`                | `docker`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ụzọ gaa na binary Docker e ji emepụta container VNC.                          |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IMAGE`                 | `omniroute-vnc-chromium:local` | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Image Docker maka container VNC Chromium.                                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CHROMIUM_ARGS`         | _(flags arụnyere n'ime)_       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Arụmụka CLI Chromium ndị ọzọ a na-ebufe na nchọgharị dị n'ime container.      |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_VNC_PORT`    | `3000`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port VNC dị n'ime container.                                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_CDP_PORT`    | `9223`                         | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Port Chrome DevTools Protocol dị n'ime container.                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_CONTAINER_PROFILE_DIR` | `/config`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ profaịlụ dị n'ime container.                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_PROFILE_DIR`           | _(edobeghị)_                   | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ndekọ dị n'akụkụ host maka profaịlụ nchọgharị na-adịgide adịgide.             |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_IDLE_MS`               | `600000`                       | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe ịnọ nkịtị (ms) tupu a chịkọta oge VNC.                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_MS`                | `1800000`                      | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ogologo oge kachasị nke session (ms).                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_MAX_SESSIONS`          | `4`                            | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Ọnụọgụ session VNC kachasị nwere ike ịrụkọ ọrụ n'otu oge.                     |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_READY_MS`              | `45000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchọgharị ịdị njikere (ms).                                  |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_HARVEST_MS`            | `20000`                        | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Oge njedebe maka nchịkọta/nhicha (ms).                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_VNC_NETWORK`               | `omniroute-vnc-browser-login`  | `src/lib/vncSession/manifest.ts`            | Netwọkụ Docker pụrụ iche container na-esonyere (#12571), kama bridge ndabara. |
| `VIBEPROXY_DATA_DIR`                  | _(edobeghị)_                   | `open-sse/services/notionThreadSessions.ts` | Ndekọ maka nchekwa na-adịgide adịgide nke session eriri Notion.               |

### Nkwenye njirimara ọrụ ime

| Mgbanwe                                 | Ndabara | Nkọwa                                                                                        |
| --------------------------------------- | ------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN`      | –       | Token inline maka nkwenye njirimara site n'otu ọrụ gaa n'ọzọ na management plane.            |
| `OMNIROUTE_INTERNAL_SERVICE_TOKEN_FILE` | –       | Ụzọ gaa na faịlụ nwere token ọrụ ime (ọ ka mma n'ime container; ọ na-akagbu mgbanwe inline). |

### Ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter

| Mgbanwe                             | Ndabara   | Nkọwa                                                                                                              |
| ----------------------------------- | --------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_ENABLED` | `true`    | Tọọ ya ka ọ bụrụ `false` iji mafere iweta ọnụọgụ OpenRouter nke provider ọ bụla maka ime ka katalọgụ baa ọgaranya. |
| `OPENROUTER_PROVIDER_STATS_TTL_MS`  | `3600000` | TTL cache (ms) maka ọnụọgụ provider OpenRouter e wetara.                                                           |

### Njikọ Redis agbakwunyere n'ime

| Mgbanwe                     | Ndabara     | Nkọwa                                                               |
| --------------------------- | ----------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `REDIS_BIND_HOST`           | `127.0.0.1` | Adreesị njikọ maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                          |
| `REDIS_PORT`                | `6379`      | Ọdụ ụgbọ mmiri maka ọrụ Redis agbakwunyere.                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_REDIS_BIND_HOST` | –           | Ntọgharị nwere oke OmniRoute maka adreesị njikọ Redis agbakwunyere. |
