## 24. Mgbakwunye na mwepụta v3.8.50

E webatara ntọala ndị a mgbe oyiyi nkwụsịtụ environment-contract gara aga.

| Mgbanwe                                     | Ọdịnala                    | Faịlụ isi                                                                                | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     |
| ------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_QUEUE_MS`         | `2000`                     | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Oge kacha ogologo e ga-eche ohere nabata chat dị arọ tupu `503` nke enwere ike ịnwagharị; obere oge eche nke ekenyere ókè na-edobe ukwuu ọrụ agent n'usoro kama ịbụ `503` ozugbo. `0` naeweghachite njụ ozugbo.                                                                                                                                                           |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_ADMISSION_MAX_QUEUED_BYTES` | `4194304` (4 MB)           | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ego bytes nke e debere n'ahịrị maka oge eche nabata: na-ekenye ókè na ngụkọta bytes nke ahịrị e kwụsịtụrụ n'usoro dum ka oge eche ghara ịkwalite heap (#4380). Ndị eche gafere ego na-anata `503` nke enwere ike ịnwagharị ozugbo.                                                                                                                                        |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_TTL_MS`             | `60000` (60 s)             | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ihe emegbuola nke na-adịghị arụ ọrụ kemgbe #10110: ewepụrụ ụzọ nabata nke session ọ bụla ka e jiri otu ego nke usoro dum. A nabatara ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_MAX_SESSIONS`       | `64`                       | `src/shared/middleware/chatBodyAdmission.ts`                                             | Ihe emegbuola nke na-adịghị arụ ọrụ kemgbe #10110: ewepụrụ ụzọ nabata nke session ọ bụla ka e jiri otu ego nke usoro dum. A nabatara ya maka ndakọrịta nhazi; a na-eleghara ya anya.                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `OMNIROUTE_CHAT_VIRTUAL_LANES`              | `0` (gbanyụrụ)             | `open-sse/services/admission/runtime.ts`                                                 | Ụzọ nabata nke oge ọrụ na-agbanwe agbanwe (#9654): mgbaji isi maka ọnụ ụzọ na-agbanwe agbanwe nke tenant ọ bụla (system 2). Ọ dị iche na mgbanwe ụzọ nke njikọ ọ bụla emegbuola n'elu (TTL_MS / MAX_SESSIONS, ndị na-adịghị arụ ọrụ kemgbe #10110). Ọ bụ ihe ịrịba ama atụmatụ Dashboard nke aha otu aha; mgbanwe env na-eri nke dashboard override; ọ chọrọ mmalite ọzọ. |
| `OMNIROUTE_RUNNOW_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `30000`                    | `src/app/api/jobs/[id]/run-now/route.ts`                                                 | Na-ekenye ókè oge oku run-now na-eche ọrụ nọ na-arụ tupu ọ malite ọrụ e debere n'ahịrị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BROWSER_REFRESH`             | gbadoro                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-edobe ọnọdụ IMS na ihe ize ndụ ihe nchọgharị ka ọ dị ọhụrụ site na oge Chrome CDP ekenyere akaụntụ; debe `0` iji gbanyụọ ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_SESSION_DISK`                | gbadoro                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Na-echekwa oge Adobe e meziri n'okpuru `DATA_DIR`; debe `0` maka ọnọdụ dị na ebe nchekwa naanị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_MIN_SUBMIT_GAP_MS`           | `12000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oghere kacha nta n'etiti iziga ọrụ generate nke Adobe Firefly.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_BATCH_EXTRA_GAP_MS`          | `15000`                    | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflySession.ts`                                               | Oge izu ike ọzọ mgbe iziga Adobe nke atọ gara nke ọma ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `ADOBE_FIREFLY_CHROME_HEADLESS`             | `0`                        | `open-sse/services/adobeFireflyBrowserLogin.ts`                                          | Ọnọdụ true-headless maka naanị nyocha; Adobe colligo na-ajụkarị oge ihe ize ndụ nke ọ na-emepụta.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHROME_PATH`                               | nchọpụta onwe              | `open-sse/executors/cloudflare-playground.ts`, `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts` | Ụzọ faịlụ Chrome zuru oke nke bụ nhọrọ nke ndị na-agba ọrụ site n'ihe nchọgharị ji mgbe nchọpụta onwe nke ikpo okwu zughị oke.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN`                        | _(edeberebeghị)_           | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Token BotFather nke na-agbado webhook na-abata ma bụrụ aka na `initData` nke Mini App.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_SECRET`                   | _(edeberebeghị)_           | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Ihe nzuzo ekekọrịtara edebanyere site na `setWebhook` nke a na-enyocha tụnyere isiokwu `X-Telegram-Bot-Api-Secret-Token` na nnyefe webhook ọ bụla. Achọrọ ya maka ụzọ webhook; ịdabeghị ya pụtara na a ga-ajụ nnyefe webhook site na 503.                                                                                                                                 |
| `TELEGRAM_DEFAULT_MODEL`                    | `auto/chat`                | `src/lib/telegram/chatProxy.ts`                                                          | Ihe nlereanya e ji azịza chat Telegram.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `TELEGRAM_BOT_API_BASE`                     | `https://api.telegram.org` | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | URL ntọala Bot API maka proxies ma ọ bụ sava Bot API nke onwe na-akwado.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  |
| `TELEGRAM_WEBHOOK_TIMEOUT_MS`               | `60000`                    | `src/lib/telegram/config.ts`                                                             | Oge njedebe na milliseconds maka oku Bot API na-aga na mpụga.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             |
| `OMNIROUTE_OPTIONAL_PACK_TAR`               | `1` (gbadoro)              | `scripts/build/optionalPackStaging.mjs`                                                  | Debe `0` iji gbafere imipụta `.tar.gz` tarballs mgbe a na-edobe ML/browser packs nhọrọ maka osisi Electron standalone (a ka na-emepụta ndekọ pack na `optional-packs.index.json`). Usoro ọrụ mwepụta desktọpụ ji ya belata nha bulite artifact.                                                                                                                           |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED`          | `false`                    | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`                                                       | Na-agbado ndebanye aha ụzọ ihe oyiyi nke xAI subscription; e gbanyụrụ ya n'ụzọ ndabere ka ụzọ ihe oyiyi API-key ghara ịgbanwe.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `OMNIROUTE_API_KEY_COOLDOWN_MS`             | `60000`                    | `open-sse/services/apiKeyRotator.ts`                                                     | Oge jụrụ oyi nke igodo ọ bụla na milliseconds mgbe nzaghachi `429` enweghị ntụziaka `Retry-After` enwere ike iji.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`           | gbadoro                    | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts`                                              | Debe ya na `off`, `0`, ma ọ bụ `false` iji gbanyụọ ndekọ na itinye nchịkwa estimator nke dị na ebe nchekwa.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `NEXT_MANUAL_SIG_HANDLE`                    | _(ime ụlọ)_                | `scripts/dev/standalone-server-ws.mjs`                                                   | Nchịkwa signal-handler nke sava standalone ime ụlọ; launcher na-edebekarị ma hichaa ya kama ka ndị ọrụ hazie ya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |

### ChatGPT Web (Codex)

Ọdịnala zuru ụwa ọnụ maka ihe nchọgharị headless na ọwa tunnel ngwá ọrụ na-aga na mpụga. Ọnụ ọgụgụ njikọ e setịpụrụ na Dashboard ka nwere ọkwa mbụ.

| Mgbanwe                                 | Ndabara                        | Faịlụ isi                                                                  | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              |
| --------------------------------------- | ------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------- | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CHROME_PATH`         | _(chọpụta onwe ya)_            | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ụzọ Chrome/Chromium doro anya maka ọrụ npm, systemd na PM2.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CHROME_PATH`                           | _(chọpụta onwe ya)_            | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Fallback nkete maka ụzọ Chrome/Chromium doro anya.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CDP_URL`             | _(a na-etobeghị)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Ebe njedebe CDP dị n'ime; Docker na-eji sidecar na ọdụ ụgbọ `9223`.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_USE_CDP_PROFILE`     | _(a na-etobeghị — gbanyụrụ)_   | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/persistent-cdp-context.ts`              | Tọwanye na `1` (mgbe a haziri ebe njedebe CDP) iji jiri peeji ChatGPT dị adị nke profaịlụ ihe nchọgharị e jikwanyere banyere nke a na-elekwa site n'èzí, kama oke dị iche maka ọ bụ ọkwa; ọ na-agbakwa ọnụ ọgụgụ tabs ChatGPT na-arụkọ ọrụ na 1. A gbanyụrụ ya na ndabara. Lee [profaịlụ CDP na-adịgide](../guides/CHATGPT-WEB-PERSISTENT-CDP.md). |
| `CDP_PROXY_TOKEN`                       | _(a na-etobeghị)_              | `docker/chatgpt-web-codex-browser/cdp-proxy.mjs`                           | Ọ bụrụ na a tọwanye ya, arịrị ọ bụla zigaara CDP proxy sidecar ga-ebu uru a n'isiokwu `X-Omni-Cdp-Token` (#13679). Ọ bụrụ na enweghị uru, proxy na-agafegharị n'ihu na-enweghị nyocha — mgbe ahụ, naanị ịkewapụ netwọk nke Compose net `chatgpt-web-codex-net` na-echebe. Mepụta ya site na `openssl rand -hex 32`.                                |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_TUNNEL_ID`           | _(a na-etobeghị)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | OpenAI tunnel ID zuru ụwa ọnụ maka gburugburu ngwá ọrụ Codex nke mpaghara.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_RUNTIME_KEY`         | _(a na-etobeghị)_              | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Igodo runtime tunnel zuru ụwa ọnụ; ekwesịghị ibipụta ya na logs ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            |
| `CHATGPT_WEB_CODEX_CONNECTOR_NAME`      | `OmniRoute Codex v2`           | `open-sse/executors/chatgpt-web-codex.ts`                                  | Aha kpọmkwem nke ChatGPT custom connector kacha ọhụrụ maka akwa MCP.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_HOME`                | `<DATA_DIR>/chatgpt-web-codex` | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ndekọ pụrụ iche maka ọnọdụ ihe nchọgharị, broker na tunnel.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BROWSER_DIAGNOSTICS` | `0`                            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/adapters/chatgpt-web/browser-worker.ts` | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, a na-ewere foto nyocha ihe nchọgharị na checkpoint ọ bụla.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_LAUNCHER`            | _(a na-etobeghị)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na binary launcher na-adịgide adịgide.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`                 | _(chọpụta onwe ya)_            | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Ụzọ zuru oke nhọrọ gaa na binary Bun runtime.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      |
| `CODEX_WEB_GPT_BUN`                     | _(a na-etobeghị)_              | `open-sse/vendor/codex-chatgpt-web/config.ts`                              | Fallback legacy maka `CODEX_CHATGPT_WEB_BUN`; ntọpụ ọhụrụ na-eji aha nkịtị.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        |

---

## OmniConductor Bridge

Onye na-anabata SSE na-adịgide ogologo oge nke na-emegharị oyiri ọrụ hub OmniConductor n'ime A2A TaskManager mpaghara (`src/lib/conductor/`). Nhọrọ n'onwe — bridge na-amalite naanị mgbe a tọrọ `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`. Naanị n'akụkụ sava: a gaghị ekwe ka token hub ruo na ihe nchọgharị mgbe ọ bụla.

| Mgbanwe                        | Ndabara                 | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                    | Nkọwa                                                                                                                           |
| ------------------------------ | ----------------------- | -------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_URL`            | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | URL isi nke hub OmniConductor (dịka `http://127.0.0.1:7910`). A na-atọghị ya = bridge gbanyụrụ.                                 |
| `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN`          | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/boot.ts`      | Ihe njirimara hub maka nri SSE — mepụta peer ụdị `spokesperson` na hub ahụ (`POST /v1/peers`, admin).                           |
| `CONDUCTOR_ORCHESTRATOR_TOKEN` | _(efu)_                 | `src/lib/conductor/hubProxy.ts`  | Ihe njirimara maka ịnyefe ọrụ A2A→hub na-abata (`POST /v1/tasks`); ọ na-adachigha na `CONDUCTOR_HUB_TOKEN` mgbe a na-atọghị ya. |
| `CONDUCTOR_SPOKESPERSON_URL`   | `http://127.0.0.1:7920` | `src/lib/conductor/faroProxy.ts` | URL isi nke ọrụ spokesperson (Faro) dị n'azụ proxy nkata dashboard (`/api/conductor/ask`).                                      |

### Nhazi nke amatara quota

`open-sse/services/combo.ts` na `src/lib/quota/quotaScheduler.ts` na-eji ya maka nyocha mmefu ego token tupu arịrị ọnụ. Nhọrọ n'onwe — omume nhazi ụzọ ndabara anaghị agbanwe mgbe a na-atọghị ya.

| Mgbanwe                            | Ndabara | Faịlụ Isi Iyi                               | Nkọwa                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| ---------------------------------- | ------- | ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `OMNIROUTE_QUOTA_AWARE_ROUTING`    | `0`     | `open-sse/services/combo.ts`                | Mgbe ọ bụ `1`, gafee njikọ ndị mmefu ego token ha kwa-oge (`rateLimitOverrides.tpm`, tebụl `provider_quota_state`) na-enweghị ike ịkwụ ụgwọ arịrị ọnụ atụrụ anya tupu izipu. Na-emeghe ka ọ dị (fail-open) mgbe ọ dịghị mmefu ego a haziri.           |
| `OMNIROUTE_ESTIMATOR_CALIBRATION`  | _(ọrụ)_ | `open-sse/services/estimatorCalibration.ts` | Nhazi nzaghachi nke onye na-atụle token nke nche ọnọdụ (#14931): kwa (provider, model, ngwá ọrụ dị) EMA nke `usage.prompt_tokens` / atụmatụ, a kpọchiri na 0.15–2.0, na ncheta naanị. `off`, `0` ma ọ bụ `false` na-agbanyụ ndekọ na itinye ya n'ọrụ. |
| `GROK_SUBSCRIPTION_IMAGES_ENABLED` | `false` | `open-sse/config/imageRegistry.ts`          | Na-edebanye ụzọ oyiyi `xai-oauth` (`xao`) na `grok-cli`. Flag atụmatụ dashboard nke otu aha; ndochi DB na-emeri uru a.                                                                                                                                |
